escalated: null
escalation_class: null
questions_for_human: []
proposed_invariants:
- An icon placed beside a visible word is aria-hidden, and adding it leaves the control's
  accessible name unchanged.
- An icon-only control always has an accessible name and a 44px target.
- NombreIcono is defined once, and every name in it has a drawn path and at least
  one screen that uses it.
- 'More info''s open state is local component state: it is never written to browser
  storage or history and never marks a form as changed.'
- No emoji is used as an interface icon.
- The string a copy registry lists is the same exported constant the screen renders,
  never a second copy of the text.
- After piece 1 no subtask diff changes Icono.tsx or componentes.ts, and every subtask
  leaves tsc and oxlint green on its own branch.
- A control with visible text and no icon is allowed only as a link, a Deshacer, an
  option control, or a named text-only kind (submit or answer), and the type of Boton
  makes any other combination a compile error.
- 'Close is one form on every form and sheet: the cerrar icon in a 44px target with
  the accessible name "Cerrar", never the word beside it on one screen and the icon
  alone on another.'
- The text-only kinds of Boton are exactly submit, link, undo and back, plus the internal
  dialog answer, and no screen defines a text-only action another way.
- Every TRAZOS entry inherits stroke, size and fill from Icono's svg and sets none
  of its own.
- A length limit is measured on fixed copy with SAMPLES, never on fixture or API data.
discard_reason: null

subtasks:
  icon-names:
    id: icon-names
    title: '1a: one list of icon names pinned to the design, no per-call size or stroke,
      no emoji'
    brief: 'Make the drawn icon set and the design contract one checked list, before
      any screen changes.


      - `web/src/components/Icono.tsx`: export `ICON_NAMES = [...] as const` and `type
      NombreIcono = (typeof ICON_NAMES)[number]`; `TRAZOS: Record<NombreIcono, ReactNode>`
      stays. Today''s 17 names stay (the 13 design names plus `agregar`, `stock`,
      `whatsapp`, `telegram`).

      - `docs/product/design-system/componentes.ts`: its `NombreIcono` union gains
      `agregar`, `stock`, `whatsapp`, `telegram`, so both lists are equal; `IconoProps`
      there loses `size` and `strokeWidth`.

      - Remove `size` and `strokeWidth` from `Icono`''s props: every icon is 22 px,
      stroke 1.6, `stroke="currentColor"`, `fill="none"`, set once on `Icono`''s `<svg>`.
      Delete the 11 override call sites in 8 files in this same diff (Shell.tsx ×2,
      Gastos.tsx ×3, Mas.tsx, settings/Settings.tsx, settings/AccountGroup.tsx, ventas/Ventas.tsx,
      ventas/NuevaVenta.tsx, ventas/NuevaVentaCliente.tsx). These edits are deletions
      only; CSS that compensated for the old sizes is adjusted only if the screen''s
      comparison shows a difference.

      - New `tests/test_design_icons.py` (pytest, like `test_design_tokens.py`): parses
      the union in `componentes.ts` and `ICON_NAMES` in `Icono.tsx` and fails on any
      name in one and not the other; checks `Icono`''s svg states 22 and 1.6; fails
      if any `TRAZOS` entry carries its own `stroke-width`, a `fill` other than `none`,
      a `transform` or a nested `<svg>`.

      - New real-tree vitest beside `forbiddenApis.real.test.ts`: no Extended_Pictographic
      code point in non-test source under `web/src`.

      - `docs/product/design-system/README.md`: an icon section stating the same rule
      as `docs/product/icon-and-text-rule.md` (the two closed lists, the length limits,
      what may go behind More info, one family at 1.6/22 px, no emoji, every icon
      named by its `NombreIcono`), linking `tests/test_design_icons.py`.


      Acceptance: `tsc`, `oxlint`, `mypy`, `ruff`, pytest and vitest green; adding
      a name to one of the two files alone fails pytest; comparisons of the touched
      screens attached.'
    seams:
    - tests/test_design_icons.py over componentes.ts and Icono.tsx
    - Icono component (Testing Library)
    - real-tree emoji guard (vitest)
    depends_on: []
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-97.icon-names

    reason: 'The brief''s acceptance asks for comparisons of the touched screens,
      and they are missing. The Developer also claims no pixel change, but the diff
      moves icons from 15–20 px and stroke 1.8–2 to 22 px at 1.6. Everything else
      is verified: name parity, the fixed 22/1.6 svg, the 11 deletions and green seam
      tests.'
    return_category: spec-scope
  icon-glyphs:

    title: '1b: draw the new icons the design names, in both files at once'
    brief: 'Add the new names the GH-97 design uses: `lapiz`, `cerrar`, `info`, `compartir`,
      `candado`, `salirSesion`, `archivar`, `restaurar`, `probar`, `salidaStock`,
      `atencion`, and the Más and Configuración entry icons the design names (Integraciones''

      meaning.


      - Paths come only from the GH-97 design (`docs/product/design-system/pantallas/Componentes.dc.html`
      and the screens that draw them). A Developer never invents a glyph: if a name
      has no path in the design, stop and report it back to UI design; no placeholder

      - Each name goes into `ICON_NAMES`, `TRAZOS` and `componentes.ts`''s union in
      this diff; `tests/test_design_icons.py` stays green, family check included.

      - A name is added only if a screen of the design uses it.

      - Componentes comparison (`compare.sh`), both themes, attached; each glyph judged
      by eye.'

  control-seam:
    id: control-seam
    title: '2a: Boton and BotonLink carry the icon and text rule in their types; the
      shared test helper; ADR'
    brief: "Make the closed lists of the rule (docs/product/icon-and-text-rule.md)\
      \ a type.\n\n- `web/src/components/Boton.tsx` props become a discriminated union:\n\
      \  - `variant: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'destructive'` with either `icon: NombreIcono`\
      \ (icon + text) or `textOnly: 'submit'` together with `type: 'submit'`. No bare\
      \ `null`.\n  - `variant: 'icon'` with `action: IconOnlyAction` (`'delete' |\
      \ 'search' | 'close' | 'edit' | 'share'`) and a required `ariaLabel: string`;\
      \ the icon comes from a closed `Record<IconOnlyAction, NombreIcono>` (delete→`tacho`,\
      \ search→`buscar`, close→`cerrar`, edit→`lapiz`, share→`compartir`); `.boton-icono`\
      \ gives a 44 px target.\n  - `variant: 'link'` (enlace), `'undo'` (Deshacer)\
      \ and `'back'` (\"‹ <parent>\") are text only and accept no icon. `row` stays\
      \ for tappable list rows.\n  - Any icon rendered next to visible text is `aria-hidden`;\
      \ the accessible name is the word.\n- New `web/src/components/BotonLink.tsx`:\
      \ an in-app link (`InAppLink` underneath) with the same union minus `submit`,\
      \ for actions that navigate (\"Mandar catálogo\", \"+ Entrada de stock\", \"\
      Sacar del stock\" …).\n- `ConfirmDialog` and `LeaveConfirm` draw their answer\
      \ pairs through an internal, unexported `AnswerButton`; their words do not change.\n\
      - Update the 6 current `Boton` callers to the new props without changing what\
      \ they render, except as the union requires.\n- New `web/src/test/iconAndTextRule.ts`:\
      \ `expectIconAndTextRule(container, samples?)` asserting: every button and link\
      \ has an accessible name; every svg inside a control with visible text is `aria-hidden`;\
      \ every control with no visible text is `.boton-icono`; a control with visible\
      \ text, no svg and no `type=\"submit\"` is a link/undo/back class, an option\
      \ control from the closed list, or an answer inside `role=\"dialog\"`/`\"alertdialog\"\
      `; no two entries inside an element marked `data-menu` share an icon; every\
      \ `data-copy` element fits its limit (reading 2b's `LIMITS`; whichever of 2a/2b\
      \ merges second wires that check).\n- Real-tree vitest: no non-test module imports\
      \ from `src/test/`.\n- ADR (next number): Boton's closed action map, the required\
      \ icon on action variants, the text-only kinds, and that back is not close.\n\
      \nAcceptance: `tsc` rejects `icon` on link/undo/back, `textOnly` without `type='submit'`,\
      \ and an icon-only `Boton` without `ariaLabel` (type-level tests); existing\
      \ suites pass unchanged."
    seams:
    - Boton component (Testing Library and its types)
    - BotonLink component
    - expectIconAndTextRule helper
    - src/test import guard (real-tree vitest)
    depends_on:
    - icon-glyphs
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-97.control-seam

  info-notice-copy:
    id: info-notice-copy
    title: '2b: MasInfo, the attention icon on Notice, and the copy-limit registry'
    brief: "- New `web/src/components/MasInfo.tsx`: a `button type=\"button\"` named\
      \ \"Más info\" with `aria-expanded` and `aria-controls`, the `info` icon in\
      \ a 44 px target at the end of its line, expanding the longer text in place\
      \ below (never a modal or sheet). State is a local `useState(false)`: nothing\
      \ in history, `localStorage` or `sessionStorage`, no part of any form's values.\
      \ The \"i\" and the text use `--tinta2` (never `--tenue`). `componentes.ts`\
      \ gains its component type.\n  Test: opens closed; a tap sets `aria-expanded=\"\
      true\"`; a second tap collapses; after remount it is closed; storage and `history.length`\
      \ unchanged; inside a form with no other change, opening it then closing the\
      \ form asks nothing.\n- `Notice` (`web/src/components/Notice.tsx`) gains an\
      \ `attention` prop drawing the `atencion` icon, `aria-hidden`, in `currentColor`,\
      \ beside the sentence; sentence and colour unchanged.\n- New `web/src/copy/limits.ts`:\
      \ `LIMITS` (help, notice, moreInfo ≤ 80; emptyTitle ≤ 40; emptyLine ≤ 80), `type\
      \ CopyKind`, `SAMPLES` (date `28/09`, amount `$ 999.999` and `−$ 999.999`, grams\
      \ `999,99 g`, a 24-character name, count `99`) and a `measure` counting code\
      \ points after NFC.\n- Each copy module named `messages.ts` or `*Messages.ts`\
      \ exports `LIMITED_COPY: readonly { kind: CopyKind; text: string }[]` (functions\
      \ called with `SAMPLES`, referring to the same binding the screen renders) and\
      \ `LONG_REFUSALS: readonly string[]`. Give today's matching modules (`screens/authMessages.ts`,\
      \ `screens/settings/messages.ts` and any other) their registries.\n- `copyLimits.test.ts`:\
      \ `import.meta.glob('/src/**/{messages,*Messages}.ts', { eager: true })`; asserts\
      \ today's modules are all found (that list written once, here), every found\
      \ module exports both registries, every entry fits its limit, and every refusal\
      \ over 80 is in its own module's `LONG_REFUSALS`.\n- Rule doc and design README\
      \ name `SAMPLES` and link `copyLimits.test.ts`."
    seams:
    - MasInfo component (Testing Library)
    - Notice attention prop (Testing Library)
    - copyLimits.test.ts over the copy registries

  closing-guards:
    id: closing-guards
    title: 'Closing: the guards that end the old shapes, and the whole-app checks'
    brief: 'After every area has merged:

      - Real-tree vitest guards: no `<svg>` outside `Icono.tsx`; a raw `<button>`
      in `screens/` or `shell/` carries only a class from the closed list (`pildora`,
      `periodos__boton`, `gastos__mes-boton`, `fila-gasto`, `ajustes__item--boton`,
      `encabezado__titulo`, or `role="switch"`/`"radio"`), written into the guard;
      no element outside `Boton` or `BotonLink` carries a `boton*` class.

      - "Every name used": each name in `ICON_NAMES` appears in a non-test file under
      `screens/`, `shell/` or `components/` other than `Icono.tsx` and `Boton.tsx`;
      a name in `Boton`''s action map counts only if some screen passes that `action`;
      `info` and `atencion` count only if `MasInfo` and `attention` have a caller
      under `screens/` or `shell/`. Remove from both `Icono.tsx` and `componentes.ts`
      any name left unused (e.g. `stock`, `whatsapp`, `telegram`), keeping `tests/test_design_icons.py`
      green.

      - Playwright: the bar fits at 320 px with text at 130 % and no icon or label
      is clipped.

      - The PR list: every shortened or moved text and where its long form went (0
      silent deletions), every test edit allowed by criterion 5 with its screen and
      control, and every area''s comparisons attached.

      Acceptance: every guard green with no exemption beyond the closed list.'
    seams:
    - real-tree guards (vitest)
    - tests/test_design_icons.py
    - Playwright bar fit at 320 px / 130 %
    depends_on:
    - area-shell-settings
    - area-integrations-catalog
    - area-varieties
    - area-stock-forms
    - area-sales
    - area-customers
    - area-home-expenses-states

invariants:
- An icon next to a visible word is aria-hidden, and adding it leaves the control's
  accessible name unchanged.
- An icon-only control comes from Boton's closed action map (delete, search, close,
  edit, share), has a required ariaLabel equal to its current word, and a 44 px target.
- 'Close is one form on every form and sheet: the cerrar icon, icon-only, accessible
  name "Cerrar"; back stays the text "‹ <parent>" and is never close.'
- Text-only controls are exactly Boton's submit, link, undo and back kinds and the
  dialog answers; any other action carries a NombreIcono.
- No icon is drawn outside Icono.tsx, every icon is 22 px at stroke 1.6, and no TRAZOS
  entry sets its own stroke, fill or transform.
- Icono.tsx and componentes.ts list the same icon names; after icon-glyphs no other
  subtask edits either file, and after control-seam no area edits Boton.tsx or BotonLink.tsx.
- No emoji is used as an interface icon.
- 'MasInfo''s open state is local component state: never in browser storage or history,
  never an unsaved change.'
- Every always-visible help line, notice and empty-state string is an exported binding
  listed in its module's LIMITED_COPY, the same binding the screen renders, and fits
  its limit measured with SAMPLES, never on fixture or API data.
- No action, route, entry order, confirmation, toast wording or tap budget changes;
  existing tests change only to find a control made icon-only by role and name, or
  for copy shortened together with its owning doc, each listed in the PR.
- No non-test module imports from web/src/test/.
- Each area edits only its own sections of app.css and leaves tsc, oxlint and its
  screens' tests green on its own branch.
integration_brief: ''
findings:
- subtask: icon-names
  version: 1
  location: web/src/screens/ventas/NuevaVenta.tsx:535
  claim: The brief's acceptance requires comparisons of the touched screens, and none
    are attached. The Developer says no pixel change is expected, but the diff redraws
    icons at new sizes and strokes on eight screens. The brief's rule to adjust compensating
    CSS only when a comparison shows a difference therefore went unapplied.
  severity: blocking
  scope: subtask
  category: spec-scope
  status: open
- subtask: icon-names
  version: 1
  location: web/src/architecture/noEmoji.real.test.ts:21
  claim: 'The guard rejects only Emoji_Presentation code points, or Extended_Pictographic
    followed by U+FE0F. The brief asks for no Extended_Pictographic code point at
    all, so ⚠ or ✏ without U+FE0F pass. The deviation has a reason: a literal guard
    would fail on the ↔ in existing comments. But it is not recorded under Decisions
    and assumptions.'
  severity: important
  scope: subtask
  category: spec-behaviour
  status: open
- subtask: icon-names
  version: 1
  location: tests/test_design_icons.py:107
  claim: glyph_problems only scans the text between `] as const` and `export default
    function Icono`. A glyph declared above ICON_NAMES, or TRAZOS moved above it,
    would escape the stroke, fill and transform check without any failure. A JSX `style={{...}}`
    stroke is also not caught.
  severity: important
  scope: subtask
  category: spec-missing-test
  status: open
- subtask: icon-names
  version: 1
  location: web/src/components/Icono.tsx:48
  claim: DOTS (`mas`) changed from filled circles of r 1.5 to stroked circles of r
    0.75. At stroke 1.6 that draws a solid dot of radius about 1.55, so the look is
    effectively unchanged, and the change is what invariant 5 requires.
  severity: observation
  scope: subtask
  category: plan-invariant
  status: dismissed
- subtask: icon-names
  version: 2

    answer carries a NombreIcono, and the screen''s design draws the trash icon next
    to the word. The union did not force this: `variant="icon" action="delete" ariaLabel="Borrar
    venta"` or an icon + text variant with `icon="tacho"` both keep the icon and the
    name.'

design_items:
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Find an entry at a glance — Más.dc.html / Configuracion.dc.html
  text: Sin prototipo for the new presentation. Más and Configuración draw only a
    trailing chevron and no leading icon on any entry, so whether each entry gets
    its own icon cannot be checked here. It is left to compare.sh and UI Lead.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Find an entry at a glance — Configuracion.dc.html
  text: 'The flow names four Configuración groups (Negocio, Usuarios, Apariencia,
    Tu cuenta), but the screen has seven: Integraciones, Mensaje del catálogo and
    Categorías de gastos are missing. The one-icon-per-entry rule must cover all seven.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Record stock and withdrawals — Mas.dc.html
  text: The flow starts a withdrawal from Más → "Sacar del stock" (as navigation.md
    says), but the prototype's Más has only Cargar gasto, Gastos, Análisis and Configuración.
    The prototype is behind the docs (#59), so this step is judged on the text alone.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Publish the pinned catalog — ConfiguracionIntegraciones.dc.html
  text: The prototype draws "Enviarme una prueba", has no "Buscar mi chat" and shows
    the old own-chat help line. The flow follows pinned-catalog.md (Probarlo, Buscar
    mi chat, new help line), so these steps are sin prototipo. The 'Más info' split
    of the own-chat help line is unverified.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Publish the pinned catalog — Catalogo.dc.html, disabled Publicar
  text: The disabled-Publicar reason "Conectá el bot en Integraciones para poder publicarlo
    en Telegram." also starts "Conectá el bot…", but it is not Inicio's after-import
    notice. The flow should say whether it gets the attention icon (it reads as a
    reason, so probably not) and confirm its link stays visible.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Read a notice — Estados.dc.html, "Arrancá por las variedades"
  text: The prototype draws it as an empty state (title, line, action "Cargá una variedad").
    The flow puts it on the attention-icon list and also says empty states get no
    icon. The flow must say which rule wins. Its line (~82 characters, two sentences)
    is also over the 80-character empty-state limit and must keep "Sin precio no se
    puede cargar una venta".
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Read a notice — Estados.dc.html, Sin conexión
  text: The prototype titles this notice "Te quedaste sin internet" with a second
    line, while the flow and accounts.md call it "Sin conexión". The flow should say
    which sentence carries the state and gets the attention icon. The server-error
    retry notice is not drawn (sin prototipo).
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Getting in — ConfiguracionCuenta.dc.html / Entrar.dc.html, Mostrar/Ocultar
  text: Asked by UX Designer. The reveal control is drawn as a text-only link "Mostrar"
    inside the field, and Entrar draws none. It swaps its word by state (Mostrar/Ocultar),
    which contradicts the PRD's 'no action swaps its word today'. The flow should
    say whether one icon or two (eye / eye-off) is used, with the word carrying the
    state.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Getting in — Entrar.dc.html, Entrar hint
  text: The prototype's hint "Te quedan 3 intentos antes de esperar 15 minutos" already
    shows an attempt count. The flow says shortening must never add one. The flow
    should say the current wording is the baseline, so the copy check compares against
    the doc and not this drawing.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Read a notice or help line — More info
  text: 'Sin prototipo: no screen draws the "i" or its in-place expansion, so the
    44 px target at the end of a 13 px help line and its fit at 320 px with 130% text
    cannot be walked.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: All screens · new icons (Más, Configuración groups, Probarlo, Buscar mi chat,
    Sacar del stock, Editar, Archivar/Restaurar, Copiar, Publicar, Mandar catálogo,
    More info 'i', attention icon)
  text: No new icon is named or described. The design only offers examples ('for example
    plug', 'search-chat and send', 'an outgoing-box') and hands the choice to UI Lead.
    NombreIcono has 13 names and no pencil, plug, send, info or attention glyph, so
    these screens cannot be built from the product's components, and there is nothing
    to judge by eye. Give each entry and action its NombreIcono name and metaphor,
    with one icon per menu entry for Más (5 entries) and Configuración (7 groups).
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: 'Screens not covered: Gastos, Nuevo gasto, Ventas, Venta detalle / Retiro
    detalle, Análisis, Cliente ficha, Nuevo precio, Todas las entradas / Ver todas,
    Negocio nuevo, Inicio primera vez, computer variants'
  text: 'The PRD asks for every screen on phone and computer. These screens have no
    section, so their concrete decisions are open: ''+ Cargar gasto'' (icon + text),
    Venta detalle''s delete (icon-only), Cliente ficha''s Editar and ''Nueva venta
    a <name>'', the help lines of the expense and price forms, and the sidebar on
    each computer layout.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: Inicio · after-import 'Conectá el bot para que el catálogo fijado se actualice
    solo'
  text: This notice is one of the six that must carry the attention icon, and it lives
    on Inicio (navigation.md, results.md; drawn in InicioPrimeraVez). The design puts
    it on Integraciones and leaves it out of Inicio's states, so the required Inicio
    state is missing.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Catálogo · disabled-Publicar reason
  text: '''Conectá el bot en Integraciones para poder publicarlo…'' is a gray help
    line with a link, not one of the six closed notices. Giving it the attention icon
    breaks the closed list and the ''absent from informational'' metric. Leave it
    without the icon.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Estados · 'Arrancá por las variedades'
  text: 'Redrawing it as a notice changes the component the product shows today (empty
    state: title, line, action). Keep the empty-state structure, place the attention
    icon next to its sentence as the PRD''s explicit list requires, and shorten the
    line to 80 characters.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Estados · server-error retry
  text: Not drawn. 'A text retry' must be stated as either a link (text only) or an
    action, and an action defaults to icon + text. Say which, and name the existing
    notice component it reuses.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Configuración · Tu cuenta
  text: Keeping Mostrar/Ocultar as a text link matches the existing design and the
    text-only rule for links, which is acceptable. 'Cambiar contraseña' and 'Cerrar
    sesión', which the flow sets as icon + text, are not mentioned.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Configuración · Integraciones
  text: The design names the states but does not say where More info sits on the own-chat
    line, or which text moves behind it. State the short line and its longer text,
    so the 80-character limit can be checked.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: 'Configuración: list of seven groups'
  text: Only Integraciones has an icon (`enchufe`). Negocio, Usuarios, Mensaje del
    catálogo, Categorías de gastos, Apariencia and Tu cuenta have none, and Tu cuenta
    is only called "account". Each of the seven needs its own distinct name in NombreIcono
    before it can be built or judged by eye.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: 'Más: entries / new icon names'
  text: The design adds `mas` (a plus) as a new name, but `mas` already exists as
    the Más tab icon (three dots, Icono.tsx). One name cannot draw two shapes. Cargar
    gasto is also given both `mas` and "recibo with a plus". Rename the plus icon
    and settle on one icon for Cargar gasto.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: Catálogo screen (Catalogo / CatalogoCompu)
  text: 'The catalog screen is gone: the "Catálogo / Variedades" section only describes
    the Variedades list. Missing: the main Compartir keeping its word, Copiar and
    Publicar as icon + text, "Desactualizado" with its reason and the attention icon,
    and "Al día" with no icon. The PRD requires all of these.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: Nueva venta, Cobro, Entrada de stock, Variedad detalle, Invitación
  text: 'These screens have no section in this version, even though the prototype
    draws all of them (NuevaVenta, Cobro, EntradaStock, VariedadDetalle, VariedadNueva,
    Invitacion). The PRD requires every screen on phone and computer. Their icons
    are also not named: "+ Nueva venta", "+ Entrada de stock", Cobrar, "Mandar catálogo"
    and "Nuevo precio".'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Inicio: after-import notice'
  text: Moving the notice to Inicio is correct. But the drawn notice has no action,
    only "×" (Descartar aviso). Adding a "Conectar" button breaks "no action added".
    Keep the notice as drawn, with `atencion` next to its sentence.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Inicio primera vez: checklist'
  text: 'The checklist rows are drawn with boxes and no icons. "Checklist rows stay
    icon + text" is wrong: they keep their boxes and "Hecho", with no icon.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Icon names
  text: '"search" and "trash" should be the existing `buscar` and `tacho`. Publicar''s
    "existing icon" does not exist in NombreIcono, so it needs a new name. Copiar
    has no icon named.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Estados: server-error retry'
  text: The retry reuses `restaurar`, the icon for Restaurar (unarchive), for a different
    meaning. Give it its own icon or justify sharing it. Also confirm that "Reintentar"
    is the app's current word.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Estados: Sin conexión'
  text: The drawn state has a title ("Te quedaste sin internet") and a line. Say which
    of the two carries `atencion`. Its line ("apostrophe necesita conexión. Reintentamos
    solos…") has two sentences and needs to become one of 80 characters or fewer.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Nuevo precio
  text: The submit is "Guardá el precio" (PrecioNuevo.dc.html), not "Guardar". Text-only
    is right, but the word must stay exact.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Gastos / Nuevo gasto
  text: The expense form has no Efectivo · Transferencia · Debiendo choice (GastoNuevo.dc.html).
    Remove that line so it does not suggest a new control.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Integraciones: own-chat help line'
  text: The design still does not write the shortened own-chat line or the text that
    goes behind More info. State both so the 80-character limit and the how/why content
    can be checked.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: More info component
  text: Building it from the existing 44px icon button plus the help-line style is
    acceptable. It has no drawn state, so it is judged by eye in compare.sh, closed
    and open, in both themes.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: Configuración → Integraciones
  text: The design uses the prototype's old "Enviarme una prueba" (`probar`), but
    the built app and pinned-catalog.md call it "Probarlo". Renaming it changes its
    accessible name, which the PRD forbids. "Buscar mi chat" is also missing, though
    the PRD requires it as icon + text.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: 'Variedad detalle / Variedades list: Archivar, Restaurar'
  text: Archivar and Restaurar have no section and no icon this round. The PRD requires
    each as icon + text with its own icon. `restaurar` is mentioned only as an aside,
    it is not in NombreIcono, and no screen uses it.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: Icon-only and icon + text controls with no icon name
  text: 'Several controls have no named icon, so they cannot be built with `Icono`:
    icon-only Editar (last round `lapiz`), Cerrar and the More info "i"; and the icon
    + text main Compartir, Cambiar contraseña and Cerrar sesión (last round `candado`
    / `salirSesion`).'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Integraciones: own-chat help line'
  text: The shortened own-chat line, its More info text and the visible short consequence
    line ("El mensaje fijado anterior queda como está…") are still not written.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Catálogo: Compartir'
  text: '"Text plus its existing treatment" is ambiguous. The PRD makes the main Compartir
    icon + text, so give it an icon.'
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Configuración → Usuarios
  text: The current word is "Invitá a un socio", not "Invitar". The design should
    also say whether the Compartir under the fresh link is icon-only (it is not that
    screen's main action).
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Cobro
  text: The submit is "Registrar". "Registrar cobro" is the sheet's title and the
    ficha's entry.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: NombreIcono
  text: Icono.tsx already draws `agregar`, `stock`, `whatsapp` and `telegram`, which
    componentes.ts does not list. Reconcile them before adding `masVenta`, `entradaStock`
    and `enviar`, so the same meaning keeps the same icon.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Variedades: secondary "Sacar del stock"'
  text: navigation.md places a secondary "Sacar del stock" on Variedades and on a
    variety's page. It should reuse `salidaStock`.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Estados: Sin conexión'
  text: Show the shortened sentence so its 80-character limit can be checked by eye.
- role_slug: pm_consolidator
  kind: observation
  step: 'Inicio, primera vez / Estados: "Arrancá por las variedades"'
  text: The prototype draws this as an empty state, but it is one of the six notices
    that carry the attention icon. The design makes it the only empty state with `atencion`.
    Its line (about 82 characters) must also be cut to fit 80.
- role_slug: pm_consolidator
  kind: observation
  step: 'Estados: "Sin conexión"'
  text: The prototype draws "Te quedaste sin internet" plus a second line. The design
    puts `atencion` on one sentence of at most 80 characters. The owning doc should
    say which sentence carries the state, so the drawing and accounts.md agree.
- role_slug: pm_consolidator
  kind: observation
  step: 'Tu cuenta / getting-in screens: password reveal'
  text: The reveal is a text link that swaps "Mostrar" and "Ocultar" (PasswordField,
    ADR-0025). That contradicts the earlier PRD line "no action swaps its word today".
    The PRD now lists the reveal as text only, with the word carrying the state, and
    adds no icon.
- role_slug: pm_consolidator
  kind: observation
  step: 'Entrar: hint under the button'
  text: '`Entrar.dc.html` shows an attempt count ("Te quedan 3 intentos…"), which
    accounts.md forbids. The "adds no information" check must use accounts.md''s fixed
    hint as its baseline, not this drawing.'
- role_slug: pm_consolidator
  kind: observation
  step: 'Catálogo: disabled Publicar reason'
  text: The disabled-Publicar reason, like the after-import notice, can start with
    "Conectá el bot…". The design gives it no attention icon. Its wording should stay
    distinct from the after-import notice so the two aren't read as the same thing.
- role_slug: pm_consolidator
  kind: observation
  step: 'Integraciones: Buscar mi chat; Catálogo: main Compartir; every form: Cerrar;
    More info "i"'
  text: Decision 1 says every control needs a `NombreIcono` name, but these four are
    still unnamed. Name them at build, reusing anything `Icono.tsx` already draws,
    and add each to `componentes.ts` in the same diff.
- role_slug: pm_consolidator
  kind: observation
  step: 'Usuarios: Compartir under the fresh join link'
  text: This Compartir is icon-only (an obvious action). Its accessible name stays
    "Compartir", and "Invitá a un socio" keeps its word with `personaMas`.
- role_slug: pm_consolidator
  kind: observation
  step: 'Design system: NombreIcono'
  text: '`Icono.tsx` already draws `agregar`, `stock`, `whatsapp` and `telegram`,
    which `componentes.ts` does not list. Reconcile the two lists before adding any
    plus or stock icon (`masVenta`, `masGasto`, `entradaStock`, `salidaStock`), and
    reuse an existing drawing where the meaning is the same.'
- role_slug: pm_consolidator
  kind: observation
  step: More info and the server-error retry
  text: Neither has a drawn state in `pantallas/`. Both are assembled from the 44
    px icon button, the help line and the offline notice. Judge them by eye in `compare.sh`'s
    after pass, phone and computer, both themes.
- role_slug: pm_consolidator
  kind: observation
  step: Más / Integraciones (prototype lag)
  text: 'The prototype is behind navigation.md and pinned-catalog.md: it doesn''t
    draw "Sacar del stock" in Más or "Buscar mi chat", and it shows the old own-chat
    help line. The before pass of `compare.sh` will show those differences. List them
    in the PR as differences, not defects of this task.'
- role_slug: ux_lead_final
  kind: observation
  step: final
  text: None of the new names the validated design specifies were drawn (lapiz, cerrar,
