# Balance icons and text across the app

## Original request

### Problem

Using the app on the phone, the seller (Zeta) finds it has **too few icons and too much text**. Screens read as walls of words; finding an entry or an action takes reading instead of a glance. We need a balance, not an icon-only UI.

Today the design system (`docs/product/design-system/componentes.ts`, `NombreIcono`) has 13 icons (the five tabs, back, chevron, down, search, calendar, trash, cobro, personaMas), stroke 1.6 at 22px, and the rule "never emoji as an interface icon".

### Scope: every screen

All of these, in one pass over the whole app:

1. **Lists and menus** (Más, Configuración groups, list rows): an icon next to each entry so it can be found at a glance.
2. **Buttons and actions** (add, delete, edit, share, charge, publish…): icons on actions.
3. **Help texts and notices** (help lines under fields, empty states, explanations, banners): shorten them, and where a long explanation is still needed, keep it reachable (e.g. behind an info affordance) instead of always on screen.

### Decisions already taken (by Zeta)

- **The system proposes the design.** PM and UI Lead define the rule and extend the icon set; ask Zeta only what is genuinely doubtful.
- **Icon-only is allowed only for obvious actions**: delete, search, close, share, edit (and similar universally understood ones). Those still carry an accessible name (`aria-label`). **Navigation and menus always show icon + text** (navigation.md: "no tab is an icon alone" stays).

### Wanted

- A written rule in the product docs / design system: when an icon, when icon + text, when text only; maximum length for help lines; what moves out of always-visible text.
- The icon set extended in the design system (same stroke and size, one consistent family, no emoji), with the new names in `NombreIcono`.
- Every screen brought in line with the rule, with the copy shortened where it says the same thing twice.
- No behaviour change: same actions, same routes, same confirmations; this is presentation and copy only.

### Sequencing

Touches every screen, so it should run after the in-flight UI work merges (#59 navigation, #47 Variedades, #77, #78) to avoid a parallel rewrite of the same files.

## PjM

Visible UI change (new icons, shortened copy, help text moved behind info affordance) and a new design-system rule; product docs must change, so it is a feature.

## Interview: Product Analyst→PM

### Round 1: questions

### Round 1: the job to be done

I could not load the `grilling` skill here, so I am running the interview directly in its format. This round covers only the job. Behaviour comes after you settle it.

**Recommended job story**

> When I'm on my phone between orders or in the middle of a chat with a customer and I need to find an entry or an action, I want to recognise it at a glance by its icon and a short label, so I can get to it without reading blocks of text and get back to the customer.

This refines the job navigation.md already serves, which is getting around the app on the phone. It is not a new top-level job.

❓ **Q1** - **Situation**: When does Zeta reach for this? The request says the app has "too few icons and too much text". I read that as a scanning problem while Zeta is busy: standing, on the phone, with a customer waiting in the chat. It is not a reading problem at a desk. Is that the situation, or does it matter at other times too, for example when setting up the catalogue or the Configuración groups?
➡️ Recommendation: Take the busy, mid-chat, one-handed phone situation as the main one. The setup screens follow the same rule, but we don't design for them separately.

❓ **Q2** - **Progress wanted**: What progress does Zeta want? I propose three things:
- Recognise each menu or list entry and each action from its icon plus a short label.
- See help, notices and empty states as one short line, not a block.
- Still reach the long explanation when needed, behind an info affordance.

➡️ Recommendation: Confirm these three. Nothing else is in the job, so there is no new capability, only a clearer way to scan the existing ones.

❓ **Q3** - **What today looks like without it**: Today Zeta reads labels and help lines to tell entries apart. Many screens carry long fixed texts, such as the own-chat help line, the Entrar brake hint, the "El mensaje fijado anterior queda como está; desfijalo vos en Telegram." line, the archive confirmation, and the empty states. Does that match what Zeta experiences? Is there a screen where it hurts most (Más, Configuración, a list)?
➡️ Recommendation: Treat that as the baseline. If one screen annoys Zeta most, name it. We will use it as the first check, but every screen still follows the rule.

❓ **Q4** - **How Zeta will judge it went well**: I recommend three success criteria:
1. Every menu or list entry and every action shows an icon together with its text. Icon-only appears only for the obvious actions (delete, search, close, share, edit), each with an accessible `aria-label`. Navigation and menus keep icon + text, so "no tab is an icon alone" stays.
2. Always-visible help, notice and empty-state text is cut to a short maximum written in the design system. Longer explanations sit behind an info affordance.
3. No behaviour change: the same actions, routes and confirmations. Existing tests pass unchanged, except where the copy is deliberately shortened and the docs and tests change together.

➡️ Recommendation: Adopt these three. If you want one more, I would add "a written rule in the product docs says when to use icon, icon + text or text only", so the next screen follows it without asking.

❓ **Q5** - **Where this stops**: The job is about recognising and reading less, not about changing what the app does. Do you agree that this feature does not add or remove any action, or reorder any menu? Reordering would be a different job.
➡️ Recommendation: Agree. Scope is limited to icons, shortened copy and the info affordance, with the order and content of entries unchanged.

### Round 1: answers

### Round 1: the job

**Job story (decided):**

> **When** I'm on my phone between orders or mid-chat with a customer and I need to find an entry, an action or what a notice is telling me, **I want** to recognise it at a glance by its icon and a short label, and read any help or notice as one short line, **so I can** get to it without reading blocks of text and get back to the customer.

This refines the navigation job (navigation.md) and the "it feels like a phone app" job (accounts.md). It is not a new top-level job, and it adds no capability. The protagonist is the seller. The operator's terminal tools are outside it.

**Success criteria (decided):**

1. **Found at a glance.** Every entry in Más, in Configuración's list of groups and in list rows, and every action button, shows an icon next to its text.
   - Icon-only is allowed only for the obvious actions: delete, search, close, share, edit, and others just as universally understood that the rule lists by name. Each one has an accessible name.
   - Navigation and menus always show icon + text. "No tab is an icon alone" stays.
2. **One rule, written once.** The design system and the product docs state the same rule:
   - when to use an icon alone, icon + text, or text only;
   - the maximum length of an always-visible help line or notice;
   - what moves behind an info affordance and what must never move there.

   The next screen follows the rule without asking anyone.
3. **Short by default, nothing lost.** Always-visible help lines, notices and empty states fit the written maximum, and text that says the same thing twice is cut. A longer explanation that is still needed stays one tap away, behind the info affordance. It is never deleted silently.
4. **One icon family.** Every icon follows the existing stroke and size and is named in `NombreIcono`, in both themes. No emoji appears as an interface icon. No state is told by an icon alone or by colour alone: the word or shape that carries a state today keeps carrying it.
5. **Nothing else changes.** Actions, routes, order of entries, confirmations and tap budgets all stay the same, and so does every rule about what a screen reveals (brake, dead link, "Eso ya no está."). Existing tests pass unchanged, except where a piece of copy is deliberately shortened. In that case the doc and the test change in the same diff.
6. **Looks as designed.** `scripts/design/compare.sh` runs before and after for every screen, phone and computer, both themes, and the result is judged by eye.

**Q1**: Accepted. The main situation is the busy, mid-chat, one-handed phone use. Setup screens (Configuración groups, Integraciones, the catalog) follow the same rule and are not designed separately. The computer is the same job on a bigger screen.

**Q2**: Accepted, with one limit on the info affordance. Copy that tells the seller a consequence they would not otherwise expect stays visible, in short form, and never moves behind the info affordance. Examples: the old pinned message staying as it is, the stock-on-hand figure in the archive confirmation, the grams that return to stock on delete. Only a background explanation, the "how" or "why", may move behind it.

**Q3**: Accepted as the baseline. Assumption: the first screens to check are Más, Configuración's groups (Integraciones above all, with its help lines) and the Variedades page and list. This does not change the PRD, because every screen follows the rule anyway.

**Q4**: Accepted, and your extra criterion is in: it is criterion 2 above. I added criteria 4 and 6.
- Criterion 4 is there because adding icons must not weaken the existing "never by colour alone" and state-marking rules.
- Criterion 6 is there because every screen's look changes, and the existing design check is how we see that.

The fixed security texts are unchanged in meaning. Shortening one of them may never add information: no attempt count, no reason a link is dead. These are the brake message, the Entrar hint and the dead-link texts.

**Q5**: Agreed. Nothing is added, removed or reordered, and no route or confirmation changes. Toasts keep their wording, because they are already short and fixed, and some tests depend on them. A confirmation question may be shortened, but it keeps every fact it names today: date, amount, grams, reason.

### Round 2: questions

### Round 2: behaviour that serves the job

The job and the six criteria are settled. I won't reopen them. These questions cover the rule itself. Each one can be answered on its own.

❓ **Q1** - **Which actions count as "obvious" for icon-only**: You named delete, search, close, share and edit "and similar". The rule has to list them by name, so a screen can't decide for itself. Today `NombreIcono` has `tacho` and `buscar`. `Boton` takes an optional `icono` and `children` as its text. Which of these may be icon-only? Candidates: delete, search, close, share, edit, back, copy, and the "more options" dots. The ones left out always show icon + text: add, charge, publish, archive, pin.
➡️ Recommendation: Icon-only for delete, search, close, share, edit and back. Back is already an icon today. Copy, "more options", add, charge, publish, archive and pin always carry text. A single-letter-sized word costs less than a wrong tap. Anything not on the list defaults to icon + text.

❓ **Q2** - **Maximum length of an always-visible line**: The rule needs a number. Help lines, notices and empty states are the cases. On a 360px phone, one line is about 40 characters and two lines about 80.
➡️ Recommendation: Help line or notice: at most one sentence and 80 characters, so two lines on the phone. Empty state: a title of at most 40 characters plus one line of at most 80, and an action if there is one. Anything longer goes behind the info affordance, except consequences, which you already decided stay visible in short form.

❓ **Q3** - **What the info affordance is**: It is a term the glossary doesn't have. The seller needs one consistent gesture to reach the longer explanation. The decisions already taken fix its content: it holds the "how" and "why" only. They don't fix its form. A tap on an "i" icon next to the line could open a small panel, a bottom sheet or an inline expansion.
➡️ Recommendation: One "i" icon with an accessible name, in a 44px touch target, next to the shortened line. A tap expands the long text in place under the line, and a second tap collapses it. It is not a modal or a sheet: it doesn't take the seller out of the screen they are on. I will add it to the glossary as a term with `_In the app_`.

❓ **Q4** - **Icon for entries that have no obvious picture**: Más, Configuración's groups and list rows include entries like Integraciones or Variedades. Some entries have no universal icon. Where there is no obvious one, a decorative icon would be worse than none.
➡️ Recommendation: Every entry gets an icon, and the icon is a recognisable cue and never the only carrier of meaning. The text always stays. Where no obvious picture exists, choose a neutral family icon and judge it by eye in the design check. The new names are added to `NombreIcono` only when an entry or action uses them, so the set doesn't grow with unused icons.

❓ **Q5** - **Where the list rows get their icon**: List rows such as customers, sales or varieties already have content: a name, an amount, a state. Adding a leading icon to each row could compete with the state marking you protected in criterion 4.
➡️ Recommendation: A leading icon goes on rows that are entries to somewhere (Más, Configuración groups) and on action rows. Rows that are records (a customer, a sale, a variety) keep their current layout, because their content already identifies them and a repeated icon would add noise. Row actions follow the button rule from Q1.

### Round 2: answers

### Round 2: the rule

**Q1**: Accepted with two changes.
- **Back stays out of the icon-only list.** navigation.md defines back as "‹ <parent>", which names the screen it returns to. That label is navigation, and navigation always shows icon + text. It stays as it is.
- **Being on the list allows icon-only; it does not require it.** The main action of a screen always carries its text, even when the action is on the list. Compartir on the catalog screen is one example: it keeps its word.

The closed list for icon-only is delete, search, close and edit, plus share where it is not the main action.
- Each one keeps its exact current word as its accessible name ("Cerrar", "Editar", "Borrar…", "Buscar", "Compartir"). The existing tests that find these controls by name still pass, and "Cerrar, never Volver" still holds.
- Every action not on the list is icon + text. That includes copy, "more options", add, charge (Cobrar), publish, archive, restore and pin.
- An action that is not listed is icon + text by default.
- Adding an action to the list changes the rule doc. A screen cannot do it on its own.

**Q2**: Accepted.
- **Help line or notice:** one sentence, at most 80 characters.
- **Empty state:** a title of at most 40 characters plus one line of at most 80, plus its action if it has one.
- Consequences stay visible in short form, as decided in round 1.
- **Inline refusals and Probarlo or Buscar mi chat reasons** say what to fix. They aim for the same 80, and are shortened where they repeat themselves. They are never moved behind More info, because the seller needs the fix on screen at the moment the refusal shows. A reason that cannot fit in 80 without losing its fix keeps that fix.
- **Fixed security texts** keep their meaning and add no information: the brake message, the Entrar hint and the dead-link texts.

**Q3**: Accepted: one "i" in a 44 px target, expanding in place and collapsing on a second tap, with no modal or sheet. The glossary term is **More info**, defined as "the longer how-or-why behind a shortened line, one tap away, expanded in place; never a consequence, a refusal's fix or a state". _In the app_ its accessible name is "Más info", and it states whether it is open or closed (`aria-expanded`). It always opens closed. Whether it was opened is not remembered: the browser stores only the theme choice. Opening it is not an unsaved change and never triggers the leave confirmation. Assumption: the "i" sits at the end of the line it explains.

**Q4**: Accepted, with one change.
- **Prefer a specific picture over a neutral one.** A generic placeholder icon defeats the glance this job is about. Where no universal picture exists, choose a specific metaphor from the same family, for example a plug or a bot for Integraciones, and judge it by eye in the design check.
- **No reused icons within one menu.** Within one menu (Más, Configuración's groups, the bar, the sidebar), two entries never share an icon.
- **Same meaning, same icon.** The same action uses the same icon everywhere.
- **Names only when used.** Names are added to `NombreIcono` only when a screen uses them.

**Q5**: Accepted.
- **Leading icons:** entry rows (Más, Configuración's list of groups) and action rows get one.
- **Record rows stay as they are:** customers, sales, varieties, entries, withdrawals, expenses, price entries and the account's rows keep their layout and their state marks (`MarcaEstado`, amber "te debe", "A favor").
- **Actions on record rows** follow Q1. "Cobrar" keeps its text and its accessible name "Cobrar a <name>".

### Round 3: questions

### Round 3: where text alone is still right

Round 2 is settled: the closed icon-only list, the 80 and 40 character limits, More info, one icon per entry in a menu, and record rows unchanged. What's left is the third case of your rule, text only. Each question can be answered on its own.

❓ **Q1** - **Which actions stay text only**: Criterion 1 says every action button shows an icon. Some actions have no picture that adds anything: Guardar, Cancelar, Seguir, Listo, "Sí, borrar". Forcing an icon on them would work against the glance this job is about. The rule needs to say which ones stay text only. Today `Boton` has four variants: `primario`, `suave`, `enlace` and `destructivo`, and `icono` is optional on all of them.
➡️ Recommendation: Text only for these cases:
- the `enlace` variant, which is a link and not a button;
- the dismiss and confirm pair inside a confirmation (Cancelar, "Sí, borrar", Seguir);
- the plain submit of a form (Guardar).

Every other `primario` or `suave` action carries icon + text. The rule lists the text-only cases by name, as it does for icon-only, so no screen decides on its own.

❓ **Q2** - **Buttons that change meaning with state**: Some actions swap their word by state, for example publish and unpublish, pin and unpin, archive and restore. If both states share one icon, the seller can't see the state change from the icon. You already decided no state is told by an icon alone, so the word has to keep carrying it. The open point is whether the icon follows the state or stays fixed.
➡️ Recommendation: Each state of a swapping action keeps its own word, and the icon stays the same for the same action (same meaning, same icon). The word carries the state, so the icon never has to. Where a state reads better with a distinct icon (for example a pin and a crossed-out pin), the rule allows a second icon. Both names are added to `NombreIcono` only if a screen uses them.

❓ **Q3** - **Banners and notices that carry a state**: Banners and notices include some that tell the seller something is wrong or waiting, such as a stale connection or a chat that needs attention. An icon can help the glance here, but colour already does part of the work, and "no state by colour alone" holds. The question is whether a notice gets a leading icon of its own.
➡️ Recommendation: A notice may carry one leading icon, and it is always optional. The shortened sentence stays the carrier of the state. The icon never replaces it and never appears without it. A notice that is purely informational gets no icon, so the icon keeps meaning "this needs your attention". The rule doc records this next to the 80 character limit.

### Round 3: answers

### Round 3: text only

**Q1**: Accepted. The text-only cases join the rule as a closed list, alongside the icon-only list. Criterion 1 now reads "every action shows an icon with its text, except the two named lists."

The text-only cases are:
- **Links (`enlace`).** These are links set in a line or under a section, such as "Editar encabezado y pie" or "Ver todas". Edit stays icon-only where Q1 of round 2 allows it.
- **The answer pair inside a confirmation.** They keep their exact current words: "No, dejala / Sí, borrala", "No, seguí cargando / Sí, descartala", and the rest.
- **The submit of a form or sheet.** "Guardá la venta", "Guardá el aviso", "Guardá el catálogo", "Registrar", "Guardar y entrar", "Entrá" and the rest keep their exact words.
- **Choices among options.** These are not actions. They include the period tabs (Hoy · Semana · Mes · Fechas), Efectivo · Transferencia · Debiendo, Promoción · Consumo propio, the origin of an entry, Claro · Oscuro, and "Todo: $ X".
- **Deshacer inside "Venta guardada".** It is part of a notice. The notice stays short and carries no data.

Every other `primario` or `suave` action carries icon + text. Examples are "+ Nueva venta", "+ Entrada de stock", "Sacar del stock", "Mandar catálogo", Cobrar, Publicar, Probarlo, Buscar mi chat, Archivar, Restaurar, "Invitá a un socio", Copiar and the main Compartir. A new action that fits neither list is icon + text.

**Q2**: Accepted in principle, but there is less to design than the question assumes. The app has no action that swaps its word by state: Publicar has no unpublish, and nothing in the app pins or unpins.
- **Archivar and Restaurar** are two different actions on two different screens. Each one has its own icon, and the word names each.
- **The low-stock warning's on/off switch** is a switch, not a button. It keeps its own on/off shape and its words ("Aviso apagado", "Avisame abajo de…"), with no icon added.
- The rule still records the general case for any future swapping action: the word carries the state, and a second icon is allowed only where a screen uses it.

**Q3**: Overruled on "optional." An optional icon puts the choice back on each screen, which the rule exists to prevent. One attention icon, from the family, goes on every notice or status that asks the seller to act or tells them something failed, and it always sits next to its sentence. A purely informational line gets none. The rule doc lists both groups by name:

- **Attention (icon + sentence):**
  - Inicio's "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado";
  - the "Desactualizado" status with its reason;
  - "Arrancá por las variedades";
  - the after-import "Conectá el bot…";
  - "Sin conexión";
  - the generic server-error retry.
- **No icon:**
  - "Al día";
  - "Eso ya no está." and its list form;
  - toasts;
  - the first-run checklist, which keeps its boxes and "Hecho";
  - help lines and empty states.

These notices are not told by colour today, and adding the icon changes neither colour nor wording. The sentence stays the carrier, and the "Al día" / "Desactualizado" words still tell the state.

### Closing

### Closing summary

The interview is complete. Nothing is left for you to decide, and the PRD can be written from this.

**Job (round 1, settled).** When the seller is on the phone between orders or mid-chat, they want to recognise an entry, an action or a notice at a glance by its icon and a short label, and to read help as one short line. That way they can get to it without reading blocks of text and get back to the customer. This refines the navigation job and adds no capability. It is judged by six criteria:
- found at a glance;
- one rule written once;
- short by default with nothing lost;
- one icon family;
- no behaviour change;
- looks as designed, checked with `scripts/design/compare.sh`.

**The rule (rounds 2 and 3, settled).**
- **Icon-only (closed list).** Delete, search, close and edit, plus share where it is not the screen's main action.
  - The accessible name is the current word ("Cerrar", "Editar", "Borrar…", "Buscar", "Compartir").
  - Back is not on the list, because back is navigation and keeps its label.
  - The main action of a screen always carries its text.
- **Text only (closed list).**
  - Links (`enlace`).
  - The answer pair inside a confirmation.
  - The submit of a form or sheet.
  - Choices among options, which are not actions.
  - Deshacer inside the "Venta guardada" notice.
- **Everything else** is icon + text, and so is any new action that fits neither list. Adding to either list changes the rule doc, never a single screen.
- **Navigation and menus** always show icon + text.
- **Entry rows** (Más, Configuración's groups) and action rows get a leading icon. Record rows keep their layout and their state marks.
- **Icons.**
  - Prefer a specific metaphor over a neutral one.
  - Within one menu, no two entries share an icon.
  - The same meaning gets the same icon everywhere.
  - Names are added to `NombreIcono` only when a screen uses them.
  - The family stays at stroke 1.6 and 22px, with no emoji.
- **Length.**
  - A help line or notice is one sentence of at most 80 characters.
  - An empty state has a title of at most 40 characters, plus one line of at most 80 and its action.
  - Inline refusals and Probarlo or Buscar mi chat reasons aim for 80 and keep their fix.
- **More info (new glossary term).**
  - It is an "i" in a 44 px target at the end of the line it explains.
  - It expands in place and collapses on a second tap, always opens closed, and is not remembered.
  - Its accessible name is "Más info", with `aria-expanded`.
  - It never triggers the leave confirmation.
  - It holds the "how" or "why" only. A consequence, a refusal's fix or a state is never moved behind it.
- **Notices.**
  - One attention icon goes on every notice that asks the seller to act or says something failed: Inicio's stale catalog, "Desactualizado" with its reason, "Arrancá por las variedades", the after-import "Conectá el bot…", "Sin conexión" and the server-error retry.
  - It sits next to its sentence, which stays the carrier of the state.
  - Informational lines get none: "Al día", "Eso ya no está.", toasts, the first-run checklist, help lines and empty states.
- **Swapping actions.** No action swaps its word by state today. The rule records the general case: the word carries the state.

**What does not change.**
- Actions, routes, the order of entries and confirmations.
- Toast wording.
- The security texts keep their meaning and add no information. These are the brake message, the Entrar hint and the dead-link texts.
- Existing tests pass unchanged, except where copy is deliberately shortened. Then the doc and the test change in the same diff.

**Sequencing.** This runs after #59, #47, #77 and #78 merge. The first screens to check are Más, Configuración's groups (Integraciones above all) and the Variedades page and list.

**Open items for the human.** None.

## Product Analyst

### Job to be done

**Job story (decided):** When I'm on my phone between orders or mid-chat with a customer and I need to find an entry, an action or what a notice is telling me, I want to recognise it at a glance by its icon and a short label, and read any help or notice as one short line, so I can get to it without reading blocks of text and get back to the customer.

This refines the navigation job (navigation.md) and the "feels like a phone app" job (accounts.md). It is not a new capability. The protagonist is the seller. The main situation is busy, one-handed use on the phone. Setup screens (Configuración, Integraciones, the catalog) follow the same rule and are not designed apart. The computer is the same job on a bigger screen.

**Success criteria (decided):**
1. **Found at a glance.** Every entry in Más, in Configuración's list of groups and in list rows, and every action, shows an icon next to its text, except the two closed lists below (icon-only, text only). Navigation and menus always show icon + text, and "no tab is an icon alone" stays.
2. **One rule, written once.** The design system and the product docs state the same rule: icon only, icon + text or text only; the maximum length of a line; what goes behind More info and what never does. The next screen follows it without asking.
3. **Short by default, nothing lost.** Always-visible help lines, notices and empty states fit the written maximum, and text that says the same thing twice is cut. A needed long explanation stays one tap away behind More info and is never deleted silently.
4. **One icon family, no state by icon or colour alone.** Every icon keeps stroke 1.6 and 22px, is named in `NombreIcono` and works in both themes. No emoji is used as an interface icon. The word or shape that carries a state today keeps carrying it.
5. **Nothing else changes.** Actions, routes, order of entries, confirmations, toast wording and tap budgets stay. Every rule about what a screen reveals stays. Existing tests pass unchanged, except where copy is deliberately shortened. Then the doc and the test change in the same diff.
6. **Looks as designed.** `scripts/design/compare.sh` runs before and after for every screen, phone and computer, both themes, and the result is judged by eye.

**Asked for but left out, because it does not serve the job:**
- An icon-only UI. Icon-only is allowed only for the closed list below.
- Back as icon-only. Back is navigation ("‹ <parent>") and keeps its label.
- Changing the main action of a screen to icon-only, even where its action is on the list.
- Adding, removing or reordering any action or menu entry, or changing any route or confirmation.
- An icon on record rows (customer, sale, variety, entry, withdrawal, expense, price entry, account rows).
- An optional icon on notices, and any new icon not used by a screen.
- Moving a consequence, a refusal's fix or a state behind More info.

### Competitors

I have no real data on how other products handle this, and I will not invent it. The criteria come from the decisions and the existing design system. These are the criteria the product docs already state:
- Navigation always shows icon + text (navigation.md).
- No state is told by colour alone (accounts.md).
- Touch targets are at least 44px and text contrast is at least 4.5:1 (ADR-0029).

If PM wants a comparison with named products, it needs a separate, sourced review.

### Behaviour definition

**1. Where each form applies.** Every action is one of three kinds. The lists are closed. Adding to either list changes the rule doc, never a single screen.

- **Icon-only** (an icon, with an accessible name that is the exact current word):
  - delete ("Borrar…"), search ("Buscar"), close ("Cerrar"), edit ("Editar");
  - share ("Compartir") only where it is not the screen's main action.
  - The control stays at least 44px. The accessible name does not change, so tests that find these by name still pass, and "Cerrar, never Volver" still holds.
  - The main action of a screen always carries its text, even when the action is on this list. Compartir on the catalog screen keeps its word.
- **Text only:**
  - links (`enlace`), such as "Editar encabezado y pie" and "Ver todas";
  - the answer pair inside a confirmation, with its exact current words ("No, dejala / Sí, borrala", "No, seguí cargando / Sí, descartala"…);
  - the submit of a form or sheet ("Guardá la venta", "Guardá el aviso", "Guardá el catálogo", "Registrar", "Guardar y entrar", "Entrá"…);
  - choices among options, which are not actions: period tabs (Hoy · Semana · Mes · Fechas), Efectivo · Transferencia · Debiendo, Promoción · Consumo propio, an entry's origin, Claro · Oscuro, "Todo: $ X";
  - Deshacer inside "Venta guardada".
- **Icon + text:** everything else. This includes "+ Nueva venta", "+ Entrada de stock", "Sacar del stock", "Mandar catálogo", Cobrar (accessible name stays "Cobrar a <name>"), Publicar, Probarlo, Buscar mi chat, Archivar, Restaurar, "Invitá a un socio", Copiar and the main Compartir. A new action that fits neither list is icon + text by default.

**2. Navigation, menus and rows.**
- The bar, the sidebar, Más and Configuración's list of groups always show icon + text. Back stays "‹ <parent>".
- Entry rows (Más, Configuración's groups) and action rows get a leading icon.
- Record rows keep their layout and their state marks (`MarcaEstado`, amber "te debe", "A favor"). Actions on record rows follow section 1.
- Within one menu (Más, Configuración's groups, the bar, the sidebar), no two entries share an icon.

**3. Icons.**
- One family, stroke 1.6, 22px, no emoji.
- Prefer a specific metaphor over a neutral one (for example a plug or a bot for Integraciones). A generic placeholder icon is not acceptable.
- The same meaning gets the same icon everywhere.
- Names are added to `NombreIcono` only when a screen uses them. The 13 existing names stay.
- Each new icon is judged by eye in the design check.

**4. Length of always-visible text.**
- A help line or notice is one sentence of at most 80 characters.
- An empty state has a title of at most 40 characters plus one line of at most 80, and its action if it has one.
- Inline refusals, and the reasons of Probarlo and Buscar mi chat, aim for 80. They stay on screen and keep what the seller must fix. A reason that cannot fit in 80 without losing its fix keeps the fix.
- Consequences stay visible in short form: the old pinned message staying as it is, the grams on hand in the archive confirmation, the grams that return to stock on delete.
- A confirmation question may be shortened, but it keeps every fact it names today (date, amount, grams, reason).
- Fixed security texts keep their meaning and add no information: the brake message, the Entrar hint and the dead-link texts. Shortening them never adds an attempt count or a reason a link is dead.

**5. More info (new term).**
- It is an "i" icon in a 44px target at the end of the line it explains.
- A tap expands the longer text in place under the line. A second tap collapses it. It is not a modal or a sheet.
- It always opens closed, and whether it was opened is not remembered (the browser stores only the theme choice).
- Its accessible name is "Más info", with `aria-expanded` stating open or closed.
- Opening it is not an unsaved change and never triggers the leave confirmation.
- It holds only the "how" or "why". It never holds a consequence, a refusal's fix or a state.
- Empty case: a line whose explanation fits in 80 characters has no More info.
- Assumption: the "i" sits at the end of the line it explains.

**6. Notices.**
- One attention icon, mandatory, sits next to the sentence on every notice or status that asks the seller to act or says something failed:
  - Inicio's "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado";
  - "Desactualizado" with its reason;
  - "Arrancá por las variedades";
  - the after-import "Conectá el bot…";
  - "Sin conexión";
  - the server-error retry.
- The sentence stays the carrier of the state. The icon never replaces it and never appears without it. Colour and wording do not change.
- These get no icon: "Al día", "Eso ya no está." and its list form, toasts, the first-run checklist (it keeps its boxes and "Hecho"), help lines and empty states.

**7. Actions that swap their word by state.** None exists today. Archivar and Restaurar are two actions on two screens, each with its own icon. The low-stock warning's on/off switch is a switch, not a button, and gets no icon. The rule records the general case: the word carries the state, and a second icon is allowed only where a screen uses it.

**8. What does not change.**
- Actions, routes, order of entries, confirmations, toast wording and tap budgets.
- Every rule about what a screen reveals.
- Existing tests pass unchanged, except where copy is deliberately shortened. Then the doc and the test change in the same diff.

**9. Where the rule is written.** The rule lives in the design-system README and in the product docs, stated the same way. CONTEXT.md gains the term More info (_In the app_: Más info). `componentes.ts` gains the new `NombreIcono` names and, for More info, a component type.

**10. Screens.** Every screen is brought in line, phone and computer, both themes. Check first: Más, Configuración's groups (Integraciones above all) and the Variedades page and list.

**11. Sequencing.** The work starts after #59, #47, #77 and #78 merge.

### Metrics and dashboard

There is no telemetry. Metrics come from tests and the design check, and they ship with the feature.

- **Found at a glance (criterion 1).** A front test over every screen in `scripts/design/screens.json` checks that:
  - each entry row and each action not on a closed list has an icon and its text;
  - each icon-only control has the exact accessible name of its current word and a 44px target;
  - no menu has two entries with the same icon.
  - Target: 0 exceptions.
- **One rule (criterion 2).** A check that the README and the product docs state the same two closed lists and the same limits, and that every `NombreIcono` name is used by at least one screen. Target: 0 unused names.
- **Short by default (criterion 3).**
  - A test over the fixed copy asserts that help lines and notices are at most 80 characters, and empty-state titles at most 40 with one line of at most 80.
  - A test shows More info opens closed, toggles `aria-expanded`, never triggers the leave confirmation and holds no consequence or fix.
  - A review list shows every shortened or moved text, with where its long form went. Target: 0 silent deletions.
- **Icon family and states (criterion 4).**
  - A guard that icons use stroke 1.6 and 22px and that no emoji appears as an interface icon.
  - Front tests show every state still carries its word or shape in both themes, with no state told by icon or colour alone.
  - The attention icon is present on each listed notice and absent from the informational ones.
- **Nothing else changes (criterion 5).**
  - The existing suites pass; the only edited tests are those of deliberately shortened copy, listed in the PR with their doc change.
  - Tap budgets and the redirect table are unchanged.
  - The brake message, the Entrar hint and the dead-link texts add no information, checked by test.
- **Looks as designed (criterion 6).** `scripts/design/compare.sh` runs before and after for every screen, phone and computer, both themes, attached to the PR and judged by eye. A by-eye pass at 320px with text at 130% shows no icon or label clipped, and the bar still fits.
- **Dashboard.** None is added. The feature adds no business metric, so nothing new is shown to the seller.

### Flags

- `touches_ui`: yes. New icons, icon + text on actions, the More info affordance and attention icons on notices all change what the user sees and taps.
- `touches_copy`: yes. Help lines, notices, empty states and some refusals are shortened, and "Más info" is new user-facing text.

### For the next role

What PM needs to validate:
- The two closed lists (icon-only, text only) are as decided in rounds 2 and 3, and everything else is icon + text.
- The attention icon is mandatory on the six listed notices and absent from the informational ones, as PM overruled in round 3.
- The 80 and 40 character limits and the exceptions: consequences, refusal fixes and security texts stay visible.
- More info is as described, with the accessible name "Más info". The only assumption is that the "i" sits at the end of its line.
- This feature adds no action, route, confirmation or toast change, and it touches existing tests only where copy is shortened.
- The new glossary term More info (and `NombreIcono` names added only when used) is the only product-knowledge change besides the written rule.
- No dashboard is added, and the by-eye `compare.sh` check is the main gate for the look.

## PM (v1)

### Verdict

`approved`: the PRD matches every decision from rounds 1–3 and serves the settled job. I closed the remaining small gaps myself (below).

### Adjustments

1. **Accessible names when an icon sits next to a word.** In icon + text, the icon is decorative and hidden from assistive technology, so the control's accessible name stays its word. Without this, adding an icon could change names that existing tests look controls up by. That would break criterion 5. This was missing; I added it to sections 1 and 3.
2. **More info's "i" is the one icon-only control outside the action list.** The closed icon-only list covers actions. More info is an affordance with its own term, its own fixed name "Más info" and its own icon in the family. I wrote this into section 1 so no screen reads it as a sixth entry on the list.
3. **The attention icon is one icon.** The same family icon goes on every listed notice ("same meaning, same icon"). It is decorative next to its sentence. I made this explicit in section 6 and added "Attention icon" to the glossary, so it isn't redefined screen by screen.
4. **The unused-name metric now covers new names only.** As written, it could fail on one of the 13 existing names, which the PRD itself keeps. The rule from the interview is "added only when a screen uses them", so the check applies to the names this task adds.
5. **The length check is measurable.** The hard 80 and 40 limits apply to help lines, notices and empty states, counted in characters of the Spanish copy as shown, `dd/mm` dates and figures substituted as written. Refusals and the reasons of Probarlo and Buscar mi chat aim for 80 but are listed for review rather than failed, because a fix that can't fit keeps the fix (round 2).
6. **Where the rule lives in the product docs.** The rule gets its own capability file, `icon-and-text-rule.md`, and two glossary terms, "Icon and text rule" and "Attention icon", join "More info". Copy that is deliberately shortened is changed in its owning capability file (navigation.md, pinned-catalog.md, varieties.md and others) in the same diff as its test, as criterion 5 already says. The new file holds the rule, not the copy of every screen.

### Validated flags

- `touches_ui`: **true**. Confirmed: icons on entries and actions, More info and the attention icon all change what the seller sees and taps.
- `touches_copy`: **true**. Confirmed: help lines, notices, empty states and some refusals are shortened, and "Más info" is new text.

### PRD

#### Job to be done

**When** I'm on my phone between orders or mid-chat with a customer and I need to find an entry, an action or what a notice is telling me, **I want** to recognise it at a glance by its icon and a short label, and read any help or notice as one short line, **so I can** get to it without reading blocks of text and get back to the customer.

- It refines the navigation job (navigation.md) and the "feels like a phone app" job (accounts.md). It adds no capability.
- The protagonist is the seller, in busy one-handed use on the phone.
- Setup screens follow the same rule. The computer is the same job on a bigger screen.

#### Success criteria

1. **Found at a glance.**
   - Every entry in Más, in Configuración's list of groups and in action rows, and every action, shows an icon next to its text. The only exceptions are the two closed lists below.
   - Navigation and menus always show icon + text. "No tab is an icon alone" stays.
2. **One rule, written once.** The design-system README and the product docs (`icon-and-text-rule.md`) state the same rule:
   - the two closed lists;
   - the length limits;
   - what may go behind More info and what never does.
3. **Short by default, nothing lost.**
   - Always-visible help lines, notices and empty states fit the limits.
   - Text that repeats itself is cut.
   - A needed longer explanation stays one tap away behind More info and is never deleted silently.
4. **One icon family; no state by icon or colour alone.**
   - Every icon uses stroke 1.6 at 22 px, is named in `NombreIcono` and works in both themes.
   - No emoji is used as an interface icon.
   - The word or shape that carries a state today keeps carrying it.
5. **Nothing else changes.** These all stay as they are:
   - actions, routes, the order of entries, confirmations, toast wording and tap budgets;
   - every rule about what a screen reveals;
   - every accessible name.

   Existing tests pass unchanged, except where copy is deliberately shortened. In that case the owning doc and the test change in the same diff.
6. **Looks as designed.** `scripts/design/compare.sh` runs before and after for every screen, phone and computer, both themes, and the result is judged by eye.

#### Behaviour

**1. Which form each control takes.** Both lists are closed. Adding to either one changes the rule doc, never a single screen.

- **Icon-only:**
  - delete ("Borrar…"), search ("Buscar"), close ("Cerrar") and edit ("Editar");
  - share ("Compartir"), only where it is not the screen's main action.

  Rules for these controls:
  - The accessible name is the exact current word, so "Cerrar, never Volver" still holds.
  - The target is at least 44 px.
  - Being on the list allows icon-only; it does not require it. The main action of a screen always carries its text: Compartir on the catalog screen keeps its word.
  - Back is not on the list. It stays "‹ <parent>".
  - More info's "i" is the one icon-only control outside this list. Its own term (section 5) defines it.
- **Text only:**
  - links (`enlace`), such as "Editar encabezado y pie" or "Ver todas";
  - the answer pair inside a confirmation, with its exact current words;
  - the submit of a form or sheet ("Guardá la venta", "Guardá el aviso", "Guardá el catálogo", "Registrar", "Guardar y entrar", "Entrá" and the rest);
  - choices among options (period tabs, Efectivo · Transferencia · Debiendo, Promoción · Consumo propio, an entry's origin, Claro · Oscuro, "Todo: $ X");
  - Deshacer inside "Venta guardada".
- **Icon + text: everything else.** For example:
  - "+ Nueva venta", "+ Entrada de stock", "Sacar del stock", "Mandar catálogo";
  - Cobrar (its accessible name stays "Cobrar a <name>");
  - Publicar, Probarlo, Buscar mi chat;
  - Archivar, Restaurar, "Invitá a un socio", Copiar and the main Compartir.

  A new action that fits neither list is icon + text. The icon is decorative and hidden from assistive technology, so the accessible name stays the word.

**2. Navigation, menus and rows.**
- The bar, the sidebar, Más and Configuración's list of groups always show icon + text.
- Entry rows and action rows get a leading icon.
- Record rows keep their layout and their state marks. These are customer, sale, variety, stock entry, withdrawal, expense, price entry and account rows. The marks are `MarcaEstado`, amber "te debe" and "A favor".
- Actions on record rows follow section 1.
- Within one menu (Más, Configuración's groups, the bar, the sidebar), no two entries share an icon.

**3. Icons.**
- One family: stroke 1.6, 22 px, no emoji.
- A specific metaphor beats a neutral one, for example a plug or a bot for Integraciones. No generic placeholder.
- The same meaning gets the same icon everywhere.
- A name joins `NombreIcono` only when a screen uses it. The 13 existing names stay.
- Each new icon is judged by eye.
- An icon next to a word is decorative.

**4. Length of always-visible text.**
- **Help line or notice:** one sentence of at most 80 characters.
- **Empty state:** a title of at most 40 characters, plus one line of at most 80, plus its action if it has one.
- **Inline refusals and the reasons of Probarlo and Buscar mi chat:**
  - they aim for 80 characters and stay on screen;
  - they keep the fix even when that takes more than 80;
  - they never go behind More info.
- **Consequences stay visible in short form.** Examples: the old pinned message staying as it is, the grams on hand in the archive confirmation, the grams that return to stock on delete.
- **Confirmation questions** may be shortened, but each keeps every fact it names today: date, amount, grams, reason.
- **Fixed security texts** (the brake message, the Entrar hint, the dead-link texts) keep their meaning. Shortening one never adds information, such as an attempt count or the reason a link is dead.

**5. More info (new term).**
- **Form:** an "i" from the family, in a 44 px target, at the end of the line it explains.
- **Behaviour:**
  - a tap expands the longer text in place under the line, and a second tap collapses it;
  - it is not a modal or a sheet;
  - it always opens closed, and whether it was opened is never remembered;
  - opening it is never an unsaved change and never triggers the leave confirmation.
- **Accessibility:** the accessible name is "Más info", with `aria-expanded`.
- **Content:** only the "how" or "why", never a consequence, a refusal's fix or a state.
- **When it appears:** never on a line whose explanation already fits in 80 characters.

**6. Notices.**
- One attention icon from the family, the same everywhere and decorative, sits next to its sentence on these notices:
  - Inicio's "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado";
  - "Desactualizado" with its reason;
  - "Arrancá por las variedades";
  - the after-import "Conectá el bot…";
  - "Sin conexión";
  - the server-error retry.
- The sentence stays the carrier of the state. Colour and wording do not change.
- These get no icon:
  - "Al día";
  - "Eso ya no está." and its list form;
  - toasts;
  - the first-run checklist, which keeps its boxes and "Hecho";
  - help lines and empty states.

**7. Swapping actions.**
- No action swaps its word by state today.
- Archivar and Restaurar are two actions, each with its own icon.
- The low-stock switch is a switch, not a button, and gets no icon.
- For any future swapping action, the word carries the state. A second icon is allowed only where a screen uses it.

**8. Where the rule is written.**
- The design-system README and `docs/product/icon-and-text-rule.md` state the rule the same way.
- CONTEXT.md gains three terms: More info, Icon and text rule, and Attention icon.
- `componentes.ts` gains the new `NombreIcono` names and a component type for More info.
- Shortened copy changes in its owning capability file in the same diff as its test.

**9. Screens and sequencing.**
- Every screen is brought in line, on phone and computer, in both themes.
- Check these first: Más, Configuración's groups (Integraciones above all), and the Variedades page and list.
- Work starts after #59, #47, #77 and #78 merge.

#### Metrics

None of these use telemetry.

- **Found at a glance (criterion 1).** A front test over every screen in `screens.json` checks that:
  - every entry row, action row and action outside the closed lists has an icon and its text;
  - every icon-only control has its exact current accessible name and a 44 px target;
  - the icon next to a word is hidden from assistive technology;
  - no menu repeats an icon.

  Target: 0 exceptions.
- **One rule (criterion 2).**
  - The README and `icon-and-text-rule.md` state the same two lists and limits.
  - Every `NombreIcono` name this task adds is used by a screen. Target: 0 unused new names.
- **Short by default (criterion 3).**
  - A test asserts the limits for help lines, notices and empty states, counting characters of the copy as shown.
  - Refusals over 80 characters are listed for review.
  - A More info test shows it opens closed, toggles `aria-expanded`, never triggers the leave confirmation and holds no consequence or fix.
  - A PR list shows every shortened or moved text and where its long form went. Target: 0 silent deletions.
- **Icon family and states (criterion 4).**
  - A guard checks stroke 1.6, 22 px and no emoji.
  - Front tests show every state keeps its word or shape in both themes.
  - The attention icon is present on the six listed notices and absent from the informational ones.
- **Nothing else changes (criterion 5).**
  - Existing suites pass. The only edited tests are those for shortened copy, listed in the PR next to their doc change.
  - Tap budgets and the redirect table are unchanged.
  - Security texts add no information.
- **Looks as designed (criterion 6).**
  - `compare.sh` runs before and after on every screen, phone and computer, both themes, judged by eye.
  - At 320 px with text at 130%, nothing is clipped and the bar still fits.
- **Dashboard:** none.

#### Out of scope

- An icon-only UI, an icon-only back, or an icon-only main action.
- Adding, removing or reordering actions or entries; changing routes, confirmations or toast wording.
- Icons on record rows.
- Optional icons on notices.
- Icons that no screen uses.
- Moving a consequence, a refusal's fix or a state behind More info.
- Remembering whether More info was opened.

### For the next role

For the Tech Lead:
- The new definition file `docs/product/icon-and-text-rule.md` holds the rule. Each screen's own copy stays in its capability file, and a deliberately shortened text changes that file and its test in the same diff, listed in the PR.
- **Accessible names must not move.** The icon next to a word is `aria-hidden`. Icon-only controls keep their exact current word as their name. A test that has to change only because of a name change means the implementation is wrong; it is not a reason to edit the test.
- More info, the attention icon and the new `NombreIcono` names are design-system additions under ADR-0029's contrast guard. Check whether the "i" and the attention icon's colours need rows there. Neither one carries a state.
- More info's open state is component state only. Nothing goes in browser storage (ADR-0026), and it is invisible to `useLeaveGuard`.
- **Sequencing:** wait for #59, #47, #77 and #78. Start the checks with Más, Configuración (Integraciones) and Variedades.
- The main gate is `compare.sh` before and after, phone and computer, both themes. It can't be automated further, so attach it to the PR.

## PM

### Verdict

`approved`: the PRD matches every decision from rounds 1–3 and serves the settled job. I closed the remaining small gaps myself (below).

### Adjustments

1. **Accessible names when an icon sits next to a word.** In icon + text, the icon is decorative and hidden from assistive technology, so the control's accessible name stays its word. Without this, adding an icon could change names that existing tests look controls up by. That would break criterion 5. This was missing; I added it to sections 1 and 3.
2. **More info's "i" is the one icon-only control outside the action list.** The closed icon-only list covers actions. More info is an affordance with its own term, its own fixed name "Más info" and its own icon in the family. I wrote this into section 1 so no screen reads it as a sixth entry on the list.
3. **The attention icon is one icon.** The same family icon goes on every listed notice ("same meaning, same icon"). It is decorative next to its sentence. I made this explicit in section 6 and added "Attention icon" to the glossary, so it isn't redefined screen by screen.
4. **The unused-name metric now covers new names only.** As written, it could fail on one of the 13 existing names, which the PRD itself keeps. The rule from the interview is "added only when a screen uses them", so the check applies to the names this task adds.
5. **The length check is measurable.** The hard 80 and 40 limits apply to help lines, notices and empty states, counted in characters of the Spanish copy as shown, `dd/mm` dates and figures substituted as written. Refusals and the reasons of Probarlo and Buscar mi chat aim for 80 but are listed for review rather than failed, because a fix that can't fit keeps the fix (round 2).
6. **Where the rule lives in the product docs.** The rule gets its own capability file, `icon-and-text-rule.md`, and two glossary terms, "Icon and text rule" and "Attention icon", join "More info". Copy that is deliberately shortened is changed in its owning capability file (navigation.md, pinned-catalog.md, varieties.md and others) in the same diff as its test, as criterion 5 already says. The new file holds the rule, not the copy of every screen.

### Validated flags

- `touches_ui`: **true**. Confirmed: icons on entries and actions, More info and the attention icon all change what the seller sees and taps.
- `touches_copy`: **true**. Confirmed: help lines, notices, empty states and some refusals are shortened, and "Más info" is new text.

### PRD

#### Job to be done

**When** I'm on my phone between orders or mid-chat with a customer and I need to find an entry, an action or what a notice is telling me, **I want** to recognise it at a glance by its icon and a short label, and read any help or notice as one short line, **so I can** get to it without reading blocks of text and get back to the customer.

- It refines the navigation job (navigation.md) and the "feels like a phone app" job (accounts.md). It adds no capability.
- The protagonist is the seller, in busy one-handed use on the phone.
- Setup screens follow the same rule. The computer is the same job on a bigger screen.

#### Success criteria

1. **Found at a glance.**
   - Every entry in Más, in Configuración's list of groups and in action rows, and every action, shows an icon next to its text. The only exceptions are the two closed lists below.
   - Navigation and menus always show icon + text. "No tab is an icon alone" stays.
2. **One rule, written once.** The design-system README and the product docs (`icon-and-text-rule.md`) state the same rule:
   - the two closed lists;
   - the length limits;
   - what may go behind More info and what never does.
3. **Short by default, nothing lost.**
   - Always-visible help lines, notices and empty states fit the limits.
   - Text that repeats itself is cut.
   - A needed longer explanation stays one tap away behind More info and is never deleted silently.
4. **One icon family; no state by icon or colour alone.**
   - Every icon uses stroke 1.6 at 22 px, is named in `NombreIcono` and works in both themes.
   - No emoji is used as an interface icon.
   - The word or shape that carries a state today keeps carrying it.
5. **Nothing else changes.** These all stay as they are:
   - actions, routes, the order of entries, confirmations, toast wording and tap budgets;
   - every rule about what a screen reveals;
   - every accessible name.

   Existing tests pass unchanged, except where copy is deliberately shortened. In that case the owning doc and the test change in the same diff.
6. **Looks as designed.** `scripts/design/compare.sh` runs before and after for every screen, phone and computer, both themes, and the result is judged by eye.

#### Behaviour

**1. Which form each control takes.** Both lists are closed. Adding to either one changes the rule doc, never a single screen.

- **Icon-only:**
  - delete ("Borrar…"), search ("Buscar"), close ("Cerrar") and edit ("Editar");
  - share ("Compartir"), only where it is not the screen's main action.

  Rules for these controls:
  - The accessible name is the exact current word, so "Cerrar, never Volver" still holds.
  - The target is at least 44 px.
  - Being on the list allows icon-only; it does not require it. The main action of a screen always carries its text: Compartir on the catalog screen keeps its word.
  - Back is not on the list. It stays "‹ <parent>".
  - More info's "i" is the one icon-only control outside this list. Its own term (section 5) defines it.
- **Text only:**
  - links (`enlace`), such as "Editar encabezado y pie" or "Ver todas";
  - the answer pair inside a confirmation, with its exact current words;
  - the submit of a form or sheet ("Guardá la venta", "Guardá el aviso", "Guardá el catálogo", "Registrar", "Guardar y entrar", "Entrá" and the rest);
  - choices among options (period tabs, Efectivo · Transferencia · Debiendo, Promoción · Consumo propio, an entry's origin, Claro · Oscuro, "Todo: $ X");
  - Deshacer inside "Venta guardada".
- **Icon + text: everything else.** For example:
  - "+ Nueva venta", "+ Entrada de stock", "Sacar del stock", "Mandar catálogo";
  - Cobrar (its accessible name stays "Cobrar a <name>");
  - Publicar, Probarlo, Buscar mi chat;
  - Archivar, Restaurar, "Invitá a un socio", Copiar and the main Compartir.

  A new action that fits neither list is icon + text. The icon is decorative and hidden from assistive technology, so the accessible name stays the word.

**2. Navigation, menus and rows.**
- The bar, the sidebar, Más and Configuración's list of groups always show icon + text.
- Entry rows and action rows get a leading icon.
- Record rows keep their layout and their state marks. These are customer, sale, variety, stock entry, withdrawal, expense, price entry and account rows. The marks are `MarcaEstado`, amber "te debe" and "A favor".
- Actions on record rows follow section 1.
- Within one menu (Más, Configuración's groups, the bar, the sidebar), no two entries share an icon.

**3. Icons.**
- One family: stroke 1.6, 22 px, no emoji.
- A specific metaphor beats a neutral one, for example a plug or a bot for Integraciones. No generic placeholder.
- The same meaning gets the same icon everywhere.
- A name joins `NombreIcono` only when a screen uses it. The 13 existing names stay.
- Each new icon is judged by eye.
- An icon next to a word is decorative.

**4. Length of always-visible text.**
- **Help line or notice:** one sentence of at most 80 characters.
- **Empty state:** a title of at most 40 characters, plus one line of at most 80, plus its action if it has one.
- **Inline refusals and the reasons of Probarlo and Buscar mi chat:**
  - they aim for 80 characters and stay on screen;
  - they keep the fix even when that takes more than 80;
  - they never go behind More info.
- **Consequences stay visible in short form.** Examples: the old pinned message staying as it is, the grams on hand in the archive confirmation, the grams that return to stock on delete.
- **Confirmation questions** may be shortened, but each keeps every fact it names today: date, amount, grams, reason.
- **Fixed security texts** (the brake message, the Entrar hint, the dead-link texts) keep their meaning. Shortening one never adds information, such as an attempt count or the reason a link is dead.

**5. More info (new term).**
- **Form:** an "i" from the family, in a 44 px target, at the end of the line it explains.
- **Behaviour:**
  - a tap expands the longer text in place under the line, and a second tap collapses it;
  - it is not a modal or a sheet;
  - it always opens closed, and whether it was opened is never remembered;
  - opening it is never an unsaved change and never triggers the leave confirmation.
- **Accessibility:** the accessible name is "Más info", with `aria-expanded`.
- **Content:** only the "how" or "why", never a consequence, a refusal's fix or a state.
- **When it appears:** never on a line whose explanation already fits in 80 characters.

**6. Notices.**
- One attention icon from the family, the same everywhere and decorative, sits next to its sentence on these notices:
  - Inicio's "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado";
  - "Desactualizado" with its reason;
  - "Arrancá por las variedades";
  - the after-import "Conectá el bot…";
  - "Sin conexión";
  - the server-error retry.
- The sentence stays the carrier of the state. Colour and wording do not change.
- These get no icon:
  - "Al día";
  - "Eso ya no está." and its list form;
  - toasts;
  - the first-run checklist, which keeps its boxes and "Hecho";
  - help lines and empty states.

**7. Swapping actions.**
- No action swaps its word by state today.
- Archivar and Restaurar are two actions, each with its own icon.
- The low-stock switch is a switch, not a button, and gets no icon.
- For any future swapping action, the word carries the state. A second icon is allowed only where a screen uses it.

**8. Where the rule is written.**
- The design-system README and `docs/product/icon-and-text-rule.md` state the rule the same way.
- CONTEXT.md gains three terms: More info, Icon and text rule, and Attention icon.
- `componentes.ts` gains the new `NombreIcono` names and a component type for More info.
- Shortened copy changes in its owning capability file in the same diff as its test.

**9. Screens and sequencing.**
- Every screen is brought in line, on phone and computer, in both themes.
- Check these first: Más, Configuración's groups (Integraciones above all), and the Variedades page and list.
- Work starts after #59, #47, #77 and #78 merge.
