### Clarifications (round 3)

### Answers to Tech Lead (round 3)

**Q1: "Cerrar" turned into an icon-only control, and the tests that find it by visible text.**

Yes, that edit counts as the criterion 5 exception and must be listed in the PR. It works under these conditions:

- **One form everywhere.** Close is on the icon-only list, and the design uses it that way: every form and sheet closes with the same icon-only control. That is the `cerrar` icon in a 44 px target, with the accessible name "Cerrar".
  - No form shows the word while another shows the icon. Same meaning, same icon.
  - "Cerrar, never Volver" (navigation.md) still holds, now through the accessible name.
- **The test edit is the narrowest possible.** A test that found the control by its visible text ("Cerrar") now finds it by role and accessible name ("button", name "Cerrar"). Nothing else in that test changes:
  - no assertion is dropped or loosened;
  - the "never Volver" check stays, against the accessible name.
- **Each edit is listed in the PR**, with the screen and the control. A test that has to change in any other way means the implementation is wrong. It does not mean the test should be edited.
- **The same applies to the other icon-only actions** that show a word today: Editar, Borrar…, Buscar, and Compartir where it is not the main action. The accessible name stays the exact current word.

I have written this into `icon-and-text-rule.md` as a second, named exception in criterion 5. Following the Human's Decision 1, I also added that every icon is specified by its `NombreIcono` name, and that names `Icono.tsx` already draws are reused before any new name is added.

### Not reopened

Human Decision 1 is settled and applied:

- "Probarlo" keeps its name.
- "Buscar mi chat" is icon + text.
- Archivar and Restaurar are back, each with its own icon.
- Every control's icon is named.
- `NombreIcono` is reconciled with `Icono.tsx`.
- "Invitá a un socio" and "Registrar" stay as they are.
- The Compartir under a fresh invitation link may be icon-only, because it is not the main action of Usuarios.

## UX Designer

### Flow

This task changes presentation and copy only. Every job keeps its actions, routes, entry order, confirmations, toast wording and tap budgets. What changes in each flow is how a step is recognised (an icon next to its word, a shorter line, a "Más info" for the longer explanation) and not the number of steps. Touch counts below are the current budgets and stay unchanged. The one new touch is optional: opening "Más info", which is never a required step.

I did not open the prototype screens (`Inicio.dc.html` and the rest of `pantallas/`). The screen names below come from the PRD and the product docs. Where a flow depends on a drawn screen I could not check, the step is stated as unverified.

### Flow: find an entry or action at a glance (the main job)

- **Starts at:** any tab root on the phone, or the sidebar on a computer, mid-chat with a customer.
- **Steps:**
  1. Look at Más, Configuración's list of groups or an action row (for example Variedades' "+ Entrada de stock", "Sacar del stock", "Mandar catálogo"). Each entry shows an icon and its word. Cost: 0 touches, a glance.
  2. Tap the entry. Cost: 1 touch, as today.
- **Screens:** Más, Configuración (list of groups), Variedades, a variety's page, Clientes, Inicio, the catalog screen, Integraciones.
- **What changes:** entry rows and action rows gain a leading icon. Within one menu (the bar, the sidebar, Más, Configuración's groups) no two entries share an icon. The bar and sidebar already show icon + text and stay as they are. Back stays "‹ <parent>".
- **Icon-only controls** (their accessible name stays the exact current word):
  - delete ("Borrar…"), search ("Buscar"), close ("Cerrar"), edit ("Editar");
  - share ("Compartir"), only where it is not the screen's main action.

  Each target is at least 44 px. Being on the list allows icon-only but does not require it.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery:**
  - An icon alone is unclear. Only the closed list is icon-only, and every other control keeps its word, so the seller reads the word.
  - An icon-only control is mistapped. The 44 px target and the existing confirmations still apply, for example "Antes de borrar" on delete.
- **Ends at:** the same screen the entry opened before.
- **Touch count:** unchanged from the budgets (sale 1 tap; Registrar cobro ≤2; catalog screen ≤2; stock entry ≤2; stock withdrawal ≤2; expense form ≤2; Configuración ≤2).
- **Not verified:** which new icons the prototype draws. UX Lead should check that Más, Configuración and Integraciones each have a distinct icon per entry.

### Flow: record a sale (Nueva venta)

- **Unchanged in steps, order and count.** Steps stay: "+ Nueva venta", customer, lines, shipping, note, payment, "Guardá la venta". Touch count is the same, under 60 s.
- **Presentation only:**
  - "+ Nueva venta" is icon + text.
  - "Agregar otra variedad" and the payment options keep their words.
  - The submit "Guardá la venta" and the answers "No, seguí cargando / Sí, descartala" stay text only.
  - Efectivo · Transferencia · Debiendo are choices, not actions, so they stay text only.
  - "Cerrar" may be icon-only, with its accessible name "Cerrar".
  - The hint "Para dejar debiendo elegí un cliente" is a help line and must fit 80 characters.
  - "Deshacer" inside "Venta guardada" stays text.
- **Recovery paths:** unchanged. Refusals show inline and keep what was typed. They keep their fix even when longer than 80 characters.
- **Ends at:** the sale's detail with "Venta guardada".

### Flow: record a payment (Cobrar)

- **Unchanged in steps and count.** Starts at Clientes or Inicio. Tap "Cobrar" (1 touch, ≤2 from any tab root), choose the amount ("Todo: $ X" is text), choose Efectivo or Transferencia, then "Registrar".
- **Presentation:** "Cobrar" is icon + text and its accessible name stays "Cobrar a <name>". The debtor row keeps its amber amount and "desde dd/mm".
- **Recovery:** refusals stay inline with what was typed. "Eso ya no está." keeps its wording and gets no icon.
- **Ends at:** the screen it opened from, with "Cobro guardado · queda debiendo $ X".

### Flow: record stock and withdrawals

- **Unchanged in steps and count.** Starts at Variedades, a variety's page or Más ("Sacar del stock"). ≤2 taps.
- **Presentation:**
  - "+ Entrada de stock", "+ Entrada", "Sacar del stock" and "Nuevo precio" are icon + text.
  - Origin (Compra · Cosecha · Stock inicial · Ajuste) and reason (Promoción · Consumo propio) are choices and stay text only.
  - "Obligatorio cuando es una compra" and the cost-per-gram line are help lines and must fit 80 characters.
- **Archivar variedad / Eliminar variedad / Restaurar:** each is icon + text with its own icon. The archive confirmation keeps the grams on hand, only shortened. The check that it still names the grams is for the owning docs and the shortening list.
- **Aviso de stock bajo:** the on/off switch stays a switch with no icon.
- **Recovery:** unchanged.

### Flow: publish the pinned catalog (Integraciones and catalog screen)

- **Unchanged in steps and count.** Configuración → Integraciones (token, chats, "Buscar mi chat", "Probarlo"), then the link to the catalog screen, then "Publicar".
- **Presentation:**
  - Probarlo, Buscar mi chat, Publicar, Copiar, "Mandar catálogo" and the main Compartir are icon + text.
  - Integraciones gets a specific icon (a plug or a bot).
  - The own-chat help line is shortened to fit 80 characters. Its longer how-to (for example where the number comes from) may move behind "Más info". The consequence line "El mensaje fijado anterior queda como está; desfijalo vos en Telegram." stays visible in short form.
- **Recovery paths:**
  - The reasons of Probarlo and Buscar mi chat stay inline and keep their fix, even if longer than 80. They never move behind "Más info".
  - "Desactualizado" and its reason get the attention icon, and the sentence still carries the state.
  - "Al día" gets no icon.
- **Ends at:** the catalog screen showing "Al día" or "Desactualizado".

### Flow: read a notice or a help line

- **Starts at:** Inicio, a form, an empty state or an error state.
- **Steps:**
  1. Read one short line (at most 80 characters; empty-state title at most 40 plus one line of at most 80). 0 touches.
  2. Optional: tap the "i" at the end of the line (1 touch, 44 px target, accessible name "Más info", `aria-expanded`). The longer text opens in place under the line. A second tap closes it.
- **Recovery:** More info always opens closed, is not remembered, is never an unsaved change and never triggers the leave confirmation. It holds only the how or why, never a consequence, a refusal's fix or a state.
- **Attention icon:** on exactly six notices, always next to the sentence and never alone:
  - "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado";
  - "Desactualizado" with its reason;
  - "Arrancá por las variedades";
  - the after-import "Conectá el bot…";
  - "Sin conexión";
  - the server-error retry.

  It is not on "Al día", "Eso ya no está.", toasts, the first-run checklist, help lines or empty states.
- **Ends at:** the same screen. Nothing is stored.

### Flow: getting in (Entrar, invitation, reset link)

- **Unchanged in steps and count.** Entrar: Email, Contraseña, "Entrá". Invitation: name, email, password, then "Guardar y entrar". Reset: "Contraseña nueva", then "Guardar y entrar".
- **Presentation:** the submit buttons stay text only. "Mostrar/Ocultar" on a password field is not on either closed list, so it follows the default: icon + text. This needs UX Lead's confirmation that the drawn reveal control fits the rule.
- **Security texts** (the brake message, the Entrar hint, the dead-link texts) keep their meaning. Shortening never adds an attempt count or a reason a link is dead.
- **Recovery:** unchanged. The dead-link screen keeps its single button to Entrar.

### Flow: other jobs (flow unchanged)

The flow of each of these is unchanged: no step, screen, recovery or touch count differs. Only the icon-and-text and copy treatment above applies to their entries and actions.

- **Expenses** ("+ Cargar gasto", Gastos, categories in Configuración): flow unchanged. The expense form's submit stays text only. The category link in Gastos stays a text link.
- **Analysis** (Más → Análisis): read only, flow unchanged.
- **Customers** (find, register, edit, "Nueva venta a <name>"): flow unchanged.
- **Configuración groups** (Negocio, Usuarios, Apariencia, Tu cuenta): flow unchanged.
  - "Invitá a un socio" is icon + text.
  - Claro · Oscuro stay text-only choices marked by word or shape.
  - "Cambiar contraseña" and "Cerrar sesión" are icon + text.
- **Spreadsheet import, business removal and password reset by the operator:** not part of the app screens. They have no flow here.

### Risks for UX Lead

- Accessible names must not change. If an existing test needs editing only because of a name change, the implementation is wrong. Only deliberately shortened copy may change a test, in the same diff as its doc.
- The icon next to a word is `aria-hidden`.
- I have no prototype evidence for the new icons or for whether every screen fits the 80/40 limits. Both are by-eye checks with `scripts/design/compare.sh`, phone and computer, both themes, including 320 px at 130% text.

## UX Lead

_The system denied 1 write attempt: this role writes nothing; reproduce by reading the code, the task documents and the existing tests, or by running the declared checks. A denial here is not a syntax problem._

### Walk

The prototype (`docs/product/design-system/pantallas`) draws the app **as it is today**. It has no new icons, no "Más info" and no attention icon. So every step that changes presentation is judged on the flow's text alone ("sin prototipo"). What the prototype does let me check is that every step still exists, connects and recovers the same way. It does, because the flow changes no step.

**Find an entry or action at a glance**
- `Mas.dc.html`:
  - The four entries draw only a trailing chevron. The leading icons are sin prototipo.
  - The "Sacar del stock" entry the flow starts from is not drawn. The prototype is behind navigation.md (#59), so that step is judged on the text alone.
  - "Nueva venta" already shows icon + text.
- `Configuracion.dc.html`:
  - Back "‹ Más" stays text.
  - There are seven groups (Negocio, Usuarios, Integraciones, Mensaje del catálogo, Categorías de gastos, Apariencia, Tu cuenta). The flow names only four. The one-icon-per-entry check must cover all seven.
- Bar: icon + text on every tab, as the flow says.

**Record a sale / Cobrar / Stock**
- Steps and recoveries are unchanged.
- `Estados.dc.html`: the "Antes de borrar" confirmation keeps date, amount and grams ("del 02/10 de $ 56.000 y vuelven 4 g"), and its answer pair is text. This matches the flow.
- "Venta guardada · Deshacer" is text, as the flow says.

**Publish the pinned catalog**
- `ConfiguracionIntegraciones.dc.html` draws "Enviarme una prueba", no "Buscar mi chat", and the old own-chat help line. The flow follows pinned-catalog.md instead, so Probarlo, Buscar mi chat and the "Más info" split of the help line are sin prototipo.
- The consequence banner ("El mensaje fijado anterior queda como está…") is visible, as the flow keeps it.
- "Cerrar" is text, a candidate for icon-only.
- `Catalogo.dc.html`:
  - Compartir is the main action and keeps its word.
  - Copiar and Publicar are present.
  - "Al día" and "Desactualizado" each carry their word.
  - Open question: the disabled-Publicar reason also begins "Conectá el bot…", and the flow does not say whether it gets the attention icon.

**Read a notice or help line**
- "Arrancá por las variedades" is drawn as an empty state (title, line, action). The flow puts it on the attention-icon list but also says empty states get no icon, so the two rules conflict on this drawn screen. Its line is about 82 characters and must also be shortened.
- "Sin conexión" is drawn as "Te quedaste sin internet" plus a line. The flow should say which sentence carries the state.
- The server-error retry and More info itself are sin prototipo.

**Getting in**
- `Entrar.dc.html`:
  - "Entrá" is text.
  - No reveal control is drawn.
  - The hint already shows an attempt count ("Te quedan 3 intentos…"), so the baseline for the "add no information" check should be the doc, not this drawing.
- `ConfiguracionCuenta.dc.html` answers UX Designer's question about Mostrar/Ocultar:
  - The reveal control is drawn as a text link "Mostrar" inside the field.
  - It swaps its word by state, which contradicts the PRD's "no action swaps its word today".
  - The flow should say whether it uses one icon or eye / eye-off.

**Other jobs:** unchanged in the prototype, and no finding.

### Verdict

`approved`. No step is a dead end and no recovery is lost. The open points are observations for UX Designer and UI Lead to settle in the screens.

## UI Designer (v1)

### Screens

Rule applied everywhere (`icon-and-text-rule.md`): icon-only (44px target, accessible name = the exact current word) for delete "Borrar…", search "Buscar", close "Cerrar", edit "Editar", secondary "Compartir", plus the "i" More info. Text-only for links, confirmation answer pairs, form and sheet submits, choices among options (period tabs, Efectivo·Transferencia·Debiendo, Promoción·Consumo propio, origin, Claro·Oscuro, "Todo: $ X") and Deshacer. Icon + text for everything else. The icon is decorative (`aria-hidden`) and the accessible name stays the word. Icons are stroke 1.6, 22px, no emoji, drawn from `NombreIcono`. Both themes apply, with contrast per ADR-0029. Each new name is added to `NombreIcono` only on the screen that uses it, and UI Lead judges it by eye. Check with `scripts/design/compare.sh`. At 320px and 130% text nothing clips.

### Más (`Mas.dc.html`)
- **Built from:** the existing Más list rows (leading icon, label, trailing chevron).
- **Change:** every entry shows icon + text, with no two entries sharing an icon. Reuse the existing tab icons where the entry is also a tab. Add the "Sacar del stock" entry, which is not drawn today. It is icon + text and uses a new name (an outgoing-box icon, distinct from the stock-entry icon).
- **States:** filled only. It has no empty or loading state, and "Sin conexión" shows the shared notice from the Estados section.
- **Gap for UI Lead:** the new icon names for the entries that have none today. Judge them by eye.

### Configuración (`Configuracion.dc.html`)
- **Built from:** the grouped list with seven groups: Negocio, Usuarios, Integraciones, Mensaje del catálogo, Categorías de gastos, Apariencia, Tu cuenta.
- **Change:** each group row gets its own leading icon, distinct within the menu (new names where none exist, for example plug for Integraciones). Chevrons stay. Back stays "‹ <parent>".
- **States:** filled. If the user has no permission, a group is hidden (as today).

### Configuración · subscreens (Negocio, Usuarios, Mensaje del catálogo, Categorías de gastos, Apariencia)
- **Built from:** the existing form, list and row components of each `Configuracion*.dc.html`.
- **Change:**
  - Submits stay text-only, with no icon.
  - Delete "Borrar…" becomes icon-only (trash, accessible name with the exact word). "Editar" is icon-only.
  - Claro·Oscuro and other option choices stay text-only.
  - "Invitá a un socio" is icon + text (the existing personaMas).
  - Help lines are one sentence of at most 80 characters. Anything longer moves behind More info only if it is the how or why.
- **States:** empty (title of at most 40, one line of at most 80, action), loading, error (inline refusal that keeps the fix), filled.

### Configuración · Integraciones (`ConfiguracionIntegraciones.dc.html`)
- **Built from:** the existing integration card, help lines and banner.
- **Change:**
  - "Enviarme una prueba" becomes "Probarlo" (icon + text), and "Buscar mi chat" is added (icon + text). The reason line of each is at most 80 characters and stays visible, not behind More info.
  - The old own-chat help line is replaced by the shorter PRD line.
  - The consequence banner stays visible in short form.
  - The "Conectá el bot…" notice after import carries the attention icon.
  - How and why details go behind More info.
- **States:** not connected, connected, testing (Probarlo loading), chat found, chat not found (error with the fix), "Sin conexión".
- **Gap for UI Lead:** "Buscar mi chat" and "Probarlo" need new icon names (for example search-chat and send). The magnifier `search` is reserved for icon-only "Buscar", so it is not reused.

### Configuración · Tu cuenta (`ConfiguracionCuenta.dc.html`)
- **Built from:** the existing field with the "Mostrar" link inside it.
- **Change:** the reveal control swaps its word between Mostrar and Ocultar. The rule says links are text-only, so it stays a text link and gets no icon.
- **Gap for UI Lead:** if the PRD wants an icon + text reveal, it needs eye and eye-off names, which do not exist. The default here is text-only, which is the existing design.
- **States:** filled, password hidden, password shown, validation error, saved.

### Entrar, invitation and reset (`Entrar.dc.html` and siblings)
- **Built from:** the existing form screens.
- **Change:**
  - The hint is fixed and shows no attempt count (the doc wins over the prototype).
  - Submits are text-only. "Mostrar" is as above. Links stay text-only.
  - Security texts add no information.
- **States:** default, loading, error (the same text for any failure), locked or throttled, success.

### Variedades, list and page
- **Built from:** the existing list rows, search and page form.
- **Change:**
  - Search is icon-only ("Buscar"). Edit and delete are icon-only.
  - "+ Nueva variedad" and "Archivar" / "Restaurar" are icon + text.
  - Record rows get no icons and keep `MarcaEstado`.
  - The low-stock switch stays a switch with no icon.
  - "Arrancá por las variedades" (see Estados) is shortened to at most 80 characters.
- **States:** empty, loading, filled, archived, error.

### Nueva venta, Registrar cobro, Clientes and Inicio debtor rows
- **Built from:** the existing sale form, payment sheet and customer rows.
- **Change:**
  - "+ Nueva venta" is icon + text.
  - Efectivo·Transferencia·Debiendo and "Todo: $ X" are text-only.
  - "Cobrar" is icon + text (the existing cobro icon).
  - Sheet submits are text-only.
  - Debtor rows keep amber "te debe" and "A favor", with no row icons.
  - "Venta guardada · Deshacer": Deshacer is a text-only link and the toast has no attention icon.
- **States:** empty, filled, saving, error, saved toast.

### Stock (entry and withdrawal)
- **Built from:** the existing stock forms and movement rows.
- **Change:**
  - "+ Entrada de stock" and "Sacar del stock" are icon + text, with distinct icons.
  - The origin choice and Promoción·Consumo propio are text-only.
  - Consequences stay visible in short form.
- **States:** empty, filled, error (for example not enough stock, with the fix), saved.

### Catálogo (`Catalogo.dc.html`)
- **Built from:** the existing catalog screen with the Al día / Desactualizado banner.
- **Change:**
  - "Compartir" is icon-only when secondary and icon + text when it is the screen's main action.
  - "Copiar", "Publicar", "Mandar catálogo" and "Probarlo" are icon + text.
  - "Desactualizado" and its reason carry the attention icon. "Al día" has none.
  - The disabled-Publicar reason ("Conectá el bot…") follows the sentence rule and takes the attention icon, because it is the same kind of notice. UI Lead may flag this if it reads as a help line.
- **States:** al día, desactualizado, Publicar disabled (with its reason), publishing, published, error, "Sin conexión".

### Inicio (`Inicio.dc.html`, entry screen)
- **Built from:** the existing home with the period tabs and summary cards.
- **Change:**
  - Period tabs are text-only.
  - "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" carries the attention icon.
  - Debtor rows are as above.
  - The first-run checklist has no attention icon.
- **States:** first run (checklist), empty, loading, filled, notice shown, "Sin conexión".

### Navigation bar and sidebar
- **Built from:** the five existing tab icons.
- **Change:** entries always show icon + text, with no two sharing an icon. Back stays "‹ <parent>".
- **States:** default, active, and the narrow layout at 320px and 130% text without clipping.

### Estados (`Estados.dc.html`: notices, empty states, errors, More info)
- **Attention notices.** One icon, the same everywhere and decorative, next to the sentence. It appears only on the six notices: Inicio's desactualizado, "Desactualizado" with its reason, "Arrancá por las variedades", the after-import "Conectá el bot…", "Sin conexión" and the server-error retry. It does not appear on "Al día", "Eso ya no está.", toasts, the first-run checklist, help lines or empty states.
- **"Arrancá por las variedades".** It is both an empty state and a listed notice. Decision: it is drawn as a notice with the attention icon, shortened to at most 80 characters, with its action. UI Lead should confirm this against the "empty state has no icon" rule.
- **"Sin conexión".** The notice sentence carries the state, with the attention icon and no extra banner.
- **Server error.** Composed from the existing notice plus a text retry. It is not drawn in the prototype, so UI Lead should check it.
- **"Eso ya no está."** No icon.
- **"Antes de borrar".** The answer pair is text-only.
- **More info.**
  - An "i" icon in a 44px target at the end of the line, with accessible name "Más info" and `aria-expanded`.
  - It expands in place, always opens closed, is never remembered and never triggers the leave guard.
  - It holds only the how or why. Inline refusals, the reasons of Probarlo and Buscar mi chat, and consequences never move behind it.
- **Gaps for UI Lead:**
  - More info needs a component entry in `componentes.ts` (the "i" name and the expandable area), because the prototype does not draw it.
  - The server-error retry is also undrawn.
  - All new icon names are to be judged by eye.

## UI Designer (v2)

### Screens

Rule applied everywhere: icon-only (44px target, accessible name = the exact current word, icon `aria-hidden`) only for delete ("Borrar…"), search ("Buscar"), close ("Cerrar"), edit ("Editar"), share ("Compartir", when not the main action) and More info. Text-only for links, confirmation answer pairs, form/sheet submits, option choices (period tabs, Efectivo·Transferencia·Debiendo, Promoción·Consumo propio, origin, Claro·Oscuro, "Todo: $ X") and Deshacer. Everything else is icon + text. Icons: stroke 1.6, 22px, no emoji, from `NombreIcono`. Contrast per ADR-0029 in both themes; checked with compare.sh and at 320px with 130% text. Every screen below keeps its computer layout (same content, same rule) as the product draws it.

**New icons (all new `NombreIcono` names, each added only because a screen below uses it; UI Lead to judge by eye).** Existing 13 are reused wherever they fit.
- `lapiz` (pencil): Editar.
- `enchufe` (plug): Integraciones.
- `avion` (paper plane): Probarlo.
- `arroba` (at-sign in a speech bubble): Buscar mi chat.
- `salida` (arrow out of a box): Sacar del stock.
- `recibo` (receipt): Gastos and Cargar gasto (Cargar gasto uses the existing `personaMas`-style "+" form: `recibo` with a plus).
- `grafico` (bars): Análisis.
- `engranaje` (cog): Configuración.
- `archivo` (archive box): Archivar.
- `restaurar` (counter-clockwise arrow): Restaurar.
- `info` ("i" in a circle): More info.
- `atencion` (circle with exclamation): the single attention icon.
- `compartir` (share nodes): Compartir.
- `candado` (padlock): Cambiar contraseña.
- `salirSesion` (arrow leaving a door): Cerrar sesión.
- `cerrar` (x): Cerrar.
- `mas` (plus): "+ Cargar…" actions.

**More info component (gap, for UI Lead).** The product has no "i" disclosure. It is built from the existing icon button (44px) plus the existing help-line text style: "i" at the end of the line, accessible name "Más info", `aria-expanded`, opens closed every time, not remembered, no leave-guard, holds only how/why. The disclosed text uses the existing help-line style and wraps at 320px. Flagged as a gap: no drawn state for it.

**Attention icon.** One, decorative, only on six notices: Inicio "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado"; "Desactualizado" + reason; "Arrancá por las variedades"; after-import "Conectá el bot…" (on Inicio); "Sin conexión"; server-error retry. Never on "Al día", "Eso ya no está.", toasts, the first-run checklist, help lines, empty states or the disabled-Publicar reason.

#### Inicio (Inicio / InicioCompu)
- Built from Inicio as is. States: filled, empty/new (see Inicio primera vez), loading (skeleton as drawn), error (server-error), offline, desactualizado.
- Change: the notice "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" gets `atencion` + text; "Al día" stays text only. Action buttons are icon + text (Publicar with its existing icon). Reason and consequence lines stay visible, ≤80 chars. Computer layout: same change in InicioCompu.

#### Inicio primera vez
- Built from InicioPrimeraVez. States: first-run checklist (no attention icon), "Arrancá por las variedades" (empty-state structure: title ≤40 + one line ≤80, with `atencion`), after-import.
- After-import: the notice "Conectá el bot…" lives here on Inicio, not in Integraciones; `atencion` + text, ≤80 chars, with its action as icon + text ("Conectar" with `enchufe`).
- Checklist rows stay icon + text, no attention icon.

#### Más (Mas)
- Built from Mas as is. Entries in navigation order: + Cargar gasto, Sacar del stock, Gastos, Análisis, Configuración. Each is icon + text with a distinct icon (`mas`/`recibo`, `salida`, `recibo`, `grafico`, `engranaje`); Cargar gasto uses `mas` and Gastos `recibo` so no two entries share an icon. Chevron stays at the end. States: filled only. Computer layout: sidebar entries get the same icons + text.

#### Catálogo / Variedades (Catalogo, two-pane on computer)
- Built from Catalogo. States: filled, empty, loading, error, no results.
- Changes: search is icon-only `search` named "Buscar"; Editar is icon-only `lapiz`; "Borrar…" icon-only `trash`; Archivar / Restaurar icon + text; period/option tabs stay text. Empty state: title ≤40 + one line ≤80, no attention icon. Computer: same in the list pane and detail pane.

#### Nuevo precio / Todas las entradas / Ver todas
- Built from the existing sheet/list screens. Submit ("Guardar") text only; "Cerrar" icon-only `cerrar`; "Ver todas" is a link, text only. States: empty, filled, validation error (refusal keeps its fix and stays visible).

#### Clientes y ficha de cliente
- Built from Clientes and the ficha. Share "Compartir" icon-only only when it is not the main action (otherwise icon + text). Editar `lapiz` icon-only; Borrar icon-only. Debiendo/Efectivo/Transferencia stay text-only choices. States: empty, filled, loading, error. Computer: two-pane, same rule.

#### Ventas y Venta detalle (Ventas / VentasCompu / Venta detalle)
- Built from the existing screens. Period tabs text-only; "Todo: $ X" text-only; Borrar… icon-only; Compartir icon-only unless it is the main action; Deshacer text-only in the toast (no attention icon on toasts). States: empty, filled, loading, error. Consequence lines stay visible.

#### Retiro detalle / Sacar del stock
- Built from the existing detail and form. "Sacar del stock" icon + text with `salida`; origin and Promoción·Consumo propio are text-only choices; submit text-only; Borrar… icon-only. States: filled, validation error with fix visible, confirmation (answer pair text-only).

#### Gastos y Nuevo gasto
- Built from the list and form screens. Gastos list rows: icon + text actions where present; "Borrar…" icon-only; submit text-only; Efectivo·Transferencia·Debiendo text-only. States: empty (title ≤40 + one line ≤80), filled, loading, error.

#### Análisis (Analisis / AnalisisCompu)
- Built from Analisis. Period tabs text-only; any ayuda line ≤80 chars with its how/why behind More info; chart legend unchanged. States: empty, filled, loading, error. Computer: same.

#### Configuración (Configuracion*)
- Built from Configuracion, Integraciones, Cuenta. Rows are icon + text with distinct icons (`engranaje`, `enchufe`, account). Claro·Oscuro is text-only. Help lines ≤80 chars; longer how-to goes behind More info (e.g. own-chat line is short, how-to behind "i"). Security texts get no extra information.
- Integraciones: Probarlo `avion` + text; Buscar mi chat `arroba` + text; their reasons keep the fix and stay visible. No "Conectá el bot…" notice here. States: not connected, connected, testing, error (reason + fix visible).
- Cuenta: "Cambiar contraseña" `candado` + text; "Cerrar sesión" `salirSesion` + text; "Mostrar/Ocultar" on the reveal is a text link.

#### Entrar / Negocio nuevo
- Built from Entrar and the new-business form. Submits text-only; links text-only; password reveal as a text link; refusals keep their fix and stay visible. States: default, validation error, loading.

#### Estados (empty / loading / error / offline)
- Built from Estados. "Sin conexión" and the server-error retry get `atencion` + text; the retry action is icon + text (`restaurar` + "Reintentar"). Empty states have no attention icon.

**Gaps for UI Lead to flag.** (1) The server-error retry has no drawn screen; composed from Estados error block + the new `atencion` and `restaurar`. (2) More info "i" has no drawn state. (3) All new icon names above do not exist in `NombreIcono`; they are added only with the screens that use them. (4) Where a screen listed above has no exact prototype file, it is assembled from the nearest list/form screen and should be judged by eye.

## UI Designer

### Screens

Rule applied everywhere: icon + word by default. Icon-only (44px target, accessible name = the exact current word, icon aria-hidden when beside a word) only for delete "Borrar…" (`tacho`), search "Buscar" (`buscar`), close "Cerrar", edit "Editar", share "Compartir" (only when it is not the screen's main action), and More info "i". Text-only: links, confirmation answer pairs, form/sheet submits, option choices (period tabs, Efectivo·Transferencia·Debiendo, Promoción·Consumo propio, origin, Claro·Oscuro, "Todo: $ X"), Deshacer. Icons come from the one family (stroke 1.6, 22px, no emoji). No behaviour, route, confirmation, toast, tap-budget or accessible-name change anywhere. Contrast 4.5:1 in both themes; checked by eye with compare.sh at phone and computer, and at 320px with 130% text.

### Icon names (NombreIcono)

Existing and reused as is: the five tabs, `atras`, `chevron`, `abajo`, `buscar`, `calendario`, `tacho`, `cobro`, `personaMas`. The existing `mas` stays the Más tab's three dots and is NOT reused for any "+" action.

New names, added to `NombreIcono` only when a screen below uses them. Each is distinct inside its own menu:

- Más menu: `masGasto` (+ Cargar gasto), `salidaStock` (Sacar del stock), `recibo` (Gastos), `grafico` (Análisis), `ajustes` (Configuración).
- Configuración groups (seven, all different): `negocio` (Negocio), `usuarios` (Usuarios), `enchufe` (Integraciones), `mensaje` (Mensaje del catálogo), `etiqueta` (Categorías de gastos), `apariencia` (Apariencia), `cuenta` (Tu cuenta).
- Actions: `masVenta` (+ Nueva venta), `entradaStock` (+ Entrada de stock), `enviar` (Mandar catálogo), `copiar` (Copiar), `publicar` (Publicar), `precio` (Nuevo precio), `reintentar` (Reintentar), `restaurar` stays only with its current meaning (restore), `probar` (Enviarme una prueba).
- `atencion`: the single decorative attention icon, only on the six allowed notices.

No icon repeats within one menu, and none duplicates a bar or sidebar icon in the same view.

### Screens

**Inicio** (built from the existing Inicio screen, `Inicio.dc.html`)
- States: first time (checklist), empty, loading, filled, stale catalogue, offline, server error.
- Change: the quick actions "+ Nueva venta" (`masVenta`), "+ Entrada de stock" (`entradaStock`), Cobrar (`cobro`) and "Mandar catálogo" (`enviar`) become icon + word. The period tabs stay text-only. "Todo: $ X" stays text. The help line is ≤80 chars, one sentence. The notice "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" carries `atencion`. The after-import notice "Conectá el bot…" carries `atencion` and keeps only its existing "×" Descartar aviso. No "Conectar" button is added. The first-run checklist, help lines and toasts get no `atencion`.
- Empty state: title ≤40 chars, one line ≤80 chars, one action.

**Inicio, primera vez** (Estados/Inicio checklist)
- The checklist stays text-led with no `atencion`. "Arrancá por las variedades" is drawn as an empty state, yet it is one of the six allowed notices, so it is the single empty-state exception and carries `atencion`. Its action is icon + word.

**Más** (`Mas.dc.html`)
- Order unchanged: + Cargar gasto (`masGasto`), Sacar del stock (`salidaStock`), Gastos (`recibo`), Análisis (`grafico`), Configuración (`ajustes`), each icon + word. Cargar gasto has one icon only, different from Gastos.
- States: filled only (static list).

**Configuración, list** (`Configuracion*.dc.html`)
- Seven groups, each icon + word with its own icon: Negocio `negocio`, Usuarios `usuarios`, Integraciones `enchufe`, Mensaje del catálogo `mensaje`, Categorías de gastos `etiqueta`, Apariencia `apariencia`, Tu cuenta `cuenta`. The chevron stays at the end.
- States: filled; loading skeleton rows keep the icon slot.

**Configuración, groups**
- Negocio, Usuarios, Mensaje del catálogo, Categorías de gastos: help line ≤80 chars, one sentence. Submits stay text-only. Delete "Borrar…" is icon-only (`tacho`, name unchanged). Edit "Editar" is icon-only. Usuarios: "Invitar" keeps `personaMas` + word.
- Integraciones: "Enviarme una prueba" becomes icon + word (`probar`). Its reason stays visible, aims for 80 chars and keeps the fix.
- Apariencia: Claro·Oscuro stay text-only option choices.
- Tu cuenta: the reveal control stays the text link "Mostrar". Security texts add no information. Submits are text-only.
- Each group has states: filled, saving, error (inline, no `atencion` unless it is the server-error retry), and a refusal state whose text aims for 80 chars and keeps the fix.

**Variedades, list** (existing list screen)
- Search "Buscar" is icon-only (`buscar`). The "+" add action is icon + word. States: empty, loading, filled, no results, error.
- The empty state follows the title ≤40 + one line ≤80 + action pattern, with no `atencion`.

**Variedad, detalle**
- Edit "Editar" is icon-only. Delete "Borrar…" is icon-only (`tacho`). "Nuevo precio" is icon + word (`precio`). "+ Entrada de stock" is icon + word (`entradaStock`).
- More info "i": 44px at the end of the line, accessible name "Más info", aria-expanded, opens closed, not remembered (no storage per ADR-0026), never triggers the leave guard, holds only how/why.
- States: filled, loading, error, no stock.

**Nuevo precio**
- The submit "Guardá el precio" is text-only. Origin choices are text-only. Help line ≤80 chars. States: pristine, invalid, saving, error.

**Catálogo** (`Catalogo.dc.html`)
- Compartir is the screen's main action, so it keeps its word (text plus its existing treatment) and is not icon-only. Copiar is icon + word (`copiar`). Publicar is icon + word (`publicar`).
- "Desactualizado" plus its reason carries `atencion`. The reason is one sentence ≤80 chars. "Al día" has no icon. The disabled-Publicar reason has no icon.
- States: up to date, stale, disabled Publicar, publishing, empty, loading, error. The error and offline notices carry `atencion`.

**Entrada de stock**
- The submit is text-only. The Promoción·Consumo propio and origin choices are text-only. Help line ≤80 chars. The "i" More info follows the pattern above. States: pristine, invalid, saving, error, confirmed (toast, no icon).

**Todas las entradas**
- Rows are icon + word where they carry actions. Delete "Borrar…" is icon-only. Date filter keeps `calendario`. States: empty, loading, filled, error.

**Sacar del stock / Retiro**
- The submit and the answer pair in the confirmation are text-only, and the confirmation keeps every fact. Consequences stay visible and short. States: pristine, invalid, confirming, saving, error.

**Nueva venta**
- Efectivo·Transferencia·Debiendo are text-only option choices. "+ Cliente" is icon + word (`personaMas`). The submit is text-only. The leave guard confirmation is a text-only answer pair. States: pristine, invalid, saving, error, offline.

**Ventas / Venta, detalle**
- Search is icon-only. Rows are filled, empty, loading, error. Detail: Editar and Borrar… icon-only, Deshacer text-only in its toast.

**Clientes / ficha**
- "Cobrar" is icon + word (`cobro`). Search is icon-only. States: empty, loading, filled, debt, no debt, error.

**Cobro** (Registrar cobro)
- The submit "Registrar cobro" is text-only. "Todo: $ X" stays text. Efectivo·Transferencia stay text. States: pristine, invalid, saving, error.

**Gastos / Nuevo gasto**
- The expense form has no Efectivo·Transferencia·Debiendo choice. The submit is text-only. Category choices are text-only. States: empty, loading, filled, saving, error.

**Análisis**
- The period tabs are text-only. States: empty, loading, filled, error.

**Entrar / Invitación / Negocio nuevo / Reset**
- Submits and links are text-only. Security texts add no information. Invitación's accept action is text-only. States: pristine, invalid, submitting, error.

**Estados** (`Estados.dc.html`)
- Empty states get no icon. "Sin conexión" carries `atencion` on its single sentence, ≤80 chars, one sentence. The server-error notice carries `atencion` and its retry is icon + word "Reintentar" (`reintentar`, the current word, not reusing `restaurar`). "Eso ya no está.", "Al día" and toasts get no `atencion`.

### Gaps for UI Lead

- More info "i" has no drawn state in the prototype; it is assembled from the 44px icon button and the existing help line.
- The server-error retry has no drawn screen; it is assembled from the offline notice pattern plus "Reintentar".
- All new names above (`masGasto`, `salidaStock`, `recibo`, `grafico`, `ajustes`, the seven group icons, `masVenta`, `entradaStock`, `enviar`, `copiar`, `publicar`, `precio`, `reintentar`, `probar`, `atencion`) are not yet in `NombreIcono` or `Icono.tsx` and must be added in the same stroke family.
- Screens such as Nueva venta, Cobro, Entrada de stock, Variedad detalle and Invitación are assembled from the product's components and have no exact prototype to compare beyond the as-is draw.
