## Human

### Decision 1

Escalated: UI Lead→UI Designer hit 2 returns: Restore Integraciones' Probarlo and Buscar mi chat, put Archivar and Restaurar back with their own icons, and name the icon for every icon-only and icon + text control (Editar, Cerrar, Más info, main Compartir, Cambiar contraseña, Cerrar sesión, Archivar, Restaurar).

**Decision for the next design attempt** (after UI Lead → UI Designer hit its return limit):

The UI Lead's last return is right; the next attempt keeps everything already fixed and does exactly this:

1. **Integraciones:** keep the built name **"Probarlo"** (do not rename to "Enviarme una prueba"; the accessible name and pinned-catalog.md stay). Give it its icon (`probar`). Restore **"Buscar mi chat"** as icon + text. Write the own-chat line, its More info text and the short consequence line.
2. **Variedades / a variety's page:** put **Archivar** and **Restaurar** back, each icon + text with its own icon (`archivar`, `restaurar`), on the screens that have them today (the variety's page, Archivadas and the archived page). The secondary "Sacar del stock" reuses `salidaStock`.
3. **Name the icon of every icon-only and icon + text control**: Editar (`lapiz`), Cerrar, More info "i", main Compartir (Catálogo), Cambiar contraseña (`candado`), Cerrar sesión (`salirSesion`), Archivar, Restaurar. Nothing may be specified as "icon" without a `NombreIcono` name.
4. **NombreIcono:** reconcile `componentes.ts` with `Icono.tsx` first (`agregar`, `stock`, `whatsapp`, `telegram` already drawn), and reuse them instead of adding duplicates.
5. Small copy: Usuarios keeps "Invitá a un socio"; Cobro's submit stays "Registrar". Say whether the Compartir under the fresh invitation link is icon-only (obvious action: icon-only with an accessible name is fine).

Rule reminder from the issue: icon-only only for obvious actions (delete, search, close, share, edit), always with an accessible name; navigation and menus always icon + text; never remove an existing action or rename it while adding icons.

## PM: consolidation

### Verdict

`approved`: the flow and screens, with the human's Decision 1 applied, serve every job with no step, route or name lost; nothing UX found stops a job.

### PRD

#### Job to be done

**When** I'm on my phone between orders or mid-chat with a customer and I need to find an entry, an action or what a notice is telling me, **I want** to recognise it at a glance by its icon and a short label, and read any help or notice as one short line, **so I can** get to it without reading blocks of text and get back to the customer.

- This refines the navigation job (navigation.md) and the "feels like a phone app" job (accounts.md). It adds no capability.
- The protagonist is the seller, using the phone in a hurry and with one hand.
- Setup screens (Configuración, Integraciones, the catalog) follow the same rule. The computer is the same job on a bigger screen.

#### Success criteria

1. **Found at a glance.** Every entry in Más, in Configuración's list of groups and in action rows, and every action, shows an icon next to its text. The only exceptions are the two closed lists below. Navigation and menus always show icon + text, and "no tab is an icon alone" still holds.
2. **One rule, written once.** The design-system README and `docs/product/icon-and-text-rule.md` state the same thing:
   - the two closed lists;
   - the length limits;
   - what may go behind More info, and what never does.
3. **Short by default, nothing lost.** Always-visible help lines, notices and empty states fit the limits, and text that repeats itself is cut. A longer explanation that is still needed stays one tap away behind More info. Nothing is deleted silently.
4. **One icon family, no state by icon or colour alone.**
   - Every icon is stroke 1.6 at 22 px, named in `NombreIcono`, in both themes.
   - No emoji is used as an interface icon.
   - The word or shape that carries a state keeps carrying it.
5. **Nothing else changes.** These stay as they are:
   - actions, routes, entry order, confirmations, toast wording and tap budgets;
   - every rule about what a screen reveals;
   - every accessible name.

   Existing tests pass unchanged, except where copy is deliberately shortened. In that case the owning doc and its test change in the same diff.
6. **Looks as designed.** `scripts/design/compare.sh` runs before and after for every screen, phone and computer, both themes, and the result is judged by eye.

#### Behaviour: the rule

**1. Which form each control takes.** The lists are closed. Adding to either one changes the rule doc, never a single screen.

- **Icon-only.** These may be shown as an icon alone:
  - delete "Borrar…" (`tacho`);
  - search "Buscar" (`buscar`);
  - close "Cerrar";
  - edit "Editar" (`lapiz`);
  - share "Compartir", only where it is not the screen's main action.

  Rules for icon-only controls:
  - The accessible name is the exact current word, so "Cerrar, never Volver" still holds.
  - The target is at least 44 px.
  - Being on the list allows icon-only but never requires it. A screen's main action always shows its word.
  - Back is not on the list. It stays "‹ <parent>".
  - More info's "i" is the one icon-only control outside this list.
- **Text only:**
  - links (`enlace`);
  - the answer pair inside a confirmation, with its exact words;
  - the submit of a form or sheet ("Guardá la venta", "Registrar", "Guardar y entrar", "Entrá" and the rest);
  - choices among options: the period tabs, Efectivo · Transferencia · Debiendo, Promoción · Consumo propio, an entry's origin, Claro · Oscuro, "Todo: $ X";
  - Deshacer inside "Venta guardada";
  - the password reveal link "Mostrar" / "Ocultar". Its word carries the state.
- **Icon + text:** everything else, and any new action that fits neither list. The icon is decorative and hidden from assistive technology (`aria-hidden`), so the accessible name stays the word.

**2. Navigation, menus and rows.**
- The bar, the sidebar, Más and Configuración's list of groups always show icon + text.
- Entry rows and action rows get a leading icon.
- Record rows keep their layout and state marks: `MarcaEstado`, amber "te debe", "A favor". Actions on them follow section 1.
- Within one menu, no two entries share an icon.

**3. Icons.**
- One family: stroke 1.6, 22 px, no emoji.
- Prefer a specific metaphor. No generic placeholder icons.
- The same meaning gets the same icon everywhere.
- A name joins `NombreIcono` only when a screen uses it.
- No control is specified as "icon" without a `NombreIcono` name.

**4. Length of always-visible text.**
- A help line or notice is one sentence of at most 80 characters.
- An empty state is a title of at most 40 characters, plus one line of at most 80, plus its action if it has one.
- Inline refusals and the reasons of Probarlo and Buscar mi chat aim for 80 characters. They keep their fix even when it runs longer, and they never go behind More info.
- Consequences stay visible in short form:
  - the old pinned message staying as it is;
  - the grams on hand in the archive confirmation;
  - the grams that return to stock on delete.
- A shortened confirmation keeps every fact it names: date, amount, grams and reason.
- Security texts (the brake message, the Entrar hint, the dead-link texts) keep their meaning and never add information.
- Characters are counted on the Spanish copy as shown.

**5. More info.**
- An "i" in a 44 px target sits at the end of the line it explains.
- Tapping it expands the text in place, and a second tap collapses it. It is never a modal or a sheet.
- It always opens closed and is never remembered (ADR-0026).
- Its accessible name is "Más info", with `aria-expanded`.
- Opening it never counts as an unsaved change.
- It holds only the how or why: never a consequence, a refusal's fix or a state.
- A line whose explanation fits in 80 characters has no More info.

**6. Notices.**
- The attention icon `atencion` is one decorative icon, the same everywhere. It sits next to the sentence on exactly these six:
  - Inicio's "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado";
  - "Desactualizado" with its reason;
  - "Arrancá por las variedades", the only empty state that carries it;
  - the after-import "Conectá el bot…";
  - "Sin conexión";
  - the server-error retry.
- These get no icon:
  - "Al día";
  - "Eso ya no está." and its list form;
  - toasts;
  - the first-run checklist;
  - help lines;
  - every other empty state;
  - the disabled-Publicar reason.

**7. Swapping actions.**
- Archivar and Restaurar are two actions, each with its own icon.
- The low-stock switch is a switch and gets no icon.
- The password reveal swaps its word ("Mostrar" / "Ocultar") and stays a text link.
- For any future swapping action, the word carries the state.

**8. Where it is written.**
- The rule lives in `icon-and-text-rule.md` and in the design-system README.
- CONTEXT.md gains three terms: More info, Icon and text rule, and Attention icon.
- `componentes.ts` gains the new names and More info's component type.
- Shortened copy changes in its owning capability file, together with its test.

#### Icon names (`NombreIcono`)

- **Reconcile first.** Before adding anything, make `componentes.ts` and `Icono.tsx` match:
  - `Icono.tsx` already draws `agregar`, `stock`, `whatsapp` and `telegram`, which `componentes.ts` does not list;
  - reuse those drawings instead of adding duplicates.
- **Kept as they are:**
  - the five tabs;
  - `atras`, `chevron`, `abajo`, `buscar`, `calendario`, `tacho`, `cobro`, `personaMas`;
  - `mas` stays the Más tab's dots and is never used for "+".
- **New names, used by the screens below:**
  - Más: `masGasto`, `salidaStock`, `recibo`, `grafico`, `ajustes`.
  - Configuración: `negocio`, `usuarios`, `enchufe`, `mensaje`, `etiqueta`, `apariencia`, `cuenta`.
  - Actions:
    - `masVenta`, `entradaStock`, `enviar`, `copiar`, `publicar`, `precio`;
    - `reintentar`, `probar`;
    - `archivar`, `restaurar`;
    - `lapiz` (Editar), `candado` (Cambiar contraseña), `salirSesion` (Cerrar sesión);
    - `atencion`.
- **Still to name.** Cerrar, More info's "i", the main Compartir and Buscar mi chat each need a name at build, reusing an existing drawing where the meaning is the same.

#### Flows (validated, folded into each job)

Every flow keeps its steps, routes, confirmations, recoveries and touch budgets. The one new touch is opening More info, which is optional and never a required step.

**Find an entry or action at a glance (main job).**
- Starts at: any tab root on the phone, or the sidebar on a computer.
- Steps:
  1. Glance at an entry or action row, each shown as icon + word (0 touches).
  2. Tap it (1 touch).
- Screens: Más, Configuración's 7 groups, Variedades, a variety's page, Clientes, Inicio, the catalog screen, Integraciones.
- Recovery:
  - if an icon is unclear, the word is still there;
  - icon-only controls keep their 44 px target and their existing confirmations.
- Budgets, unchanged:
  - sale: 1 tap;
  - Registrar cobro, the catalog screen, a stock entry, a withdrawal, an expense and Configuración: at most 2 taps each.

**Record a sale.**
- Steps, unchanged: "+ Nueva venta" (`masVenta`) → customer → lines → shipping → note → payment → "Guardá la venta". The sale is recorded in under 60 s.
- Text only: Efectivo · Transferencia · Debiendo, the submit, and "No, seguí cargando / Sí, descartala".
- "Cerrar" may be icon-only.
- "Para dejar debiendo elegí un cliente" fits in 80 characters.
- Refusals stay inline, keeping what was typed and their fix.
- Ends at: the sale's detail, with "Venta guardada" and its text-only Deshacer.

**Record a payment.**
- Steps, unchanged: Clientes or Inicio → "Cobrar" (`cobro` + word, accessible name "Cobrar a <name>") → amount ("Todo: $ X" as text) → method → "Registrar". At most 2 taps.
- Ends at: the opening screen, with "Cobro guardado · queda debiendo $ X".

**Record stock and withdrawals, and take a variety out.**
- Steps, unchanged, from Variedades, a variety's page or Más. At most 2 taps.
- Icon + text:
  - "+ Entrada de stock" and "+ Entrada" (`entradaStock`);
  - "Sacar del stock" (`salidaStock`, in Más, on Variedades and on a variety's page);
  - "Nuevo precio" (`precio`).
- Origin and reason are text-only choices. Help lines fit in 80 characters.
- Taking a variety out:
  - "Archivar variedad" (`archivar`) and "Restaurar" (`restaurar`) are icon + text, on the variety's page, Archivadas and the archived page;
  - the archive confirmation keeps the grams on hand;
  - "Eliminar variedad" keeps its destructive confirmation.
- The low-stock switch is unchanged.

**Publish the pinned catalog.**
- Steps, unchanged: Configuración → Integraciones (`enchufe`) → token and chats → "Buscar mi chat" (icon + text) → "Probarlo" (`probar` + its unchanged word; the accessible name and pinned-catalog.md are untouched) → link to the catalog screen → "Publicar" (`publicar`).
- Own-chat line: one sentence of at most 80 characters, telling the seller to tap Iniciar on the bot. Its More info holds the how: getting the number from @userinfobot, or tapping Buscar mi chat.
- After a token or channel change, a short consequence line stays visible: the old pinned message stays as it is, and the seller unpins it in Telegram.
- Reasons of Probarlo and Buscar mi chat stay inline with their fix.
- Ends at: the catalog screen, showing "Al día" (no icon) or "Desactualizado" plus its reason (`atencion`).

**Read a notice or a help line.**
- Steps:
  1. Read one short line (0 touches).
  2. Optionally, tap "Más info" (1 touch) to expand it in place.
- Nothing is stored.
- The attention icon goes on the six notices only.

**Getting in.**
- Steps, unchanged.
- Submits and links are text only, and the reveal is the "Mostrar" / "Ocultar" link.
- Security texts keep their meaning.
- The dead-link screen keeps its one button to Entrar.

**Other jobs.** Expenses, Análisis, customers, and the Negocio, Usuarios, Apariencia and Tu cuenta groups keep their flow. Only the rule applies to them:
- "Invitá a un socio": `personaMas` + its word;
- the Compartir under the fresh join link: icon-only, accessible name "Compartir";
- "Cambiar contraseña": `candado` + word;
- "Cerrar sesión": `salirSesion` + word;
- Claro · Oscuro: text only.

#### Screens design (validated)

**Inicio** (`Inicio.dc.html`)
- States: first run, empty, loading, filled, stale catalog, offline, server error.
- Icon + text: "+ Nueva venta" (`masVenta`), "+ Entrada de stock" (`entradaStock`), Cobrar (`cobro`) and "Mandar catálogo" (`enviar`).
- Period tabs and "Todo: $ X" stay text.
- The stale-catalog notice and the after-import notice carry `atencion`. The after-import notice keeps only its "×" (Descartar aviso), with no new button.
- The first-run checklist keeps its boxes and "Hecho", with no icon.
- "Arrancá por las variedades" is drawn as an empty state, carries `atencion`, and its action is icon + text.

**Más** (`Mas.dc.html`)
- In order: "+ Cargar gasto" (`masGasto`), "Sacar del stock" (`salidaStock`), Gastos (`recibo`), Análisis (`grafico`), Configuración (`ajustes`).
- Each entry is icon + word, with the chevron at the end.

**Configuración, list**
- Negocio `negocio`, Usuarios `usuarios`, Integraciones `enchufe`, Mensaje del catálogo `mensaje`, Categorías de gastos `etiqueta`, Apariencia `apariencia`, Tu cuenta `cuenta`.
- The loading skeleton keeps the icon slot.

**Configuración, groups**
- Shared:
  - help lines at most 80 characters;
  - submits text only;
  - "Borrar…" (`tacho`) and "Editar" (`lapiz`) icon-only;
  - "Cerrar" icon-only, with the name "Cerrar".
- Per group:
  - Usuarios: "Invitá a un socio" (`personaMas`), and the link's Compartir icon-only.
  - Integraciones: "Probarlo" (`probar`) and "Buscar mi chat", both icon + text, plus the own-chat line, its More info and the consequence line described above.
  - Apariencia: Claro · Oscuro, text only.
  - Tu cuenta: "Cambiar contraseña" (`candado`) and "Cerrar sesión" (`salirSesion`), with the "Mostrar" link.
- States: filled, saving, inline refusal (keeping the fix), error.

**Variedades, list**
- Search is icon-only (`buscar`).
- Icon + text: "Nueva variedad", "+ Entrada de stock" (`entradaStock`), "Sacar del stock" (`salidaStock`) and "Mandar catálogo" (`enviar`).
- Empty state: a title of at most 40 characters plus a line of at most 80, with no icon.
- States: empty, loading, filled, no results, error.

**A variety's page**
- Icon + text: "+ Entrada" (`entradaStock`), "Nuevo precio" (`precio`), "Sacar del stock" (`salidaStock`) and "Archivar variedad" (`archivar`) or "Eliminar variedad".
- "Editar" (`lapiz`) and "Borrar…" (`tacho`) are icon-only.
- More info appears where a line was shortened.
- **The archived page and Archivadas** each show "Restaurar" (`restaurar` + word).
- States: filled, loading, error, no stock.

**Catálogo** (`Catalogo.dc.html`)
- Compartir is the main action and keeps its word, with its icon.
- Copiar (`copiar`) and Publicar (`publicar`) are icon + text.
- "Desactualizado" and its reason carry `atencion`. "Al día" and the disabled-Publicar reason carry none.
- States: up to date, stale, Publicar disabled, publishing, empty, loading, error.

**Forms**
- Covered: Nueva venta, Cobro, Nuevo precio, Entrada de stock, Sacar del stock / Retiro, Nuevo gasto.
- Submits, answer pairs and choices are text only.
- In Cobro the submit is "Registrar".
- Help lines are at most 80 characters, with More info where needed.
- States: pristine, invalid, saving, error. Nueva venta also has offline.

**Lists**
- Covered: Ventas, Clientes, Todas las entradas, Gastos, Análisis.
- Search and delete are icon-only, and Cobrar is icon + text.
- Record rows are unchanged.
- States: empty, loading, filled, error.

**Getting in**
- Covered: Entrar, Invitación, Negocio nuevo, Reset.
- Submits and links are text only, and the reveal is the "Mostrar" / "Ocultar" link.
- Security texts add nothing.

**Estados** (`Estados.dc.html`)
- "Sin conexión" carries `atencion` on one sentence of at most 80 characters.
- The server error carries `atencion`, and its retry "Reintentar" is `reintentar` + word.
- Empty states, toasts and "Eso ya no está." carry no icon.

#### Metrics

No telemetry.
- **Found at a glance:** a front test over `screens.json` checks that:
  - every entry row, action row and action outside the closed lists has an icon and its text;
  - every icon-only control has its exact current accessible name and a 44 px target;
  - icons next to words are `aria-hidden`;
  - no menu repeats an icon.

  Target: 0 exceptions.
- **One rule:**
  - the README and `icon-and-text-rule.md` state the same lists and limits;
  - every `NombreIcono` name added here is used by a screen (0 unused new names);
  - `componentes.ts` and `Icono.tsx` hold the same names.
- **Short by default:**
  - a test asserts the length limits on the fixed copy;
  - refusals over 80 characters are listed for review;
  - More info opens closed, toggles `aria-expanded`, never triggers the leave confirmation, and holds no consequence or fix;
  - a PR list shows every shortened or moved text (0 silent deletions).
- **Icon family and states:**
  - a guard checks stroke 1.6, 22 px and no emoji;
  - every state keeps its word or shape in both themes;
  - `atencion` is on the six listed notices only.
- **Nothing else changes:**
  - the existing suites pass, with edits only for listed shortened copy;
  - "Probarlo", "Cobrar a <name>", "Cerrar" and every other name are unchanged;
  - tap budgets and the redirect table are unchanged;
  - security texts add no information.
- **Looks as designed:**
  - `compare.sh` before and after, on every screen, phone and computer, both themes;
  - at 320 px with text at 130%, nothing is clipped and the bar still fits.
- **Dashboard:** none.

#### Out of scope

- An icon-only UI, an icon-only back, or an icon-only main action.
- Adding, removing, renaming or reordering any action or entry; changing routes, confirmations or toast wording.
- Icons on record rows; optional icons on notices; icons that no screen uses.
- Moving a consequence, a refusal's fix or a state behind More info; remembering whether More info was opened.

#### Sequencing

- Work starts after #59, #47, #77 and #78 merge.
- Check these first: Más, Configuración (Integraciones above all), and Variedades with a variety's page.

### Questions for the human

None.

### Observations on the design

- **Inicio, first run: "Arrancá por las variedades".** The prototype draws it as an empty state, and the design makes it the only empty state that carries `atencion`. Its line (about 82 characters) must also be cut to 80.
- **Estados: "Sin conexión".** The prototype draws two lines. The owning doc should say which single sentence carries the state.
- **Password reveal.** It swaps its word ("Mostrar" / "Ocultar"). The PRD now lists it as text only, with the word carrying the state.
- **Entrar hint.** The prototype shows an attempt count. The "adds no information" check must use accounts.md as its baseline, not the drawing.
- **Catálogo: disabled Publicar reason.** It has no icon. Its wording should stay distinct from the after-import "Conectá el bot…".
- **Unnamed icons.** Buscar mi chat, the main Compartir, Cerrar and More info's "i" still need a `NombreIcono` name at build, reusing an existing drawing where possible.
- **Usuarios.** The Compartir under the fresh join link is icon-only, with the accessible name "Compartir".
- **`NombreIcono` vs `Icono.tsx`.** `agregar`, `stock`, `whatsapp` and `telegram` are drawn but not listed. Reconcile them before adding any plus or stock icon.
- **More info and the server-error retry** have no drawn state. Judge them by eye in the after pass.
- **Prototype lag.** The prototype doesn't draw "Sacar del stock" in Más or "Buscar mi chat", and it shows the old own-chat line. List these in the PR as differences, not defects.

## Interview: Tech Lead efímero→Tech Lead

### Round 1: questions

### Round 1: GH-97, balance icons and text

Concrete failure of the plan as it stands: `web/src/components/Icono.tsx` declares its own `NombreIcono` union (line 17, with a comment saying it adds `'agregar'` to the design's union). `docs/product/design-system/componentes.ts` declares another (line ~262). Both compile on their own. Add `candado` to one and not the other, and `tsc` stays green while the contract and the drawn set disagree. The Human decision to reconcile first only holds if something fails when they drift again. The closed lists of icon-only and text-only controls are also spread over about 15 components and every screen. Without one seam, each screen decides alone, and a delete button can ship with a visible word or an unnamed icon.

❓ **Q1** - **One source of truth for icon names**: Two unions exist, and `TRAZOS: Record<NombreIcono, ReactNode>` only guarantees that every name in the `Icono.tsx` union is drawn. It does not guarantee that the design contract matches. Where does the reconciliation live, and what fails when someone adds a name to one file only? The Human also said new names enter only when a screen uses them. What checks that?
➡️ Make `Icono.tsx` import the type from the design contract, or derive one from the other. Do not keep two literal unions. Add a test that compares the `TRAZOS` keys with the contract's names and fails on any name no screen uses. Put it in the first subtask, before any screen is touched.

❓ **Q2** - **Seam for icon-only versus icon + text**: The PRD fixes the icon-only list (delete, search, close, edit, secondary share) and the text-only list (links, confirmation pairs, submits, option choices, Deshacer). Everything else is icon + text with the icon `aria-hidden`. Today `Boton` and the screens each assemble icon and label. Do you add a small shared control (for example a `variant` or `kind` on `Boton`, plus an `IconButton` that requires an `aria-label` and a 44px target), with the lists as data? Or does each screen edit its own markup?
➡️ Use one shared control. `IconButton` should make `aria-label` required in its types, so an unnamed icon-only control does not compile. `Boton` should render its icon `aria-hidden`. Then add one Testing Library test that renders each screen in `screens.json` with `fetch` faked. It should assert that every button has an accessible name and that the icon inside a labelled button is `aria-hidden`. The test also catches an accidental accessible-name change if you snapshot the names before the edit.

❓ **Q3** - **More info component and its state**: "Más info" is an "i" affordance with `aria-expanded`. It always opens closed, is never remembered, and is not an unsaved change. ADR-0026 allows the browser to store only the theme. ADR-0027 puts sheet state in history, and `useLeaveGuard` ignores only sheet-depth changes. What is the state model, and which module owns it? Where does the 44px "i" come from? Which ADR-0029 contrast row covers the "i" and the expanded text, given that `--tenue` is icon-only?
➡️ Use local component state, with no history entry, no storage and no form-dirty flag. Keep it in one component in `web/src/components/`. Give the expanded text a text-contrast row that already exists. Do not use `--tenue` for text. Add a test that opens it, checks `aria-expanded`, remounts, and checks it is closed. The same test should check that `localStorage` and `history.length` are unchanged.

❓ **Q4** - **Copy limits and where the rule is enforced**: Help lines and notices are at most 80 characters. Empty-state titles are at most 40 characters plus one line of at most 80. The new own-chat line, its More info text and the consequence line must obey this too. As written, only the doc `docs/product/icon-and-text-rule.md` states the limit. Which test fails when a Spanish string goes over?
➡️ Keep the copy for notices, help lines and empty states as constants in one module per screen, or in a shared `copy` module. Write one test that imports them and asserts the limits. Link the rule doc and the design-system README to that test. Do not count characters over rendered DOM only, because strings built at runtime escape the check.

❓ **Q5** - **Subtask split and sizing**: The change reaches every screen, and the Human also added new controls (Buscar mi chat with its own-chat line, Archivar and Restaurar on three pages, Cambiar contraseña, Cerrar sesión, main Compartir). The standing question is which piece would carry more than about four production modules plus tests plus an ADR and need its own seam. A possible split follows. Which of these pieces exceeds the cap, and what would you cut?
1. Icon set, reconciliation and guard test (Q1).
2. `IconButton`, `Boton` icon handling and More info (Q2 and Q3), plus the attention icon on six notices.
3. Screens grouped by area. Candidates are auth and account (Cambiar contraseña, Cerrar sesión), then Integraciones and the chat screen, then variety and Archivadas pages, then stock and sales.
4. Doc and README rule, copy-limit test (Q4).
Which pieces can run in parallel after piece 1 and 2? Does the closed list deserve an ADR, or does the rule doc suffice?
➡️ Run pieces 1 and 2 in sequence. Then run the screen groups in parallel, one per area, each with the Q2 screen test scoped to its screens. Keep the ADR for piece 2 only if the shared control is a new boundary. The rule doc is enough for the closed lists.

### Invariants proposed

- An icon placed beside a visible word is `aria-hidden`, and adding it leaves the control's accessible name unchanged.
- An icon-only control always has an accessible name and a 44px target.
- `NombreIcono` is defined once, and every name in it has a drawn path and at least one screen that uses it.
- More info's open state is local component state: it is never written to browser storage or history and never marks a form as changed.
- No emoji is used as an interface icon.

### Round 1: answers

### What the code shows (it decides several answers)

- `web/src/components/Icono.tsx` holds its own literal union: 17 names, the 13 from the design plus `stock`, `whatsapp`, `telegram` and `agregar`. `docs/product/design-system/componentes.ts` holds the 13. `tsconfig.app.json` includes only `src`. The front build does not depend on `docs/` today, and ADR-0029 / `tests/test_design_tokens.py` already show how this repo keeps a docs copy and an app copy in step: a pytest reading both files.
- `Boton` already has an icon-only mode, and its types force `ariaLabel` when there is no text. `Icono` is already `aria-hidden` unless it gets a `label`. But only 6 screens use `Boton`; `screens/` and `shell/` hold about 80 raw `<button>`s. Several screens pass `size`/`strokeWidth` overrides: the chevron at 18, the shell's "+" at 19 with stroke 2, Gastos' "+" at 20 with 1.8.
- Copy already lives partly in modules: `screens/authMessages.ts`, `screens/settings/messages.ts`, `api/refusals.ts`.

**Q1**: **Overruled in part.** Keep one *drawn* source and pin the design copy to it by test. Do not make the app import the design file:
- **The app's list.** `Icono.tsx` exports `ICON_NAMES = [...] as const` and `type NombreIcono = (typeof ICON_NAMES)[number]`. `TRAZOS: Record<NombreIcono, ReactNode>` stays, so every name is drawn (tsc).
- **The design's list.** `componentes.ts` keeps its literal union; it is the design's readable contract and stays standalone. Importing it into `web/src` would make the app build depend on `docs/`, against the line ADR-0029 and ADR-0033 already draw.
- **The pin.** A new pytest, `tests/test_design_icons.py`, does what `test_design_tokens.py` does: it parses the union in `componentes.ts` and `ICON_NAMES` in `Icono.tsx` and fails on any difference in either direction. Adding a name to one file only is then a red build.
- **Reconciling first** (Human decision 4): `agregar` joins `componentes.ts`. `stock`, `whatsapp` and `telegram` are reused wherever the design's new names would duplicate them. If nothing uses one of them by the end, it leaves `Icono.tsx` (none of the three is among the 13 the PRD keeps).
- **Stroke 1.6 at 22 px** (PRD criterion 4, applied as written). `size` and `strokeWidth` leave `IconoProps` in both files, so tsc flags every override at 18, 19 or 20. The pytest also checks that `componentes.ts` and `Icono` state 22 and 1.6.
- **No emoji.** A vitest real-tree guard, beside `forbiddenApis.real.test.ts`, fails on any Extended_Pictographic code point in non-test `web/src` source. Catalog text comes from the API and is not in source.
- **"Used by a screen".** A real-tree vitest fails for any name in `ICON_NAMES` that no non-test file outside `Icono.tsx` names. A name used only through `Boton`'s closed action map (Q2) counts, because screens reach it through that map. This check lands in the closing subtask (Q5), once every screen has its icons; until then it would be red by construction.
- The pin, the stroke/size change and the emoji guard land in subtask 1, before any screen.

**Q2**: **Accepted, with `Boton` as the one seam instead of a second component:**
- **Icon-only.** `variant: 'icon'` takes `action: IconOnlyAction` (`'delete' | 'search' | 'close' | 'edit' | 'share'`) and a required `ariaLabel: string`. The icon comes from a closed `Record<IconOnlyAction, NombreIcono>` in `Boton.tsx`: delete→`tacho`, search→`buscar`, close→`cerrar`, edit→`lapiz`, share→`compartir`. A screen cannot pick a free icon for an icon-only control, cannot leave it unnamed, and the name is still the exact current word (ADR-0008 style: the code carries the rule, the screen carries the word). The 44 px target lives in `.boton-icono` in `app.css`.
- **Icon + text versus text-only.** On `primary`, `secondary` and `destructive`, `icon` becomes a required `NombreIcono | null`. `null` marks the text-only list (submits, confirmation pairs), so every text-only control is a decision you can see in review. `link` and `row` take no icon. `Icono` inside a labelled `Boton` is always rendered `aria-hidden` (it already is by default, and the `label` path is closed for `Boton`).
- **Options** (`Pestanas`, `SelectorPago`, `OpcionInterruptor`) stay text-only components and are untouched.
- **Tests.** One shared Testing Library helper, `expectIconAndTextRule(container)` in `web/src/components/`. It asserts that:
  - every button and link has an accessible name;
  - every `svg` inside a control with visible text is `aria-hidden`;
  - every control with no visible text has the `boton-icono` class;
  - no two entries of an element marked `data-menu` share an icon.

  Each area subtask calls it from its own screens' existing tests, over the screens it touches. A single test that mounts all ~40 screens with fakes is refused: one fixture to maintain for every screen's fetches, and it breaks on unrelated changes.
- **The snapshot of accessible names** is the existing suites, which find controls by role and name. Per PRD criterion 5 they must pass unedited, except for deliberately shortened copy.
- **Closing guard.** The last subtask adds a real-tree guard: no raw `<button` and no raw `<svg>` in `screens/` and `shell/`. That is the point when every screen goes through the seam.
- **Where old and new shapes coexist:** raw buttons remain only until each area subtask migrates its screens; nothing is left after the closing subtask.

**Q3**: **Accepted:**
- **Ownership and state.** `web/src/components/MasInfo.tsx` owns it with a local `useState(false)`. There is no history entry (so ADR-0027 is untouched), no storage (ADR-0026 is untouched), and no participation in any form's values, so `useLeaveGuard`'s dirty flag cannot see it.
- **Markup.** It renders a `button type="button"` named "Más info" with `aria-expanded` and `aria-controls`, using the family's `info` icon at 22 px in a 44 px target (the same `.boton-icono` sizing). The text expands in place below the line.
- **Contrast.** The expanded text uses `--tinta2`, a *general* text token in ADR-0029's table that clears 4.5:1 on all four surfaces, so it needs no new README row. The "i" also takes `--tinta2`, not `--tenue`: it is a control, and `--tenue` is 3.03:1 on `--papel` in light and unmeasured elsewhere.
- **The attention icon** draws in `currentColor`, the sentence's own colour, so it needs no row either.
- **Test.** It opens the info and checks `aria-expanded="true"`, then remounts and checks that it is closed. It checks that `localStorage` and `sessionStorage` are unchanged and `history.length` is unchanged. Inside a dirty-tracking form (Integraciones' fixture), opening it and then pressing "Cerrar" closes the form at once with no leave confirmation.

**Q4**: **Accepted, made automatic:**
- **The limits.** `web/src/copy/limits.ts` holds `LIMITS` and `type CopyKind = 'help' | 'notice' | 'emptyTitle' | 'emptyLine' | 'refusal' | 'moreInfo'`.
- **The copy modules.** Each area's copy module stays where it is (`authMessages.ts`, `settings/messages.ts`, and one per area that has none yet). Each exports a `LIMITED_COPY: readonly { kind: CopyKind; text: string }[]`.
- **Copy with a date or figure** is a function. It is registered by calling it with a worst-case sample: `dd/mm`, `$ 999.999`, `999,99 g`.
- **The test.** `copyLimits.test.ts` collects every module with `import.meta.glob('../**/*messages.ts', { eager: true })` and fails on:
  - help, notice or moreInfo text over 80 characters;
  - emptyTitle over 40, emptyLine over 80;
  - a module matched by the glob that exports no registry.

  A new module is therefore covered without anyone listing it.
- **Counting.** Characters are counted as code points after NFC (`[...s.normalize('NFC')].length`), on the Spanish text as shown.
- **Refusals** (`api/refusals.ts` plus the Probarlo and Buscar mi chat reasons) are not failed at 80. Instead, the test asserts that the set of refusals over 80 equals a fixed list written in the test. That list is the "listed for review" of the PRD: a new long refusal fails until someone adds it to the list on purpose.
- Copy built in JSX at runtime is moved into these modules by the area that touches it. The rule doc and the design README link to `copyLimits.test.ts`.

**Q5**: **Modified.** The pieces run in sequence through 2; the areas then run in parallel; a closing piece ends it. All new icon names are drawn at once in piece 1, so parallel areas never edit `Icono.tsx` or `componentes.ts`.

1. **Icon set** (Q1):
   - every name the approved design names, drawn in `Icono.tsx` and added to `componentes.ts`: `lapiz`, `cerrar`, `info`, `compartir`, `candado`, `salirSesion`, `archivar`, `restaurar`, `probar`, `salidaStock`, `atencion`, the Más and Configuración entry icons, with the existing four reused;
   - the pytest pin, the removal of `size`/`strokeWidth`, the emoji guard;
   - the README's icon section and the closed lists, mirroring `icon-and-text-rule.md` (the PM has written it; this piece links the tests).
2. **Seam:**
   - the `Boton` contract (Q2), updating its 6 current callers, plus `expectIconAndTextRule`;
   - `MasInfo` (Q3);
   - the attention icon as an `attention` prop on the shared notice component (`Notice.tsx`), so "one icon, same everywhere" has one author;
   - `copy/limits.ts` with its glob test (Q4).

   That is four production modules, at the cap; the six notice call sites move in their areas. **It gets a short ADR**: `Boton`'s closed icon-only action map and the required `icon: NombreIcono | null` are a new rule every screen must follow, which is the kind of boundary an ADR records. The closed lists themselves are product rules, and `icon-and-text-rule.md` is enough for them.
3. **Screens, in parallel, one subtask per area.** Each area migrates its raw buttons, registers its copy, sets its notices' `attention`, calls the helper in its tests, and runs `compare.sh` before and after for its screens (phone and computer, both themes):
   - **(a)** shell (bar, sidebar), Más, Configuración's list and the Negocio, Usuarios, Apariencia, Tu cuenta, Mensaje del catálogo and Categorías groups;
   - **(b)** Integraciones (Probarlo with `probar`, Buscar mi chat, the own-chat line with its More info and the consequence line), the catalog screen (main Compartir with its text), PinPublish;
   - **(c)** Variedades, a variety's page, Archivadas, the archived page (Archivar/Restaurar with their own icons), the stock entry form, the entries lists, withdrawals (`salidaStock`);
   - **(d)** Ventas, Nueva venta, a sale's detail, Clientes, the customer card and form, Registrar cobro;
   - **(e)** Inicio (the four notices), Gastos with its expense form, Análisis, SinConexion and ErrorGenerico, and the getting-in screens.

   `app.css` is shared: each area edits only its own commented section, and piece 2 owns the shared classes.

   Areas (b) and (c) go first, as the PM asked. Their edits are mechanical on existing modules and open no new seam, so the four-module cap does not apply to them the same way.
4. **Closing** (after every area):
   - the "every name used" check (Q1);
   - the no-raw-`<button>`/`<svg>` guard (Q2);
   - the 320 px at 130 % bar check;
   - the PR list of shortened and moved texts, with the comparisons attached.

**Invariants**: all five accepted, plus four:
- an icon-only control's icon is chosen from `Boton`'s closed action map, never by the screen;
- every always-visible help line, notice and empty-state string is in a copy registry that the limit test reads;
- `size` and `strokeWidth` are not props of `Icono`;
- `componentes.ts` and `Icono.tsx` list exactly the same names (pytest).

**Coexisting shapes**: a screen's raw buttons and inline copy stay until its area subtask migrates them; the closing guard removes that state. Existing hand-written icon sizes go away in piece 1, since that is a type change.

Round 1 is settled as you answered it, except for the points below where the plan still breaks.

**Where the plan fails now.** Three concrete failures:

- The "every name used" check counts a name that appears in `Boton`'s closed map. That map names all five actions whether or not a screen uses them. `compartir` or `cerrar` would pass as "used" with zero screens, which breaks your own invariant.

❓ **Q1** - **Piece 1 makes the tree red**: Removing `size` and `strokeWidth` from `IconoProps` is a type change. Until areas (a) to (e) land, `tsc` fails on every override. The areas run in parallel and each is reviewed on its own branch, so each branch would have to fix the overrides of other areas to build. Who edits those call sites, and in which diff?

Run `grep -rn "strokeWidth\|size=" web/src` and put the count in the subtask brief. If that count exceeds about four production modules, split it out as piece 1b before piece 2. Do not leave the tree red between pieces.

❓ **Q2** - **"Used by a screen" check can be satisfied by `Boton` itself**: The check greps non-test files outside `Icono.tsx` for each name. `Boton.tsx` contains the `Record<IconOnlyAction, NombreIcono>` map, which names `tacho`, `buscar`, `cerrar`, `lapiz` and `compartir` unconditionally. The PRD says new names enter only when a screen uses them. Your check cannot tell a used icon from one that only sits in the map.

❓ **Q3** - **Copy registry is self-declared and the glob misses files**: `*messages.ts` does not match `authMessages.ts` (capital M). It would also silently skip any new module named differently. The registry then catches static copy, and the rendered check catches anything assembled in JSX.

A guard that forbids every raw `<button>` and `<svg>` forces these into `Boton` variants that do not fit, or gets an exemption list that grows. The helper `expectIconAndTextRule` also lives in `web/src/components/`. Do `tsc`, the bundler and the architecture tests treat a non-test helper there as production code?

Decide the exemptions up front as a named closed list (chart `svg`, `MarcaEstado`) written into the guard. Rename the helper `*.test-helper.ts` or put it under `web/src/test/`, and confirm that the build excludes it and that `tests/architecture/` does not flag it.

Area (e) holds Inicio, Gastos, Análisis, the error screens and getting-in. Which of these carries more than about four production modules plus tests plus an ADR and needs its own seam? Where is the cut?

### Round 2: answers

### What I re-read for this round

- **`size`/`strokeWidth` overrides:** 11 call sites in 8 files. Shell.tsx has 2, Gastos.tsx 3, and Mas, Settings, AccountGroup, Ventas, NuevaVenta and NuevaVentaCliente have one each. The 12th match is the prop's own default in Icono.tsx.

- **Raw `<button>`:** about 80 in `screens/`, none in `shell/`. By class:
  - about 31 use `boton` or `boton--suave` / `boton--destructivo`, many of them submits;
  - about 25 use `boton--enlace` / `boton-enlace`, including about 10 "Cerrar" buttons (`gasto-form__cerrar`, `nueva-venta__cerrar`, `fila-entrada__volver`);
  - one is `boton-icono`; one is `historial-fila__borrar` (a delete); one is `venta-guardada__deshacer`;
  - the rest are option or row controls: `pildora`, `periodos__boton`, `gastos__mes-boton`, `fila-gasto`, `ajustes__item--boton`, `encabezado__titulo`, `role="switch"` and `role="radio"`.
- **Links styled as actions:** 17 `InAppLink`s carry `className="boton…"`, among them "Mandar catálogo", "+ Entrada de stock" and "Sacar del stock". They are actions under the rule, and the round-1 seam did not cover them.

**Q1**: **Accepted.** Piece 1 removes the two props and deletes all 11 overrides in the same diff, so the tree is never red.
- The brief records the count: 11 call sites in 8 files.
- These edits only delete a prop: they add no logic and no seam. I keep them in piece 1 rather than opening a 1b. The only production module piece 1 actually changes is `Icono.tsx`.
- Piece 1 runs `tsc` and `oxlint` green on its own.
- From then on, no area subtask edits `Icono.tsx` or `componentes.ts`.
- If an area finds an icon name the design did not provide, that is a follow-up diff to piece 1, never an edit inside the area.

**Q2**: **Accepted.** The "used" check scans non-test files under `screens/`, `shell/` and `components/`, and leaves out `Icono.tsx` and `Boton.tsx`.
- A name in `Boton`'s icon-only map counts as used only if some non-test file under `screens/` or `shell/` passes the matching `action="…"`. The test reads the map to pair each action with its name.
- A name drawn inside a shared component counts through that component's source. That covers `info` in `MasInfo` and `atencion` in `Notice`. The closing subtask also asserts that `MasInfo` and the `attention` prop each have a caller under `screens/` or `shell/`, so a component nobody renders cannot keep its icon "used".
- The check stays in the closing subtask.

**Q3**: **Accepted, with one change: the modules stay where they are.**
- `authMessages.ts` is pinned to `screens/` by ADR-0025, and existing tests import it there.
- Copy modules follow a naming rule instead: `messages.ts` or `<area>Messages.ts`.
- The test collects them with `import.meta.glob('/src/**/{messages,*Messages}.ts', { eager: true })`.
- The same test asserts that the glob finds a fixed list of known modules. A pattern that silently stops matching fails at once.
- The second invariant holds by construction. A screen renders the exported constant (or the exported function, for copy with a date or figure). A registry entry refers to that same binding, never to a retyped string.
- **Rendered check:** `expectIconAndTextRule` also measures rendered text, in code points after NFC:
  - `role="note"`, `role="status"`, `.pie`, the help-line class and the notice classes: at most 80;
  - `.estado-vacio__titulo`: at most 40;
  - `.estado-vacio__detalle`: at most 80.
- `role="alert"` refusals are not measured there. They stay on round 1's review list.
- The registry covers static copy and the render check covers anything assembled in JSX.

**Q4**: **Accepted. The guard is narrower than in round 1, and its exemptions are fixed in advance.**
- **svg:** no `<svg>` outside `Icono.tsx`, with no exemptions. None is needed today.
- **Buttons:** a raw `<button>` in `screens/` or `shell/` may carry only a class from a closed list of non-action controls written into the guard:
  - `pildora`, `periodos__boton`, `gastos__mes-boton`;
  - the row classes `fila-gasto` and `ajustes__item--boton`;
  - `encabezado__titulo`;
  - `role="switch"` and `role="radio"` controls.

  Pills, period tabs, switches and radios are option choices, which are text-only. The month stepper and the rows keep their own markup. Every `boton*` class on a raw `<button>` or on an `InAppLink` fails the guard.
- **Where each kind goes:**
  - `boton`, `boton--suave`, `boton--destructivo` and submits go to `Boton` with `primary`, `secondary` or `destructive`. Submits and confirmation pairs pass `icon={null}`.
  - Every "Cerrar", `historial-fila__borrar` and the existing `boton-icono` go to `Boton variant="icon"` with `action="close"` or `action="delete"`, each keeping its exact current `ariaLabel`.
  - Links (`boton--enlace` / `boton-enlace`) and Deshacer go to `Boton variant="link"`.
  - The 17 action-styled `InAppLink`s go to a new `BotonLink` in `components/`. It shares `Boton`'s icon contract (`icon: NombreIcono | null` is required on action variants), so "Mandar catálogo" and the others cannot skip the rule.
- **Helper:** it goes in `web/src/test/iconAndTextRule.ts`.
  - It sits beside `test-setup.ts` under `src`, so `tsc --strict` and `oxlint` check it.
  - The Vite build bundles only what `main.tsx` reaches, so the helper never ships.
  - `tests/architecture/` is Python over `app/` and never sees `web/`.
  - A new real-tree vitest, beside `forbiddenApis.real.test.ts`, fails if a non-test module imports from `src/test/`.

**Q5**: **Accepted, cut finer.** Every brief lists its modules, counted with `ls`.

| Piece | Scope | Runs |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `ICON_NAMES`, both unions pinned by pytest, the 11 overrides deleted, emoji guard, README icon section | first |
| 2a | `Boton` contract and `BotonLink` (the 6 current `Boton` callers updated), `src/test/iconAndTextRule.ts` and its import guard, the ADR | after 1, parallel with 2b |
| 2b | `MasInfo`, the `Notice` `attention` prop, `copy/limits.ts` and the glob test | after 1, parallel with 2a |
| a1 | Shell, Más, Configuración's list | after both 2a and 2b |
| a2 | Business, Users, Appearance, Account, CambiarContrasena, CatalogMessage, ExpenseCategories groups | after both |
| b | IntegrationsGroup, Catalogo, PinPublish | after both |
| c1 | Variedades, VarietyPage with its sections, ArchivedVarieties, VarietyRemoval, PriceRows (Archivar/Restaurar) | after both |
| c2 | EntradaStock, EntriesList, WithdrawalForm, WithdrawalDetail | after both |

- **Why every area waits for both 2a and 2b:** each area needs 2a's contract and helper, and almost every area registers copy or sets `attention`, which needs 2b. Waiting for both costs one step and saves rebasing.
- **Area diffs:**
  - They are mechanical edits to existing modules and open no seam. The only seams are in 2a and 2b, and only 2a's needs an ADR.
  - `app.css`: each area edits only its own commented section. 2a owns `.boton*` and 2b owns `.mas-info` and the notice icon.

- **Order:** b and c1 go first among the areas, as the PM asked.

  - the registry entry is the same exported binding the screen renders;
  - after piece 1 no diff touches `Icono.tsx` or `componentes.ts`, and every subtask is green on its own.
- Added: no area diff touches `Boton.tsx` or `BotonLink.tsx` after 2a.
- Added: no non-test module imports from `src/test/`.
- Added: a raw `<button>` in `screens/` or `shell/` carries only a class from the guard's closed list, and no element outside `Boton` or `BotonLink` carries a `boton*` class.

**Where old and new shapes coexist:** raw buttons, action-styled `InAppLink`s and inline copy stay in an area until that area's subtask migrates them. The closing guard ends that state.

### Round 3: questions

### Round 3: GH-97

Rounds 1 and 2 are settled as you answered them. The inventory is useful: 11 call sites in 8 files, no svg outside `Icono.tsx`, and 17 action-styled `InAppLink`s. The plan still breaks in these places.

**Where the plan fails now.** Three concrete failures:
- `icon: NombreIcono | null` is an escape hatch. A developer can write `<Boton variant="primary" icon={null}>Mandar catálogo</Boton>`. It compiles, passes the helper, passes the guards and breaks the PRD. The text-only list is still decided by review, not by the code.
- Your mapping sends about 10 raw "Cerrar" buttons to `action="close"`, and the list includes `fila-entrada__volver`. A "Volver" control is back navigation. It is not in the PRD's icon-only list. Forcing it into `close` changes its role, or leaves it as an exception no guard sees.
- Piece 1 is "mechanical", but it edits `Icono.tsx` plus 8 other production modules, adds about 13 drawn icons, a pytest, an emoji guard and README edits. The standing question asks for a piece at or under about four production modules. This one has nine.

❓ **Q1** - **`icon={null}` is unchecked**: Round 2 says submits and confirmation pairs pass `icon={null}`. Nothing ties `null` to those two kinds. Any action can opt out of its icon by passing `null`, and the closed list of text-only controls then lives only in review. Where does the type or the test stop `null` on an ordinary action?
➡️ Make it a discriminated union on `Boton`. An action variant takes either `icon: NombreIcono`, or a `textOnly: 'submit' | 'answer'` that also requires `type="submit"` or a place inside `ConfirmDialog`. Drop the bare `null`. Then text-only is a named kind, and the type error appears at the call site. Add a case to `expectIconAndTextRule`: a control with visible text, no `svg` and no `type="submit"` fails unless it is a link, a Deshacer or an option control.

❓ **Q2** - **"Cerrar" and "Volver" are different controls**: About 10 raw "Cerrar" buttons are link-styled with visible text. The PRD makes close an icon-only action that keeps its exact accessible name. `fila-entrada__volver` and similar controls are back navigation, and `atras` is already a drawn icon. Which of the ~25 `boton--enlace` buttons are close, which are back, and which are plain links? Also, a control that shows the word "Cerrar" today becomes an icon with `aria-label="Cerrar"`. Tests that find it by visible text (`getByText('Cerrar')`) will break even though the accessible name is unchanged.
➡️ Add a one-time classification table to the brief of each area. Columns: control, current class, current visible text, target (close, back link, link). Close controls become `action="close"`. Back controls stay text-only links or take `atras` with text, as the PRD's rule for non-listed actions says. Change tests that find by text to find by role and name in the same diff as the control, not later.

❓ **Q3** - **Rendered length check meets data**: `expectIconAndTextRule` measures `role="status"`, `role="note"`, `.pie` and the notice classes. Some of these render API data: customer names, variety names, catalog text, amounts. A fixture with a 60-character customer name inside a 30-character notice fails the check for a reason no copy change can fix. And the check only runs in the states each test mounts, so an empty state never shown in a fixture is never measured.
➡️ Define the limits as applying to fixed copy, not to interpolated data. Measure the fixed part: render with a worst-case sample for each placeholder (`dd/mm`, `$ 999.999`, a 24-character name) and say in the rule doc which sample each uses. Keep the registry as the main check for empty states, and have each area add a test case that mounts every empty state it owns.

❓ **Q4** - **Fixed module list is a shared file**: The glob test asserts that the glob finds "a fixed list of known modules". That list is one file. Five or more parallel area branches each add a registry module and each edit that list, so they conflict at merge, or each branch has to rebase on the others. The same problem applies to the hard-coded list of refusals over 80 characters.
➡️ The fixed list pins only the modules that exist at the start of 2b, and 2b writes it once. New modules are found by the glob automatically and need no list edit. The over-80 refusal list becomes a constant inside each refusal's own module, such as `export const LONG_REFUSALS`. The test then merges them, and no area edits a shared test file.

❓ **Q5** - **Piece 1 is bigger than "mechanical"**: Piece 1 has `Icono.tsx`, 8 modules for the 11 overrides, about 13 new drawn names, `ICON_NAMES`, `test_design_icons.py`, the emoji guard and the README icon section. Who draws the paths: does the approved design in `docs/product/design-system/` already give them for stroke 1.6 at 22 px, or does a Developer invent 13 glyphs? How is "same family" checked?
➡️ Split piece 1. Piece 1a: `ICON_NAMES`, the pytest pin, the emoji guard, the README section and removal of the 11 overrides, with `tsc` green. Piece 1b: draw the new names, taking paths from the design where given, and review each one with `compare.sh` against the design on both themes. If the design gives no paths, say so in the brief and list which names need a decision from the Human. Run 1a and 1b in parallel, since 1b only adds entries to a record that 1a already types.

### Invariants proposed

- A control with visible text and no icon is allowed only as a link, a Deshacer, an option control, or a named text-only kind (`submit` or `answer`), and the type of `Boton` makes any other combination a compile error.

### Round 3: answers

### What I re-read for this round

- **The ten link-styled "Cerrar" buttons:** EntradaStock, NuevaVenta, ExpenseForm, CatalogAttributesForm, RenameForm, PriceForm, VarietyNew, ThresholdForm and WithdrawalForm, plus one more found in that group. All have `type="button"` and the visible word "Cerrar".
- **`fila-entrada__volver` (EntriesList.tsx:127):** it renders "‹ Variedades" or "‹ <variety>". It is back navigation, not close.
- **New icon names in the design:** none of the new names (`lapiz`, `candado`, `salirSesion`, `archivar`, `restaurar`, `probar`, `salidaStock`, `atencion`, `cerrar`, `compartir`, `info`) has a drawn path anywhere under `docs/product/design-system/`. They appear only as prose in the two ConfiguracionIntegraciones files.

**Q1**: **Accepted, with the union closed further.**
- **The props.** `Boton`'s props become a discriminated union:
  - `variant: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'destructive'` takes either `icon: NombreIcono` or `textOnly: 'submit'`. `textOnly: 'submit'` also requires `type: 'submit'` in the same member. Bare `null` is gone.
  - `variant: 'icon'` takes `action: IconOnlyAction` and `ariaLabel` (round 1, unchanged).
  - `variant: 'link'` and `variant: 'undo'` are text-only, and neither accepts `icon`. `link` is for `enlace` controls; `undo` is for Deshacer.
  - `variant: 'back'` is text-only. It renders "‹ <parent>" and accepts no `icon` (see Q2).
- **Confirmation answers.** Answer pairs are not a public kind. `ConfirmDialog` and `LeaveConfirm` render their answers through an internal, unexported `AnswerButton`, so no screen can mark an action as an "answer".
- **The result.** Text-only exists only as `submit`, `link`, `undo`, `back` or a dialog answer, and any other text-only action is a compile error at the call site.
- **`BotonLink`** carries the same union, minus `submit`.
- **The helper.** `expectIconAndTextRule` gains the proposed case. A control with visible text, no `svg` and no `type="submit"` fails unless it is one of:
  - the `boton--enlace` link class;
  - the undo or back class;
  - a class on the guard's closed list of option controls;
  - inside a `role="alertdialog"` or `role="dialog"` answer row.

**Q2**: **Accepted.**
- **Classification table.** Each area's brief carries a one-time table with columns control, file:line, current class, current visible text and target (close, back, link, icon + text action). The targets I already know:
  - all ten "Cerrar" buttons become `action="close"`, keeping `ariaLabel="Cerrar"`;
  - `fila-entrada__volver` becomes `variant="back"`. The PRD settles that back stays "‹ <parent>" and is not on the icon-only list, so it is not `close` and it gets no `atras` icon;
  - every remaining `boton--enlace` / `boton-enlace` is classified per area, as a link or as an action.
- **Icon-only or icon + text for Cerrar.** The PRD *allows* icon-only for Cerrar but does not require it. Close becomes icon-only only where the approved design draws it that way. Otherwise the button keeps its word and gets the `cerrar` icon beside it, through a closed `action` icon on an icon + text `Boton`.
- **Tests that find the word.** Where Cerrar does lose its visible word, the area's tests change from `getByText('Cerrar')` to `getByRole('button', { name: 'Cerrar' })` in the same diff as the control. The PR lists those edits next to the design decision. I am asking PM whether that edit counts as the "deliberately shortened copy" exception of criterion 5.

**Q3**: **Accepted.**
- **Fixed copy, not data.** The limits apply to fixed copy only.
- **Worst-case samples.** `copy/limits.ts` exports `SAMPLES` and the rule doc names each one:
  - date `28/09`;
  - amount `$ 999.999` (and `−$ 999.999` for a negative);
  - grams `999,99 g`;
  - a name of 24 characters;
  - a count of `99`.
- **The registry is the primary check.** Every copy function is registered and called with `SAMPLES`. Empty states are covered there whether or not a fixture mounts them.
- **The rendered check** in the helper measures only elements that carry `data-copy`. Screens put that attribute on fixed-copy elements: help lines, notices, empty-state title and line. Copy that takes a placeholder is measured only when the test renders it with `SAMPLES`; the helper takes those values as an argument. A real customer name in an unrelated fixture never fails it.
- **Empty states.** Each area adds one test case that mounts every empty state it owns and runs the helper over it.

**Q4**: **Accepted.**
- **Known modules.** 2b writes the list of known copy modules once, with the modules that exist when 2b starts. Later modules are found by the glob alone, and no area edits that list.
- **Long refusals.** Each copy or refusal module exports its own `LONG_REFUSALS: readonly string[]`: the exported constants that are knowingly over 80 characters. The test merges them through the same glob and fails on any refusal over 80 that is not in its own module's list.
- No area edits a shared test file.

**Q5**: **Accepted in part.** I split piece 1, but 1a and 1b cannot run in parallel. Both edit `Icono.tsx` (`ICON_NAMES` and `TRAZOS`), and 2a's action map needs `cerrar`, `lapiz` and `compartir` to exist.

- **1a.**
  - It covers `ICON_NAMES`, deleting the `size`/`strokeWidth` props with their 11 call sites in 8 files, the emoji guard and the README icon section.
  - Its pytest pin compares `componentes.ts` against the names that already exist today: the 13 design names plus `agregar`, `stock`, `whatsapp` and `telegram`.
  - It touches 9 production modules, but 8 of them lose only an argument. That cannot be split further without leaving `tsc` red between diffs, and nothing in it is a seam.
- **1b.**
  - It draws the new names into `Icono.tsx` and `componentes.ts` together.
  - It extends `test_design_icons.py` with a family check. `Icono`'s `<svg>` alone sets the viewBox, the 22 px size, `stroke-width="1.6"`, `stroke="currentColor"` and `fill="none"`. So the test fails if any `TRAZOS` entry carries its own `stroke-width`, a fill other than `none`, a `transform`, or a nested `<svg>`.
  - Each glyph is judged by eye with `compare.sh`, in both themes.
- **Who draws.** No Developer invents a glyph. The design in the repository gives no path for any of the new names. 1b takes its paths from the UI design step's icon sheet (Human decision 3 has the design name every icon). The 1b brief lists the names it needs, and 1b does not start until the design delivers their paths. If a name arrives without a path, it is sent back to UI design, not to PM.
- **Order.** 1a, then 1b and 2b in parallel (2b's `info` and `atencion` come from 1b, so 2b waits for 1b's draw of those two names). Then 2a, after 1b. Then the areas, then the closing piece.

**Invariants**:
- Accepted as proposed.
- The text-only kinds are exactly `submit`, `link`, `undo`, `back` and the internal dialog answer.
- Added: every `TRAZOS` entry inherits stroke, size and fill from `Icono`'s `<svg>`, and carries none of its own.
- Added: a length limit is measured on fixed copy with `SAMPLES`, never on fixture data.

### Answers to Tech Lead (round 3)

**Q1: "Cerrar" turned into an icon-only control, and the tests that find it by visible text.**

Yes, that edit counts as the criterion 5 exception and must be listed in the PR. It works under these conditions:

- **One form everywhere.** Close is on the icon-only list, and the design uses it that way: every form and sheet closes with the same icon-only control. That is the `cerrar` icon in a 44 px target, with the accessible name "Cerrar".
  - No form shows the word while another shows the icon. Same meaning, same icon.
  - "Cerrar, never Volver" (navigation.md) still holds, now through the accessible name.
- **The test edit is the narrowest possible.** A test that found the control by its visible text ("Cerrar") now finds it by role and accessible name ("button", name "Cerrar"). Nothing else in that test changes:
  - no assertion is dropped or loosened;
  - the "never Volver" check stays, against the accessible name.
- **Each edit is listed in the PR**, with the screen and the control. A test that has to change in any other way means the implementation is wrong. It does not mean the test should be edited.
- **The same applies to the other icon-only actions** that show a word today: Editar, Borrar…, Buscar, and Compartir where it is not the main action. The accessible name stays the exact current word.

I have written this into `icon-and-text-rule.md` as a second, named exception in criterion 5. Following the Human's Decision 1, I also added that every icon is specified by its `NombreIcono` name, and that names `Icono.tsx` already draws are reused before any new name is added.

### Closing summary: GH-97 (round 4 of 4)

This is the last round, so I close with what I have. The plan has held up under three rounds. I still name three ways it can fail, listed under known risks.

**Decided**
- **Icon names.**
  - `Icono.tsx` exports `ICON_NAMES`, and `NombreIcono` derives from it.

  - `size` and `strokeWidth` leave `IconoProps`. The 11 call sites in 8 files go in the same diff, so the tree is never red.
  - An emoji guard covers `web/src`.
- **One seam for controls.**
  - `Boton` is a discriminated union: `icon`, `submit`, `link`, `undo`, `back`, and an icon-only variant with a closed action map and a required `ariaLabel`.
  - `BotonLink` shares that contract for action-styled links.
  - Dialog answers go through an unexported `AnswerButton`.
  - There is no bare `icon={null}`.
  - Back stays a "‹ <parent>" text control and is not `close`.
- **Close.** Per the PM's answer, Cerrar is icon-only everywhere, with the `cerrar` icon, a 44px target and the accessible name "Cerrar". The "Cerrar keeps its word with a `cerrar` icon" alternative from my round 3 answer is withdrawn.
  - Tests that find a control by visible text move to role and name, with no other change.
  - Every such edit is listed in the PR.
  - The same applies to Editar, Borrar…, Buscar and secondary Compartir.
- **More info.**
  - `MasInfo` holds local state only, with no history entry, no storage and no form-dirty flag.
  - It uses `--tinta2` for the "i" and its text.
  - The test covers closed on remount, unchanged storage and `history.length`, and closing a dirty form without a leave prompt.
- **Copy limits.**
  - Limits apply to fixed copy, measured with the `SAMPLES` worst cases.
  - A registry per copy module (`messages.ts` or `*Messages.ts`) is collected by a case-safe glob.
  - 2b writes the known-module list once, so areas never edit a shared test file.
  - Each module owns its `LONG_REFUSALS`.
  - A rendered check covers elements marked `data-copy`.
- **Guards.**
  - There is no `<svg>` outside `Icono.tsx`.
  - A raw `<button>` in `screens/` or `shell/` carries only a class from a closed list of option controls.
  - No element outside `Boton` or `BotonLink` carries a `boton*` class.
  - The "every name used" check leaves out `Icono.tsx` and `Boton.tsx`. It also requires a caller under `screens/` or `shell/` for each icon-only action, for `MasInfo` and for `attention`.
  - The helper lives in `web/src/test/`, and no non-test module may import it.
- **Subtasks.**
  1. 1a: names, pin, emoji guard, README, the 11 overrides.
  2. 1b: the new glyphs, once their paths exist.
  3. 2b: `MasInfo`, `attention` and the copy limits.
  4. 2a: the `Boton` and `BotonLink` contract, the helper and the ADR.
  5. The areas a1, a2, b, c1, c2, c3, d1, d2, e1 and e2, in parallel, with b and c1 first.
  6. The closing piece.
- **ADR.** One ADR, written in 2a, for `Boton`'s closed action map and its text-only kinds. The closed lists themselves live in `icon-and-text-rule.md`.

**Known risks**
1. **Glyphs have no source.**

2. **Piece 1a is nine production modules.** It is eight one-argument deletions plus `Icono.tsx`. It stays one diff because splitting it leaves `tsc` red. A reviewer should check that the eight edits are only deletions.

## Tech Lead

_the delivery tool was called 2 times over this run before it delivered_

### Approach

This is a front-only change: presentation and copy. It adds no route, no API field, no table and nothing to browser storage. CODING_STANDARDS §8 has nothing to declare: no input reaches more than it did before, and no cost grows with data.

Four seams carry the work, and every screen then goes through them.

1. **One icon set, checked against the design.**
   - `web/src/components/Icono.tsx` exports `ICON_NAMES` as a `const` array, and `NombreIcono` is derived from it.
   - `docs/product/design-system/componentes.ts` keeps its own literal union.
   - `tests/test_design_icons.py` pins the two lists to each other, in both directions. It is a pytest because Vite cannot read `docs/` (ADR-0029).
   - `size` and `strokeWidth` leave `IconoProps`, so every icon is drawn at 22 px with stroke 1.6.
   - No `TRAZOS` entry may set its own stroke, fill or transform, or nest an `<svg>`.
2. **One control seam.**
   - `Boton` becomes a discriminated union. `BotonLink` shares that union for action-styled in-app links.
   - An icon-only control picks from a closed action map and requires an `ariaLabel`: delete→`tacho`, search→`buscar`, close→`cerrar`, edit→`lapiz`, share→`compartir`.
   - Action variants require an icon.
   - Text-only exists only as `submit`, `link`, `undo` or `back`, plus confirmation answers. `ConfirmDialog` and `LeaveConfirm` draw those answers through an unexported `AnswerButton`.
   - An icon next to a word is always `aria-hidden`, so accessible names never move.
   - This rule gets an ADR, written in 2a.
3. **More info and the attention icon.**
   - `MasInfo` keeps its open state in a local `useState` only. Nothing goes to history (ADR-0027) or storage (ADR-0026), and opening it never counts as an unsaved change.
   - The "i" and its expanded text use `--tinta2`, a general text token in ADR-0029's table, so no new contrast row is needed.
   - The attention icon is a prop of the shared `Notice` component, drawn in `currentColor`. That gives "the same icon everywhere" a single author.
4. **Copy limits on fixed copy.**
   - Placeholders are measured with `SAMPLES`, the worst cases.
   - `web/src/copy/limits.ts` holds `LIMITS`, `CopyKind` and `SAMPLES`.
   - Each copy module (`messages.ts` or `*Messages.ts`) exports a `LIMITED_COPY` registry and its own `LONG_REFUSALS`.
   - A glob test collects those modules, and a rendered check measures the elements marked `data-copy`.

### Decisions settled in the interview

- **Close.** Close is icon-only everywhere: the `cerrar` icon in a 44 px target, with the accessible name "Cerrar" (PM, round 3).
  - A test that found such a control by its visible word now finds it by role and name, and nothing else in that test changes. Each such edit is listed in the PR.
  - The same applies to Editar, Borrar…, Buscar and a secondary Compartir.
- **Back.** Back ("‹ <parent>", for example `fila-entrada__volver`) is `variant="back"`: text only, never `close`.
- **The test helper.** `expectIconAndTextRule` lives in `web/src/test/`. No non-test module may import it, and a real-tree guard enforces that.
- **The closing guards:**
  - no `<svg>` outside `Icono.tsx`;
  - a raw `<button>` in `screens/` or `shell/` carries only a class from a closed list of option and row controls;
  - no element outside `Boton` or `BotonLink` carries a `boton*` class;
  - every name in `ICON_NAMES` is used by a screen. A name in `Boton`'s map counts only when a screen passes its `action`. `info` and `atencion` count only when `MasInfo` and `attention` have a caller.

### Where old and new shapes coexist

Raw `<button>`s, action-styled `InAppLink`s and inline copy stay in each area until that area's subtask migrates them. The closing subtask's guards end that state, and after that nothing is exempted.

`stock`, `whatsapp` and `telegram` stay only if a screen still uses them at the end. Otherwise the closing subtask removes them from both lists.

### Sequencing

1. 1a.
2. 1b.
3. 2a and 2b, in parallel.
4. The seven areas, in parallel. Integraciones/catalog (b) and Variedades (c1) start first.
5. Closing.

File ownership:
- After 1b, no other subtask edits `Icono.tsx` or `componentes.ts`.
- After 2a, no area edits `Boton.tsx` or `BotonLink.tsx`.
- Each area edits only its own commented sections of `app.css`.
- 2a owns the `.boton*` classes.
- 2b owns `.mas-info` and the notice icon.

### Known risks

1. **The new glyphs have no source yet.** No path for these names exists under `docs/product/design-system/`: `lapiz`, `cerrar`, `info`, `compartir`, `candado`, `salirSesion`, `archivar`, `restaurar`, `probar`, `salidaStock`, `atencion`.
   - 1b takes them from the GH-97 design (Human decision 3).
   - A Developer never invents a glyph. If a path is missing, 1b stops and reports back to UI design.
2. **1a touches nine production modules.** Eight of those edits only delete an argument. That is the price of never leaving `tsc` red, and the reviewer checks that they are deletions only.
3. **Visual judgment stays by eye.** For each area's screens, `compare.sh` runs before and after, on phone and computer, in both themes. The comparisons and the list of shortened or moved copy are attached to the PR.

#### 2a: Boton and BotonLink carry the icon and text rule in their types; the shared test helper; ADR

Make the closed lists of the rule (docs/product/icon-and-text-rule.md) a type.

- `web/src/components/Boton.tsx` props become a discriminated union:
  - `variant: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'destructive'` with either `icon: NombreIcono` (icon + text) or `textOnly: 'submit'` together with `type: 'submit'`. No bare `null`.
  - `variant: 'icon'` with `action: IconOnlyAction` (`'delete' | 'search' | 'close' | 'edit' | 'share'`) and a required `ariaLabel: string`; the icon comes from a closed `Record<IconOnlyAction, NombreIcono>` (delete→`tacho`, search→`buscar`, close→`cerrar`, edit→`lapiz`, share→`compartir`); `.boton-icono` gives a 44 px target.
  - `variant: 'link'` (enlace), `'undo'` (Deshacer) and `'back'` ("‹ <parent>") are text only and accept no icon. `row` stays for tappable list rows.
  - Any icon rendered next to visible text is `aria-hidden`; the accessible name is the word.

- New `web/src/test/iconAndTextRule.ts`: `expectIconAndTextRule(container, samples?)` asserting: every button and link has an accessible name; every svg inside a control with visible text is `aria-hidden`; every control with no visible text is `.boton-icono`; a control with visible text, no svg and no `type="submit"` is a link/undo/back class, an option control from the closed list, or an answer inside `role="dialog"`/`"alertdialog"`; no two entries inside an element marked `data-menu` share an icon; every `data-copy` element fits its limit (reading 2b's `LIMITS`; whichever of 2a/2b merges second wires that check).
- Real-tree vitest: no non-test module imports from `src/test/`.
- ADR (next number): Boton's closed action map, the required icon on action variants, the text-only kinds, and that back is not close.

Acceptance: `tsc` rejects `icon` on link/undo/back, `textOnly` without `type='submit'`, and an icon-only `Boton` without `ariaLabel` (type-level tests); existing suites pass unchanged.

#### Closing: the guards that end the old shapes, and the whole-app checks

After every area has merged:
- Real-tree vitest guards: no `<svg>` outside `Icono.tsx`; a raw `<button>` in `screens/` or `shell/` carries only a class from the closed list (`pildora`, `periodos__boton`, `gastos__mes-boton`, `fila-gasto`, `ajustes__item--boton`, `encabezado__titulo`, or `role="switch"`/`"radio"`), written into the guard; no element outside `Boton` or `BotonLink` carries a `boton*` class.
- "Every name used": each name in `ICON_NAMES` appears in a non-test file under `screens/`, `shell/` or `components/` other than `Icono.tsx` and `Boton.tsx`; a name in `Boton`'s action map counts only if some screen passes that `action`; `info` and `atencion` count only if `MasInfo` and `attention` have a caller under `screens/` or `shell/`. Remove from both `Icono.tsx` and `componentes.ts` any name left unused (e.g. `stock`, `whatsapp`, `telegram`), keeping `tests/test_design_icons.py` green.
- Playwright: the bar fits at 320 px with text at 130 % and no icon or label is clipped.
- The PR list: every shortened or moved text and where its long form went (0 silent deletions), every test edit allowed by criterion 5 with its screen and control, and every area's comparisons attached.
Acceptance: every guard green with no exemption beyond the closed list.

Seams: `real-tree guards (vitest)`, `tests/test_design_icons.py`, `Playwright bar fit at 320 px / 130 %`.

Depends on: `area-shell-settings`, `area-integrations-catalog`, `area-varieties`, `area-stock-forms`, `area-sales`, `area-customers`, `area-home-expenses-states`.
