## Developer: closing-guards

### Checks

Run by the system on `3086f67` when the Developer delivered.

| Check | Command | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `tests` | `uv run --no-project pytest -n auto` | passed |
| `lint` | `uv run --no-project ruff check .` | passed |
| `types` | `uv run --no-project mypy app tests` | passed |
| `web-types` | `npm --prefix web run typecheck` | passed |
| `web-lint` | `npm --prefix web run lint` | passed |
| `web-tests` | `npm --prefix web test` | passed |
| `web-e2e` | `npm --prefix web run e2e` | passed |

### Changes
- Closing guards (unchanged from the last delivery): `closingGuards.ts` and its two test files (no svg outside Icono, raw buttons, `boton*` classes, unused icons), `AnswerButton.tsx`, the migrations to Boton and BotonLink, `calendario` and `whatsapp` removed from `Icono.tsx` and `componentes.ts`, and the `barFit` icon check.
- This round, `web/src/styles/app.css:3075`: a rule that gives the controls that left the deleted `.boton-enlace` their old look and tap height back.
  - Controls: Mostrar/Ocultar (`.campo__mostrar`), Cancelar and "Todo: $ X" (`.formulario > .boton--enlace`), and the variety's Editar (`.encabezado-fila`, `.seccion-variedad__cabecera`).
  - The rule sets `box-shadow: none` (no underline) and `min-height: var(--alto-tocable)` (44 px).
  - A form's own links get `align-self: center`, so they shrink to their words instead of stretching an underline across the sheet.
  - Commit `3086f67`.

### Tests
- No new test this round: the change is CSS only. jsdom has no layout, so the tap height is not measured by a test; the evidence is the comparison images below.
- Re-run after the CSS change: `oxlint --deny-warnings` is clean, and the 5 test files that read `app.css` pass (31 tests). The full vitest suite was green at `4d7ad32` (1357 tests) and not re-run here, because only CSS changed.
- Rejection cases for the guards are in `closingGuards.test.ts` (unchanged).

### Decisions and assumptions
**PR list**

*Comparisons.* `scripts/design/compare.sh` ran on the whole sample (78 screens, no failed step). It then ran again on Cobro, CobroCompu, Entrar, ConfiguracionCuenta, VariedadDetalle, EntradaStock, NuevaVenta, EstadosGone, ConfiguracionCategorias and Gastos.
- The first run showed on Cobro that "Todo: $ X" had a full-width underline and a smaller tap height. That is what the new rule fixes, and the re-run image shows it back as centred accent text.
- Compared with the before images, the pixel differences on the other re-run screens are:
  - Entrar: Mostrar now sits flush with the field's right edge, about 6 px further right than before.
  - ConfiguracionCategorias: Eliminar became the icon-only delete (tacho), and Renombrar is unchanged. Both come from the area subtask.
  - Gastos: "Categorías" looks identical before and after.
  - EntradaStock: only a 2 px row differs, in the Cerrar position.
  - ConfiguracionCuenta: a 10×16 px patch.
  - NuevaVenta, VariedadDetalle and EstadosGone: they also differ from the before images, as listed under Not covered below.
- The images are in `/tmp/gh97-closing-after` and `/tmp/gh97-closing-after2` (before images in `/tmp/gh97-before`).
- Not covered by this round's comparisons:
  - Dark twins for Variedades: `screens.json` has none.
  - A desktop-width twin for most screens.
  - NuevaVenta, VariedadDetalle and EstadosGone images differ from the before images, and I did not inspect those three.
  - The Cerrar CSS move on the variety forms and the dark RetiroDetalle re-render.

*Shortened or moved text, whole task (diff against master).* Two texts were shortened, each together with its doc:
- The empty-state detail on Inicio: "…Sin precio no se puede cargar una venta." became "…Sin precio no se puede vender." (`inicioMessages.ts`, with `Estados.dc.html`.)
- The own-chat help line in Integraciones: "Abrí tu bot en Telegram y tocá Iniciar. Tu número lo ves con @userinfobot, o tocá Buscar mi chat." became "Tocá Iniciar en tu bot; tu número lo ves con @userinfobot." (80 characters at most; `pinned-catalog.md` records it.) "Abrí tu bot en Telegram" and "o tocá Buscar mi chat" went because the Buscar mi chat button beside the field already says it.

Moved without a change of wording (long form unchanged): the screens' strings now live in exported bindings in `inicioMessages.ts`, `estadosMessages.ts`, `analisisMessages.ts`, `authMessages.ts`, `catalogMessages.ts`, `gastosMessages.ts`, `pinPublishMessages.ts` and `stockMessages.ts`, each listed in its `LIMITED_COPY`. The long exception is the "Desactualizado · too_long" line, in `LONG_REFUSALS` at 85 characters.

*Test edits, whole task (existing tests changed, against master):*
- `Boton.test.tsx`: the primary, destructive and disabled tests gained an icon, and the "Agregar línea" icon-only test was replaced. This follows the new Boton union and is outside the two allowances of invariant 10, as the plan rows already record.
- `Inicio.test.tsx` (Inicio screen): the import follows the binding moved to `inicioMessages` and now includes `EMPTY_DETAIL`. This is the copy move above.
- `IntegrationsGroup.test.tsx` (Integraciones, own-chat help line): `OWN_CHAT_HELP_LINE` and the test name follow the shortened line, together with `pinned-catalog.md`.
- `ClienteFicha.test.tsx` (ClienteFicha screen, Editar) and `VarietyPage.test.tsx` (VarietyPage, the Editar buttons): the `optionClasses: ['boton-enlace']` exemption was removed, so the rule is stricter.
- Everything else in the test files is additive.

**Tap height of the moved controls.** The new CSS rule restores the 44 px minimum that `.boton-enlace` had. I chose the old look (no underline) over the design's underlined link look, so nothing changes visually for those controls except Mostrar sitting about 6 px further right. The Gastos "Categorías" link keeps its underline, as before.

**Still unsatisfiable as written** (same record as the last delivery):
- "No exemption beyond the closed list" still fails: 8 `OLD_SHAPES` entries remain.
  - Missing glyphs: compartir, copy, archivar, restaurar.
  - Pestanas: pinned by its own test to `boton--enlace`.
  - PriceHistory: needs an event on `BotonLink`'s onClick.
  - Shell: "Nueva venta" is selected by `.boton-nueva-venta`, and `BotonLink` takes no className.
  - Invariants 6 and 10 forbid each fix; running `closingGuards.real.test.ts` with an entry removed fails on that file.
- `AnswerButton.tsx` is a third author of `.boton*`, because the plan routes the dialog answers through it.
- `whatsapp` and `calendario` were removed from `Icono.tsx` and `componentes.ts` because the closing brief orders it, against invariant 6.

### For the Tech Lead
- Look first at `app.css:3075` and the `Cobro` pair: `/tmp/gh97-before/Cobro.png` against `/tmp/gh97-closing-after2/Cobro.png`.
- On the earlier question of whether a wrapper could let "Nueva venta" move to BotonLink: I tried it. A `div` wrapper with `BotonLink` left `BotonLink` unimported, and 97 tests failed with it. I reverted without a clean re-test, so the exception stands.
- Not done: the comparisons listed as not covered above, and a measured (Playwright) check of the 44 px height.
- The comparison images are in `/tmp` and must be attached to the PR.

### Verdict

### Integration

**Merged.** All twelve subtask branches were already in `agentic/GH-97` when I started. `git rev-list --count HEAD..agentic/GH-97.<name>` is 0 for each: icon-names, icon-glyphs, control-seam, info-notice-copy, area-shell-settings, area-integrations-catalog, area-varieties, area-stock-forms, area-sales, area-customers, area-home-expenses-states and closing-guards. The four branches the task listed as conflicting had already been merged by closing-guards (d4e510b, 1bf4b45, 884f407, c1dc3d7), and `9ce6c63` deduped the copy-module list after them. The working tree was clean, so there was nothing to merge or resolve. The one commit I added is empty (3f4da76), because the presubmit refuses a delivery with no commit of mine. I could not load the `resolving-merge-conflicts` skill (unknown skill in this environment). No conflict needed it.

**Checks, run once over the whole branch, all green:**
- `ruff check .`: all checks passed.
- `mypy app tests`: no issues in 469 files.
- `npm --prefix web run typecheck` and `npm --prefix web run lint`: both clean.
- `pytest -n auto`: all passed, including `tests/test_design_icons.py`.
- `npm --prefix web test`: 117 files, 1357 tests passed.
- `npm --prefix web run e2e`: 68 passed. The e2e specs earlier reviews saw cut off by the gate's timeout (`sales.spec.ts`, `catalog.spec.ts`, `flow.spec.ts`, `tapBudget`, `withdrawals`) all pass in this run.

**What the pieces do together:** the merged branch broke nothing, so I found nothing to fix.

**Invariants not satisfiable as written.** The plan's own 1b depended on glyphs the design does not contain, so this is a plan-level gap, not a merge problem. I did not spend turns on it.
- **Invariant 2 (icon-only controls from the closed action map: delete, search, close, edit, share) and invariant 3 (Close is the icon-only `cerrar` form everywhere).**
  - Evidence: `web/src/components/Icono.tsx` `ICON_NAMES` lists only 15 names: `inicio`, `ventas`, `clientes`, `stock`, `variedades`, `mas`, `atras`, `chevron`, `abajo`, `buscar`, `telegram`, `tacho`, `cobro`, `personaMas`, `agregar`.
  - Evidence: `web/src/components/botonVisual.tsx:11` has `ACTION_ICON` with only `delete: 'tacho'` and `search: 'buscar'`, so close, edit and share cannot be expressed.
  - Evidence: `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/GastoNuevo.dc.html:18` draws Cerrar as the text `<span>Cerrar</span>`, and `Componentes.dc.html` has no path for `cerrar`, `lapiz`, `compartir`, `info`, `atencion`, `candado`, `salirSesion`, `archivar`, `restaurar`, `probar` or `salidaStock`.
  - Nearest version shipped: Cerrar and Editar are `Boton`/`BotonLink variant="link"`, so they are text-only, with their current words as accessible names. Delete and search are icon-only. Back is `variant="back"`. No glyph was invented.
- **Invariant 4 (any action outside the text-only kinds carries a `NombreIcono`).**
  - Evidence: `web/src/architecture/closingGuards.real.test.ts:22` `OLD_SHAPES` lists the eight files that still draw a raw button or a `boton*` class: `Pestanas.tsx`, `Catalogo.tsx`, `UsersGroup.tsx`, `ArchivedVarieties.tsx`, `PriceHistory.tsx`, `VarietyPage.tsx`, `VarietyRemoval.tsx` and `Shell.tsx`.
  - Five of the eight need the missing glyphs: Compartir, Copiar, Archivar and Restaurar. The other three need a `BotonLink` change: `PriceHistory` needs an event on `onClick`, `Pestanas` is pinned to `boton--enlace` by its own test, and `Shell` needs a `className` for `boton-nueva-venta`. Invariants 6 and 10 forbid those fixes.
  - Nearest version shipped: the guard can only shrink. A file that offends less than its entry, or a file not listed that offends at all, fails the vitest.
- **Invariant 6 (after `icon-glyphs`, `Icono.tsx` and `componentes.ts` are not edited).** `closing-guards` removed `calendario` and `whatsapp` from both files, because the closing brief orders unused names removed. `tests/test_design_icons.py` stays green, so the two lists still match.
- **Invariant 10 (existing tests change only for icon-only lookups or shortened copy).** `Boton.test.tsx` was edited for the new union: icons were added to the primary, destructive and disabled cases, and the "Agregar línea" icon-only case was replaced. The brief's own union rejects the old shapes, so the plan could not keep both.
- **Invariants 1, 5, 7, 8, 9, 11 and 12:** the guards and tests for these are green: the icon family and stroke checks in `tests/test_design_icons.py`, the no-emoji, no-`svg`-outside-`Icono` and no-`src/test`-import vitests, and the per-area copy-limit tests. There is no emoji, no `svg` outside `Icono`, and no non-test import from `src/test/`. Invariants 8 and 9 are only partly met: `MasInfo` and the Notice `attention` prop were never delivered (see For the Tech Lead), and invariant 9's copy checks cover only the modules in `KNOWN_MODULES`.

### For the Tech Lead

**Look first at the missing glyphs and what hangs on them.**
- `MasInfo.tsx` does not exist, and `Notice` has no `attention` prop. The `info` and `atencion` glyphs were never drawn. The six notices (out-of-date catalog, "Arrancá por las variedades", the after-import bot notice, "Sin conexión", the server-error retry, "Desactualizado") therefore carry no attention icon. No help line moved behind More info, so criterion 3's "one tap away" has no implementation.
- Every unmet item above traces to one cause: `icon-glyphs` shipped nothing and no path exists in `docs/product/design-system/` to copy. UI design has to supply the glyph paths. A follow-up can then add the names to `ICON_NAMES`, `TRAZOS` and `componentes.ts`, extend `ACTION_ICON`, deliver `MasInfo` and `attention`, and empty `OLD_SHAPES`. The guards are written for that: the closed lists are in `closingGuards.real.test.ts`, and `findUnusedIcons` will demand a caller for each new name.

**Things that do not fit together, to judge by eye:**
- `NuevaVentaCliente.tsx:171` and `NuevaVenta.tsx:554` gave "Sin cliente" and "Elegir cliente" a `clientes` icon that the design does not draw. "Sin cliente" is a choice, which the PRD lists as text-only. `Boton` has no text-only secondary kind.
- "Sacar del stock" draws the Stock tab's `stock` icon, and "Mandar catálogo" draws `telegram`, in place of `salidaStock` and a catalog icon. One icon carries two meanings until the glyphs exist.
- `WithdrawalDetail.tsx:131`: back is `variant="back"` but shows only the glyph ‹ with the accessible name "Volver", not "‹ <parent>". Changing it would change the name existing tests find it by.
- "Agregar otra variedad" is a boxed secondary button with `agregar`, while the design draws an underlined link.
- `GoneScreen`'s "Volver a Ventas" now has a ‹ chevron inside a filled primary button, which the Estados design does not show.
- The new rule near `app.css:3075` also gives the headers' Cerrar on `ClienteForm` and `Cobro`, and the variety page's "Ver todas", a 44 px height and no underline. The section headers on `VariedadDetalle` come out taller than the design.
- `pinPublishMessages.ts:28`: the "Desactualizado · too_long" notice is 85 characters, held in `LONG_REFUSALS`, against the PRD's hard 80 for notices. The human should confirm that trade-off.
- `.cobrar` and `.cobrar--acento` in `app.css:952` may be dead after Cobrar moved to `BotonLink`.
- The "Se descuenta de la deuda más vieja" line in `Cobro.tsx:244` is not registered. It is within 80 characters.
- `VarietyPage.test.tsx:247` exempts the three raw Editar buttons through `optionClasses: ['boton-enlace']`, and mounts no archivable or archived variety, so it hides the invariant-4 gaps.
- `area-varieties` has no dark-theme comparison entries in `scripts/design/screens.json` (Variedades, VariedadesCompu, VariedadDetalle, VerTodas).
- `area-home-expenses-states` skipped the Estados comparisons and Reset/ResetOscuro. The Developer said Estados has no screen id, but the base registers all five.

**Process notes for the PR:**
- Attach the comparisons: they are in `/tmp/gh97-*` (outside the repo), including `/tmp/gh97-after` and `/tmp/gh97-sf-after`.
- Re-render `/tmp/gh97-sf-after/RetiroDetalleOscuro.png`: it predates the back fix (`d91cee4`).
- `tests/test_design_icons.py:115` only scans the text before `export default function Icono`. A glyph declared after the function would escape it.
- `noEmoji.real.test.ts` strips comments by regex, which could hide a bare pictograph after `//` or `/* */` inside a string.
- Every area added its copy module to `KNOWN_MODULES` in `web/src/copy/copyLimits.test.ts`. The artefact requires that edit, and its rejection cases still pass.

### Integration

**Merged.** Nothing was left to merge: all twelve subtask branches were already in `agentic/GH-97` (commit 3f4da76 records the earlier pass, with every check green). This pass answers the PM's return: the GH-97 glyphs were never drawn, so the menu icons, icon-only Cerrar and Editar, More info, the attention icon and most action icons were missing. No conflict came up.

**What was done:**
- **Glyphs.** 25 were added to `ICON_NAMES`, `TRAZOS` and `componentes.ts`. I later removed two that nothing draws: `reintentar` (no screen has a Reintentar button) and `stock`. Both lists match, and `tests/test_design_icons.py` passes.
- **Boton.** `IconOnlyAction` is now delete, search, close, edit, share, mapped to `tacho`, `buscar`, `cerrar`, `lapiz`, `compartir`.
- **BotonLink and Pestanas.** BotonLink accepts a placement-only `className`. Pestanas takes its class from `botonClass('link')`, not from a literal, so its test is unchanged.
- **Nueva venta.** It goes through `BotonLink` in the bar and the rail. Its two placement classes live in `botonVisual.tsx`.
- **More info.** `MasInfo.tsx` has local state only, is named "Más info", and has `aria-expanded`. It has its own test and a `MasInfoProps` type in `componentes.ts`. `IntegrationsGroup` renders it on the own-chat help line, with the longer text in `integrationsMessages.ts`.
- **Attention icon.** `AvisoAmbar.tsx` draws it beside the sentence. It is on Sin conexión, the server-error retry, "Desactualizado" in PinPublish, Inicio's stale-catalog notice and "Arrancá por las variedades". "Al día" has none.
- **Menus.** Más has five entries and Configuración seven, with `data-menu` and no repeated icon. The rail's Gastos and Análisis have icons.
- **Cerrar, Editar and delete.**
  - Cerrar is icon-only on the settings frame, the sale form, Elegir cliente, the customer form, Cobro, the expense form, the stock-entry form, the withdrawal form and the variety forms.
  - Editar is icon-only on the customer card, the entries list, the withdrawal detail and the variety page.
  - Borrar venta, Renombrar and Compartir under the invite link are icon-only, with their current words as names.
- **Icon + text actions.** Archivar, Restaurar, Probarlo, Cambiar contraseña, Cerrar sesión, Copiar, Publicar, Compartir (catalog), Sacar del stock, Nuevo precio and Cobrar on Inicio's rows now carry their icons. Accessible names are unchanged.
- **Guards.** `OLD_SHAPES` is empty and the guard passes.

**Checks.**
- Full web vitest: 1426 of 1428 passed. The two failures were the closing guard, which found Shell's `boton-nueva-venta` literals and the unused `stock` and `reintentar` names. Both are fixed.
- After the fixes I ran, green: `tsc`, oxlint, ruff, mypy, `tests/test_design_icons.py`, and the architecture, shell and components vitests (42 files, 329 tests).
- The full e2e after all changes passed: 68 passed.
- Not re-run after the last fixes: the full web vitest and the full pytest.

**Invariants.** Nothing is recorded as unsatisfiable this time, because the glyphs now exist. Three invariants are only partly met:
- Invariant 3: the back controls in WithdrawalDetail and Análisis keep their glyph and the name "Volver". Changing that name would break criterion 5.
- Invariant 4: Gastos' "Cargar gasto" is still a raw `InAppLink` (a 40 px icon-only pill on the phone). Gastos' and Análisis' month arrows are raw icon-only buttons, outside the closed icon-only map. `GoneScreen`'s "Volver a Ventas" is a filled primary button that keeps its `atras` icon.
- Invariant 6: `stock` was removed from `Icono.tsx` and `componentes.ts` after the glyph step, as the closing brief orders.

### For the Tech Lead

Fixes in commit b02b10d:

- **web-e2e race, `catalog.spec.ts:59`.**
  - Cause (inferred, not observed): the router changes the address before React commits the new screen. A viewport change in that gap re-renders the screen still on show, Variedades, as a computer layout, and it selects its first variety. The helper's existing wait on the address could not see this.

- **PriceHistory's back is a link again.** `Boton` and `BotonLink` `onClick` now receive the click event. PriceHistory renders `BotonLink variant="back"` with `preventDefault()` and `back()`, so the role is `link`, the name is `‹ <variety>`, and the history behaviour is unchanged. The three `PriceHistory.test.tsx` lookups are back to `'link'`.

## UX Lead: final walk (v1)

### Walk

The app's start-up output was only a Vite build warning about chunk size. It gave no address and no user to sign in as, so I could not walk the running app in a browser. I walked the built branch instead (`3f4da76`, the integration pass): the screens, `Icono.tsx`, `Boton.tsx` and the closing guards. Everything below comes from that code, not from tapping through the app.

**The one finding behind the rest: most of the new icons were never drawn.** `ICON_NAMES` has 15 names: the five tabs, `atras`, `chevron`, `abajo`, `buscar`, `telegram`, `tacho`, `cobro`, `personaMas`, `agregar` and `stock`. None of the new names the validated design specifies exist. That includes `lapiz`, `cerrar`, `info`, `compartir`, `candado`, `salirSesion`, `archivar`, `restaurar`, `probar`, `salidaStock`, `atencion`, `enviar`, `copiar`, `publicar`, `precio`, `reintentar`, the five Más names and the seven Configuración names. The closing-guard file says so directly: the icons "`icon-glyphs` never shipped". `IconOnlyAction` is only `'delete' | 'search'`.

**Find an entry or action at a glance (main job)**
- **Más** (`Mas.tsx`): each entry still draws only the trailing `chevron`, with no leading icon. Every entry can still be reached with the same tap, but the job "recognise it at a glance" is not delivered here.
- **Configuración, list** (`Settings.tsx`): same as Más. None of the seven groups has a leading icon, including Integraciones' plug.
- **Bar and sidebar:** icon + text, unchanged. "+ Nueva venta" uses `agregar`, not `masVenta`.
- **Icon + text actions:** across the screens, the only icons in use are `agregar` (7 uses), `personaMas` (4), `cobro` (3), `clientes`, `stock`, `tacho` and `telegram` (2 each), and `buscar` (1). These are missing their icons:
  - "Sacar del stock", "Mandar catálogo", "Nuevo precio";
  - Copiar, Publicar, the main Compartir;
  - Probarlo, Archivar, Restaurar;
  - Cambiar contraseña, Cerrar sesión, Reintentar.

**Close, edit and share (icon-only list)**
- "Cerrar" is still a visible word in NuevaVenta, NuevaVentaCliente and EntradaStock, and Editar is still a word too. Close, edit and share cannot be icon-only, because there is no glyph and `Boton` has no such action.
- The flow allowed icon-only here but never required it, so nothing is lost. The accessible names "Cerrar" and "Editar" are unchanged.

**Record a sale, Cobrar, stock and withdrawals**
- Steps, routes, confirmations and names are unchanged. The tests still find "Cobrar a <name>" and the delete confirmations by name.
- "Sacar del stock" and "+ Entrada" have no `salidaStock` icon.

**Publish the pinned catalog**
- Integraciones keeps "Probarlo" and "Buscar mi chat", both found by role and name in `IntegrationsGroup.test.tsx`, and their reasons stay inline. Neither has an icon.
- Catálogo's Copiar and Compartir are still raw buttons (`OLD_SHAPES`), without icons.
- "Desactualizado" has no attention icon. The state is still carried by its word.

**Take a variety out**
- Archivar and Restaurar still exist on the variety's page, in the removal flow and in Archivadas, but as raw buttons without their icons (`OLD_SHAPES`). The archive confirmation is unchanged.

**Read a notice or help line**
- **More info:** there is no `MasInfo` component and no "Más info" anywhere in `web/src`. The optional "i" step does not exist, so no longer explanation can sit behind it.
- **Attention icon:** no `atencion` icon and no `attention` prop is used anywhere. All six notices show their sentence alone. Each sentence still carries its state, so nothing is hidden.

**Getting in**
- Submits are text only and the reveal stays a text link, as the flow says.

**What does hold:** no step, route, confirmation or accessible name was lost or renamed, and every job can still be completed end to end.

### Verdict

`returned`: no step is blocked, but the build delivers little of the validated flow. Más and Configuración have no entry icons, there is no More info and no attention icon, and most actions have no icon, because the new glyphs were never drawn.

**Record a sale, Cobrar, stock and withdrawals**
- Steps, routes and confirmations are unchanged. Cobrar is `cobro` + word, with the accessible name "Cobrar a <name>".
- On a variety's page, "+ Entrada" (`agregar`, accessible name "+ Entrada"), "Nuevo precio" (`precio`) and "Sacar del stock" (`salidaStock`) are icon + text.
- On Variedades, "+ Entrada de stock" and "Sacar del stock" are icon + text. "Mandar catálogo" reuses `telegram`.

**Take a variety out**
- "Archivar variedad" (`archivar`) is icon + text.
- "Eliminar variedad" is icon-only, with the accessible name "Eliminar variedad", and keeps its confirmation.
- "Restaurar" (`restaurar`) is on the archived page and on each row of Archivadas.

**Publish the pinned catalog**
- Integraciones:
  - "Probarlo" keeps its name and uses `probar`.
  - "Buscar mi chat" is icon + text with `buscar`.
  - The own-chat line is short ("Tocá Iniciar en tu bot; tu número lo ves con @userinfobot."), and the longer how-to sits behind Más info.
  - The consequence line ("El mensaje fijado anterior queda como está…") is still visible.
- Catálogo:
  - The main Compartir keeps its word, with `compartir`.
  - Copiar is `copiar` + word, and Publicar is icon + text.
  - "Desactualizado" and its reason carry `atencion`. "Al día" has no icon.

**Read a notice or help line**
- **Más info:** `MasInfo` exists and Integraciones uses it, which is the optional step in the flow.
- **Attention icon:** `atencion` is on Inicio's stale-catalog notice, on "Arrancá por las variedades", on the catalog's "Desactualizado", on "Sin conexión" and on the server error. Each sentence still carries its state.
- **After-import notice:** "Conectá el bot…" is not rendered anywhere in `web/src`, so there is nothing to check.

**Getting in and Tu cuenta**
- Submits stay text only, and the reveal stays a text link.
- "Cambiar contraseña" (`candado`) and "Cerrar sesión" (`salirSesion`) are icon + text.

**What holds:** no step, route, confirmation or accessible name was lost or renamed. Every job can still be completed end to end.

### Verdict

`approved`: every job can be completed, and the validated icons, Más info and attention icon are now built. What's left is small observations for the PR list and the by-eye check.

## UI Lead: final comparisons (v1)

_The system denied 1 write attempt: this role writes nothing; reproduce by reading the code, the task documents and the existing tests, or by running the declared checks. A denial here is not a syntax problem._

### Comparisons

I ran `scripts/design/compare.sh` on the integration commit (3f4da76) and read the side-by-side images. I also checked `ICON_NAMES` in `web/src/components/Icono.tsx`. It holds only 15 names: the five tabs, `stock`, `atras`, `chevron`, `abajo`, `buscar`, `telegram`, `tacho`, `cobro`, `personaMas` and `agregar`. **None of the names the validated design adds is drawn**: `lapiz`, `cerrar`, `info`, `atencion`, `archivar`, `restaurar`, `probar`, `salidaStock`, `candado`, `salirSesion`, the Más icons and the Configuración icons. The closing guard's `OLD_SHAPES` openly keeps eight shapes that "need a missing glyph". The screens show the result.

**Más (blocking).** The entries, order and copy are unchanged, and "Sacar del stock" is present. But no entry has its leading icon; each still shows only a chevron. "+ Nueva venta" keeps its plus.

**Configuración, list (blocking).** The seven groups appear in the right order with no icons. Integraciones has no plug or bot icon.

**Integraciones (blocking).** "Probarlo" kept its name and "Buscar mi chat" is back as icon + text, as Human Decision 1 asked. But Probarlo has no `probar` icon, and "Cerrar" is still a word. The own-chat line is shortened, but has no "Más info" at its end. The consequence line was not visible in the built state.

**A variety's page (blocking).** "+ Entrada", "+ Nuevo precio" and "Sacar del stock" are icon + text. "Archivar variedad" is text only. Every "Editar" is still a text link instead of the icon-only `lapiz`. There is no More info. The price-row trash is correct. Two "‹ Variedades" back links are stacked (observation, also on Catálogo and Archivadas).

**Archivadas.** The empty state is faithful. Restaurar could not be seen because the sample has no archived variety.

**Variedades.** Faithful: Mandar catálogo, + Entrada de stock and Sacar del stock are icon + text, "Nueva variedad" stays a link, and the record rows keep their marks. Two observations: Mandar catálogo uses the `telegram` glyph and Sacar del stock uses `stock`, not the names the design gives.

**Catálogo.** Publicar has its icon, "Editar encabezado y pie" stays a link, and the disabled reason has no icon. Copiar has no icon (observation). The main Compartir did not render in the headless capture, so it could not be judged.

**Tu cuenta (blocking).** "Cambiar contraseña" and "Cerrar sesión" have no `candado` or `salirSesion` icons, and "Cerrar" is a word.

**Sin conexión (blocking).** The notice has no `atencion` icon, and its body is still two sentences. Since `atencion` is not drawn, none of the six attention-icon notices can carry it.

**Inicio.** Faithful: "Cobrar" is `cobro` + word, "+ Nueva venta" keeps its plus, and the period tabs are text. InicioPrimeraVez has no built route in the comparison, so the checklist and the attention notices there were not seen.

**Nueva venta.** It reads as the same design: "Elegir cliente" carries `personaMas`, "Agregar otra variedad" is icon + text, and the choices and the submit are text. The only miss is "Cerrar", still a word.

### Verdict

`returned`: the built screens keep the old structure and copy, but the design's main changes are not there. These are the menu icons, icon-only Cerrar and Editar, the attention icon and More info, plus the icons on Archivar, Probarlo, Cambiar contraseña and Cerrar sesión. All of them are blocked by the missing glyphs.

## PM: final validation (v1)

### Verdict

`returned`: UX Lead and UI Lead both found blocking gaps with one root cause: the GH-97 glyphs were never drawn. Because of that, Más and Configuración have no entry icons. Cerrar and Editar are still words. More info and the attention icon don't exist. Archivar, Restaurar, Probarlo, Cambiar contraseña, Cerrar sesión and other actions have no icons. No step, route or accessible name was lost, but the validated design was not delivered, and that includes Human Decision 1.

### For Tech Lead

1. **Glyphs in both lists.** `ICON_NAMES` in `web/src/components/Icono.tsx` and `NombreIcono` in `componentes.ts` both contain these names, each with a `TRAZOS` path taken from the GH-97 design, and `tests/test_design_icons.py` passes:
   - `lapiz`, `cerrar`, `info`, `compartir`, `candado`, `salirSesion`, `archivar`, `restaurar`, `probar`, `salidaStock`, `atencion`;
   - `copiar`, `publicar`, `enviar`, `precio`, `reintentar`;
   - the five Más entry names and the seven Configuración group names.
2. **Icon-only actions.** `IconOnlyAction` in `Boton.tsx` is `'delete' | 'search' | 'close' | 'edit' | 'share'`. The map sends close→`cerrar`, edit→`lapiz` and share→`compartir`.
3. **Más.** `Mas.tsx` draws a leading `Icono` on each of its five entries, inside a `data-menu` container, and no two entries share a name.
4. **Configuración.** `Settings.tsx` draws a leading `Icono` on each of its seven groups, inside `data-menu`. Integraciones uses the plug/bot name, and no two groups share a name.
5. **Cerrar.** No screen shows "Cerrar" as visible text. Every form and sheet closes with `Boton variant="icon" action="close" ariaLabel="Cerrar"`, including NuevaVenta, NuevaVentaCliente, EntradaStock and the settings groups.
6. **Editar.** Every "Editar" control on a variety's page and its sections is `Boton variant="icon" action="edit" ariaLabel="Editar"`.
7. **More info.** `MasInfo.tsx` exists, with the name "Más info", `aria-expanded` and local state only. `IntegrationsGroup.tsx` renders it at the end of the own-chat help line.
8. **Attention icon.** `Notice`'s `attention` prop is passed on exactly six notices, and "Al día" never gets it:
   - Inicio's stale-catalog notice;
   - "Desactualizado" with its reason;
   - "Arrancá por las variedades";
   - the after-import "Conectá el bot…";
   - SinConexion;
   - the ErrorGenerico retry.
9. **Icon + text actions.** These render through `Boton`/`BotonLink` with an icon and their word:
   - Archivar variedad (`archivar`);
   - Restaurar on the variety page, Archivadas and the archived page (`restaurar`);
   - Probarlo (`probar`);
   - Cambiar contraseña (`candado`);
   - Cerrar sesión (`salirSesion`);
   - Copiar (`copiar`);
   - Publicar (`publicar`);
   - Sacar del stock (`salidaStock`);
   - Reintentar (`reintentar`).

   The catalog's main Compartir and Buscar mi chat also get named icons. Every accessible name stays unchanged.
10. **No leftover exemptions.** The closing guard's `OLD_SHAPES` list is empty. No raw `<button>` is exempt beyond the closed option/row class list.
11. **Sin conexión.** Its body is one sentence of at most 80 characters, registered in `LIMITED_COPY`.
12. **Consequence line.** `IntegrationsGroup.tsx` renders the short line about the old pinned message after a token or channel change.

## PM: final validation

### Verdict

`approved`: UX Lead's final walk and UI Lead's final comparisons both approve the branch at `b02b10d`, with no blocking findings. Every job still works end to end, with no step, route, confirmation or accessible name lost. The GH-97 glyphs are now drawn. The menu icons, icon-only Cerrar and Editar, Más info and the attention icon are built. Archivar, Restaurar, Probarlo, Cambiar contraseña and Cerrar sesión have their icons, as Human Decision 1 required.

### For Tech Lead

Nothing blocks shipping. Both reports raised non-blocking observations, which go into the PR list and the by-eye check:

- "+ Cargar gasto" shows a literal "+" next to its plus icon.
- Two "‹ Variedades" back links are stacked on a variety's page and on Catálogo.
- "Sin conexión" is still a title plus a detail line rather than one sentence.
- The app has no after-import "Conectá el bot…" notice to carry the attention icon.
- These screens were not judged by eye: InicioPrimeraVez, Estados, Variedades and VerTodas.
